📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: medRxiv  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-11  |  DOI: 10.64898/2026.08.10.26359943  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026

具有多尺度空间背景的深度学习可改善全球登革热适宜性绘图

Deep learning with multiscale spatial context improves global dengue suitability mapping
作者： Carlin Foka Takamgno, Jenicca Poongavanan, Moritz U. G. Kraemer, Tulio de Oliveira, Elizaveta Semenova, Houriiyah Tegally, Kraemer, M. U. G.
摘要中文翻译传染病风险模型通常依赖于不完整且因监测努力、诊断获取能力及暴发历史而存在空间偏倚的发生记录。生态位建模(ENM)可识别疾病发生在环境上可行的区域,但大多数方法仅使用点式协变量值表示位点,因此忽略了周围空间背景,且仅依赖存在性数据。本研究提出一种深度学习框架,用于存在性数据,通过比较报告发生点周围环境条件与整个研究区域采样的环境条件,估计相对疾病适宜性。该模型处理局部、邻域及更广泛景观尺度的网格化环境斑块,学习各尺度的贡献,并可容纳缺失栅格值。以登革病毒为全球案例研究,在空间分离的训练-测试设计下,评估多尺度空间表征是否优于点式ENM基线(包括随机森林与最大熵(MaxEnt))。该模型在留存测试集上实现Boyce指数0.971及AUC 0.976。学习到的尺度权重与消融实验显示,邻域背景贡献最强,而局部与更广泛尺度信息提供互补预测信号。与点式基线相比,该模型在南亚、东南亚及南美洲识别出6–18%更多环境适宜区域,覆盖数千万居民。这些发现证明多尺度空间背景可提升登革热相对适宜性估计。更广泛而言,掩码感知卷积密度比估计为利用不完整存在性发生数据绘制环境结构化病原体分布提供了灵活框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决传统传染病风险模型的局限性,该模型通常依赖于不完整且存在空间偏差的发生记录。登革病毒 由于其环境和地理变化而成为一个重大的公共卫生问题。
设计与对象
该研究采用空间不相交的训练测试设计来评估仅存在数据的深度学习框架。它重点关注 登革病毒 作为全球案例研究,分析南亚、东南亚和南美洲的环境数据。
核心方法
该模型使用深度学习方法来处理局部、邻里和更广泛景观尺度的网格化环境斑块。它结合了掩模感知卷积密度比估计来处理丢失的栅格值并了解每个空间尺度的贡献。
主要结果
该模型在保留测试集上的博伊斯指数为 0.971,AUC 为 0.976。与基于点的 ENM 基线相比,它发现南亚、东南亚和南美洲的环境适宜区域增加了 6-18%。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,多尺度空间背景可以改善对登革热相对适宜性的估计。这种方法提供了一个灵活的框架,用于根据不完整的发生数据绘制环境结构的病原体。
科研与公卫意义
结果可以为更准确的全球登革热适宜性绘图提供信息,帮助监测、风险评估和高风险地区的有针对性的公共卫生干预措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究的空间不相交的训练测试设计为模型的性能提供了适度的信心。然而,研究结果的普遍性可能受到所使用的具体环境数据的限制。

Deep learning with multiscale spatial context improves global dengue suitability mapping

Authors: Carlin Foka Takamgno, Jenicca Poongavanan, Moritz U. G. Kraemer, Tulio de Oliveira, Elizaveta Semenova, Houriiyah Tegally, Kraemer, M. U. G.
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Infectious-disease risk models often rely on occurrence records that are incomplete and spatially biased by surveillance effort, diagnostic access, and outbreak history. Ecological niche modelling (ENM) can identify areas where disease occurrence is environmentally plausible, yet most approaches represent locations using only pointwise covariate values and therefore overlook the surrounding spatial context and rely on presence-only data. Here, we present a deep-learning framework for presence-only data that estimates relative disease suitability by comparing the environmental conditions surrounding reported occurrences with those sampled across the wider study area. The model processes gridded environmental patches at local, neighbourhood, and broader landscape scales, learns the contribution of each scale, and accommodates missing raster values. Using dengue virus as a global case study, we evaluate whether multiscale spatial representation improves upon point-based ENM baselines including random forest and maximum entropy (MaxEnt) under a spatially disjoint train-test design. The model achieved a Boyce index of 0.971 and an AUC of 0.976 on the held-out test set. Learned scale weights and ablation experiments indicated that neighbourhood context contributed most strongly, while local and broader-scale information provided complementary predictive signals. Compared with point-based baselines, the model identified 6–18% more environmentally suitable area across South Asia, Southeast Asia, and South America, encompassing tens of millions of residents. These findings demonstrate that multiscale spatial context can improve estimates of relative dengue suitability. More broadly, mask-aware convolutional density-ratio estimation provides a flexible framework for mapping environmentally structured pathogens from incomplete, presence-only occurrence data.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the limitations of traditional infectious-disease risk models, which often rely on incomplete and spatially biased occurrence records. Dengue virus is a significant public health concern due to its environmental and geographic variability.
Design and population
The study employed a spatially disjoint train-test design to evaluate a deep-learning framework for presence-only data. It focused on dengue virus as a global case study, analyzing environmental data across South Asia, Southeast Asia, and South America.
Core methods
The model used a deep-learning approach to process gridded environmental patches at local, neighbourhood, and broader landscape scales. It incorporated mask-aware convolutional density-ratio estimation to handle missing raster values and learn the contribution of each spatial scale.
Main results
The model achieved a Boyce index of 0.971 and an AUC of 0.976 on the held-out test set. It identified 6–18% more environmentally suitable area across South Asia, Southeast Asia, and South America compared to point-based ENM baselines.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that multiscale spatial context can improve estimates of relative dengue suitability. This approach provides a flexible framework for mapping environmentally structured pathogens from incomplete occurrence data.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results can inform more accurate global dengue suitability mapping, aiding in surveillance, risk assessment, and targeted public health interventions in high-risk regions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's spatially disjoint train-test design provides moderate confidence in the model's performance. However, the generalizability of the findings may be limited by the specific environmental data used.

Journal: Clinical pharmacology and therapy  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-09  |  DOI: 10.1002/cpt.70439  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026

病毒动力学模型——在受控人类感染模型中莫诺登韦预防 DENV-3 的剂量选择

Viral Dynamic Model‐Informed Dose Selection of Mosnodenvir Prophylaxis against DENV‐3 in a Controlled Human Infection Model
作者： Nele Goeyvaerts, Xavier Woot de Trixhe, Martine Neyens, Thomas N. Kakuda, Liesbeth Van Wesenbeeck, Annemie Buelens, Freya Rasschaert, Anna P Durbin, Oliver Ackaert
摘要中文翻译登革病毒 (DENV) 感染是一个重大的全球健康威胁。Mosnodenvir (JNJ-64281802) 是一种泛血清型小分子 抗病毒药物,可阻断 DENV 复制。一项 IIa 期研究 (NCT05048875) 在受控人类感染模型 (CHIM) 中评估了 抗病毒药物 莫诺登韦预防 DENV-3 的活性、安全性和药代动力学。开发了联合群体药代动力学(PopPK)和病毒动力学模型(VDM)来评估莫诺登韦的预防效果并为剂量选择提供信息。PopPK 模型是一个双室模型,基于三项 I 期研究和 IIa 期研究的 队列研究 1 的血浆药代动力学数据。针对接受高、中或低剂量的参与者评估了莫诺登韦暴露指标。具有 免疫应答 隔室的 VDM 充分描述了安慰剂参与者的 DENV-3 RNA 图谱,随后通过包含与稳定状态下莫诺登韦平均浓度 (C avg,ss ) 的 sigmoid E max 关系进行扩展,以捕获 抗病毒药物 对病毒体生成速率的影响。模拟支持每日和每周莫诺登韦给药方案,以进一步表征 队列研究 2 中的药代动力学/药效关系。根据完整的 IIa 期研究数据,该 CHIM 中 DENV-3 的基本繁殖数估计为 21.7,实现完全根除病毒所需的典型 C avg,ss 估计为 1,410 ng/mL。总之,PopPK 和 VDM 相结合的方法有效地指导了剂量选择,并表征了药代动力学-抗病毒药物 活性关系,支持莫诺登韦作为登革热感染预防药物 抗病毒药物 的开发。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
抗病毒药物 感染仍然是全球主要的健康负担,有效 抗病毒药物 预防的需求尚未得到满足。该研究旨在确定 mosnodenvir 是否可以阻止 DENV-3 在人类中的复制,并使用基于模型的方法确定最佳预防剂量。
设计与对象
一项 IIa 期对照人类感染模型 (CHIM) 研究 (NCT05048875) 评估了莫诺登韦对健康志愿者预防 DENV-3 的作用。参与者接受高、中或低剂量的莫诺登韦或安慰剂,并收集药代动力学和病毒动态数据以指导剂量选择。
核心方法
使用先前 I 期研究的血浆数据和 CHIM 队列研究 1 建立了两室群体药代动力学 (PopPK) 模型。具有 免疫应答 室的病毒动力学模型 (VDM) 描述了安慰剂参与者中的 DENV-3 RNA 谱,并扩展到包括将 抗病毒药物 效应与平均稳态莫诺登韦浓度 (C avg,ss) 联系起来的 sigmoid E max 关系)。进行模拟来表征 队列研究 2 的药代动力学/药效关系并支持给药方案。
主要结果
CHIM 中 DENV-3 的基本繁殖数估计为 21.7,完全根除病毒所需的典型 C avg,ss 为 1,410 ng/mL。对各剂量组的 Mosnodenvir 暴露指标进行了估计,PopPK-VDM 组合方法证明了 抗病毒药物 对病毒粒子生产率的影响。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,整合 PopPK 和 VDM 可以有效地告知剂量选择,并表征药代动力学 - 抗病毒药物 活性关系,代表了登革热 抗病毒药物 开发的一种新的模型知情策略。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持莫诺登韦作为登革热感染预防药物 抗病毒药物 的潜力,并为未来临床试验中优化 抗病毒药物 剂量提供了框架。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量小和使用受控人类感染模型的限制,可能无法完全反映自然登革热 传播 动态。证据强度中等,依赖于基于模型的外推和模拟,而不是直接的临床疗效数据。

Viral Dynamic Model‐Informed Dose Selection of Mosnodenvir Prophylaxis against DENV‐3 in a Controlled Human Infection Model

Authors: Nele Goeyvaerts, Xavier Woot de Trixhe, Martine Neyens, Thomas N. Kakuda, Liesbeth Van Wesenbeeck, Annemie Buelens, Freya Rasschaert, Anna P Durbin, Oliver Ackaert
Original abstract (English)
Dengue virus (DENV) infection is a significant global health threat. Mosnodenvir (JNJ‐64281802) is a pan‐serotype small molecule antiviral blocking DENV replication. A Phase IIa study (NCT05048875) evaluated the antiviral activity, safety, and pharmacokinetics of mosnodenvir as prophylaxis against DENV‐3 in a controlled human infection model (CHIM). A combined population pharmacokinetic (PopPK) and viral dynamic model (VDM) was developed to estimate the prophylactic effect of mosnodenvir and to inform dose selection. The PopPK model is a two‐compartment model based on plasma pharmacokinetic data from three Phase I studies and Cohort 1 of the Phase IIa study. Mosnodenvir exposure metrics were estimated for participants receiving high, medium, or low doses. A VDM with an immune response compartment adequately described DENV‐3 RNA profiles for placebo participants and was subsequently extended by including a sigmoid E max relationship with average mosnodenvir concentration at steady state ( C avg,ss ) to capture the antiviral effect on virion production rate. Simulations supported daily and weekly mosnodenvir dosing regimens to further characterize the pharmacokinetic/pharmacodynamic relationship in Cohort 2. Based on the complete Phase IIa study data, the basic reproductive number for DENV‐3 in this CHIM was estimated at 21.7, and the typical C avg,ss required to achieve complete virus eradication was estimated to be 1,410 ng/mL. In conclusion, the combined PopPK and VDM approach effectively informed dose selection and characterized the pharmacokinetic‐antiviral activity relationship, supporting the development of mosnodenvir as a prophylactic treatment for dengue infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus infection remains a major global health burden, and there is an unmet need for effective antiviral prophylaxis. The study aimed to determine whether mosnodenvir could prevent DENV-3 replication in humans and to identify an optimal prophylactic dose using model-informed approaches.
Design and population
A Phase IIa controlled human infection model (CHIM) study (NCT05048875) evaluated mosnodenvir as prophylaxis against DENV-3 in healthy volunteers. Participants received high, medium, or low doses of mosnodenvir or placebo, with pharmacokinetic and viral dynamic data collected to inform dose selection.
Core methods
A two-compartment population pharmacokinetic (PopPK) model was built using plasma data from prior Phase I studies and the CHIM Cohort 1. A viral dynamic model (VDM) with an immune response compartment described DENV-3 RNA profiles in placebo participants and was extended to include a sigmoid E max relationship linking antiviral effect to average steady-state mosnodenvir concentration (C avg,ss ). Simulations were performed to characterize the pharmacokinetic/pharmacodynamic relationship and support dosing regimens for Cohort 2.
Main results
The basic reproductive number for DENV-3 in the CHIM was estimated at 21.7, and the typical C avg,ss required for complete virus eradication was 1,410 ng/mL. Mosnodenvir exposure metrics were estimated across dosing groups, and the combined PopPK-VDM approach demonstrated an antiviral effect on virion production rate.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that integrating PopPK and VDM effectively informed dose selection and characterized the pharmacokinetic–antiviral activity relationship, representing a novel model-informed strategy for dengue antiviral development.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support the potential of mosnodenvir as a prophylactic treatment for dengue infection and provide a framework for optimizing antiviral dosing in future clinical trials.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited by its small sample size and the use of a controlled human infection model, which may not fully reflect natural dengue transmission dynamics. The evidence strength is moderate, relying on model-based extrapolation and simulation rather than direct clinical efficacy data.

Journal: Medicine  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-07  |  DOI: 10.1097/md.0000000000050108  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 105, 32, e50108
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

登革热 和基孔肯雅热:埃塞俄比亚东部发热患者的 患病率、血液学参数和相关因素:一项横断面研究

Dengue fever and chikungunya: prevalence, hematological parameters, and associated factors among febrile patients in eastern Ethiopia: A cross-sectional study
作者： Getachew Kabew Mekonnen, Desalegn Admassu Ayana, Ahmed Mohammed, Addisu Alemu, Tadesse Shumie, Mesay Arkew, Haftu Asmerom, Delenasaw Yewhalaw
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 和基孔肯雅热被认为是新出现的虫媒病毒性疾病,对热带和亚热带地区的公共卫生构成重大威胁,但在埃塞俄比亚,它们的实际负担和影响因素尚未得到充分研究。
设计与对象
2023年10月至12月,德雷达瓦市政府对576名发热患者进行了一项基于机构的横断面研究,其中大多数是男性(54.2%)和农村居民(91%)。
核心方法
使用结构化问卷和检查表收集社会人口学和临床数据,并使用迈瑞 BC-3000 Plus 自动血液分析仪测定血液学参数。
主要结果
近 327 例 (57%) 病例被诊断为 登革热,而 73 例 (13%) 病例被诊断为基孔肯雅热,与其他发热患者相比,DENV 感染患者的白细胞、红细胞和血小板计数显着下降 (P < .001)。
解释与创新
该研究强调了临床诊断的登革热和基孔肯雅热病例的高发生率,以及某些社会人口和制度因素与虫媒病毒疾病之间的显着关联,强调了早期 诊断 的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了 监测 和针对埃塞俄比亚东部虫媒病毒性疾病的针对性干预措施的重要性,特别是考虑到在登革热患者中观察到的血液学异常。
局限与证据强度
横断面设计限制了因果推断,在没有实验室确认 诊断 感染的情况下依赖临床 诊断 会降低特异性;因此,证据强度中等。

Dengue fever and chikungunya: prevalence, hematological parameters, and associated factors among febrile patients in eastern Ethiopia: A cross-sectional study

Authors: Getachew Kabew Mekonnen, Desalegn Admassu Ayana, Ahmed Mohammed, Addisu Alemu, Tadesse Shumie, Mesay Arkew, Haftu Asmerom, Delenasaw Yewhalaw
Original abstract (English)
Dengue fever and chikungunya are emerging arboviral diseases and are becoming major public health threats in tropical and subtropical regions. However, the actual burden and contributing factors are not well studied in Ethiopia. Thus, this study aimed to determine the prevalence of dengue fever and chikungunya and assess the hematological parameters and associated factors among febrile patients in eastern Ethiopia. An institution-based cross-sectional study was conducted in Dire Dawa city administration from October to December 2023. Sociodemographic and clinical information of patients were collected using a structured questionnaire and checklist. Hematological parameters were determined using the Mindray BC-3000 Plus automated hematology analyzer. The Kruskal–Wallis test, Spearman correlation test, and logistic regression were used during data analysis. A P-value of < .05 with a 95% confidence interval was considered statistically significant. A total of 576 febrile patients were investigated. The majority of the study participants, 312 (54.2%), were male, and 524 (91%) were rural residents. Nearly 327 (57%) cases were diagnosed with dengue fever, while 73 (13%) cases were for chikungunya. The type of visited healthcare institution (Sabian Comprehensive Hospital, AOR: 8.2 [95% CI: 4.02, 17.3] and Dilchora Referral Hospital, AOR: 2.3 [95% CI: 1.5, 3.7]), urban residence (AOR: 2.3, 95% CI: 1.37–7.7), government employment (AOR: 0.34 [0.12–0.99]), and patients’ previous history of a similar AFI (acute febrile illness) AOR: 3.2 (95% CI: 1.02, 10.3) were significantly associated with empiric arboviral diseases. White blood cell, red blood cell, and platelet counts were significantly decreased among patients with DENV infection compared to other febrile patients (P < .001). In addition, a statistically significant weak negative correlation was noted between the duration of fever and white blood cell count, red blood cell count, and platelet count among dengue patients. High rates of clinical dengue fever and chikungunya cases were observed. Some socio-demographic and institutional factors were more likely to be significantly associated with clinically diagnosed arboviral diseases. Furthermore, significant differences were observed in some hematological parameters between cases with dengue/chikungunya diseases and those without. The findings highlighted the need for early diagnosis and urgent action to downplay the undesirable outcomes of arboviral diseases.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue fever and chikungunya are recognized as emerging arboviral diseases posing significant public health threats in tropical and subtropical regions, yet their actual burden and contributing factors remain insufficiently studied in Ethiopia.
Design and population
An institution-based cross-sectional study was conducted in Dire Dawa city administration from October to December 2023 among 576 febrile patients, with the majority being male (54.2%) and rural residents (91%).
Core methods
Sociodemographic and clinical data were collected using a structured questionnaire and checklist, and hematological parameters were determined using the Mindray BC-3000 Plus automated hematology analyzer.
Main results
Nearly 327 (57%) cases were diagnosed with dengue fever, while 73 (13%) cases were for chikungunya, and white blood cell, red blood cell, and platelet counts were significantly decreased among patients with DENV infection compared to other febrile patients (P < .001).
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights high rates of clinically diagnosed dengue and chikungunya cases and significant associations between certain socio-demographic and institutional factors with arboviral diseases, underscoring the need for early diagnosis.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings emphasize the importance of surveillance and targeted interventions for arboviral diseases in eastern Ethiopia, particularly given the observed hematological abnormalities in dengue patients.
Limitations and evidence strength
The cross-sectional design limits causal inference, and reliance on clinical diagnosis without laboratory confirmation of dengue virus infection reduces specificity; thus, the evidence strength is moderate.

埃及伊蚊在伊朗的消失和重新出现:历史和现状,登革热 威胁的上升

The disappearance, and re-emergence of Aedes aegypti in Iran: historical and current status, rising dengue fever threat
作者： Tahereh Sadat Asgarian, Mohammad Mehdi Sedaghat
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究调查了伊朗埃及伊蚊的历史和现状,重点关注导致其重新出现的因素以及 登革热 的相关威胁。
设计与对象
该研究回顾了 1920 年至 2018 年伊朗埃及伊蚊目击事件的历史记录和当前报告,并确定了该物种重新定居的地区。
核心方法
该研究依靠历史数据和最近的实地观察来追踪埃及伊蚊在伊朗存在的时间线,分析疟疾控制、基础设施发展、全球化、城市化和气候变化等因素。
主要结果
埃及伊蚊于 1920 年至 1950 年代初首次在伊朗出现,消失了 70 年,此后在多个地区重新出现,包括霍尔木兹甘、布什尔、锡斯坦和俾路支斯坦等南部省份。
解释与创新
埃及伊蚊在伊朗的重新出现凸显了 媒介 控制策略范式转变的必要性,强调对反应性化学方法的综合管理。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了监测 媒介 人群并采取积极措施以减轻伊朗 登革热 暴发 风险的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于历史和观察数据,这些数据可能会受到记录保存方面的空白以及重新出现的埃及伊蚊种群对 登革病毒 传播 传播 的直接证据的影响。信心程度适中。

The disappearance, and re-emergence of Aedes aegypti in Iran: historical and current status, rising dengue fever threat

Authors: Tahereh Sadat Asgarian, Mohammad Mehdi Sedaghat
Original abstract (English)
Aedes aegypti is the proven vector of dengue fever, Zika, chikungunya, and yellow fever. In Iran, the first recorded sighting of Ae. aegypti dates back to 1920 and into the early 1950s. For 70 years, the species was not seen in the country. However, since 2018, reports have confirmed the presence of Ae. aegypti in various regions. It is now established in some areas of the southern provinces, including Hormozgan, Bushehr, and Sistan and Baluchistan. The appearance, disappearance, and subsequent re-emergence of Ae. aegypti in Iran, after a 70-year absence, are attributed to a combination of factors such as historical malaria control, infrastructure, globalization, urbanization, and climate change that is discussed in this study. This study underscores that the re-emergence of Ae. aegypti in Iran necessitates an urgent paradigm shift from reactive chemical-based control toward a proactive, integrated management framework. Ultimately, the long-term mitigation of dengue fever risk depends on the seamless integration of community-based environmental management with advanced biotechnological interventions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study investigates the historical and current status of Aedes aegypti in Iran, focusing on the factors contributing to its re-emergence and the associated threat of dengue fever.
Design and population
The study reviews historical records and current reports of Aedes aegypti sightings in Iran, spanning from 1920 to 2018, and identifies regions where the species has re-established itself.
Core methods
The study relies on historical data and recent field observations to trace the timeline of Aedes aegypti presence in Iran, analyzing factors such as malaria control, infrastructure development, globalization, urbanization, and climate change.
Main results
Aedes aegypti was first recorded in Iran between 1920 and the early 1950s, disappeared for 70 years, and has since re-emerged in various regions, including southern provinces like Hormozgan, Bushehr, and Sistan and Baluchistan.
Interpretation and novelty
The re-emergence of Aedes aegypti in Iran highlights the need for a paradigm shift in vector control strategies, emphasizing integrated management over reactive chemical approaches.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the importance of monitoring vector populations and implementing proactive measures to mitigate the risk of dengue fever outbreaks in Iran.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on historical and observational data, which may be subject to gaps in record-keeping and limited direct evidence of dengue virus transmission by the re-emerging Aedes aegypti population. Confidence is moderate.

登革热垂直传播:厄瓜多尔瓜亚基尔阿尔塔综合中心报告新生儿病例。

Transmisión vertical de Dengue: Reporte de un caso neonatal en un centro de alta complejidad en Guayaquil, Ecuador.
作者： Andrés Roberto González Cabrera, Joyce Katherine Andrade Velasquez, Mildred Patricia Zambrano Leal, Juan Gregorio Chang Asinc
摘要中文翻译新生儿登革热的垂直 传播 在瓜亚基尔等高流行地区提出了诊断挑战。我们报告了一名女新生儿的病例,她是一名孕妇的女儿,死于严重的围产期登革热。该患者因严重围产期窒息、肾损伤和心肌损伤而入院。第五天,她出现发烧和皮疹,NS1抗原检测呈阳性,确诊为诊断。在关键期,她出现了血液浓缩、血小板减少和毛细血管渗漏导致的肺水肿。还记录了 2 级脑室内出血。动态液体管理无需血液制品即可实现稳定。经过多学科随访 14 天后,患者病情稳定出院。公布此病例会引起未报告年龄组中登革热的怀疑;及时的围产期检测对于预防 死亡率 相关的严重并发症至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
新生儿登革热的垂直 传播 在瓜亚基尔等高流行地区提出了诊断挑战。该研究旨在记录垂直登革热 传播 病例并强调其临床意义。
设计与对象
该研究报告了一名女性新生儿的病例,她是一名死于严重围产期登革热的孕妇的女儿,被送往厄瓜多尔瓜亚基尔的一个高度复杂的中心。对患者进行了超过 14 天的多学科随访观察。
核心方法
该患者因严重围产期窒息、肾损伤和心肌损伤而入院。采用动态液体管理来稳定患者而不需要血液制品。第五天,NS1 抗原检测呈阳性,证实了 诊断。
主要结果
新生儿第五天出现发热和皮疹,NS1抗原检测呈阳性,确诊为诊断。还记录了 2 级脑室内出血。
解释与创新
该病例强调了垂直登革热 传播 的可能性,以及及时围产期检测以预防 死亡率 与严重并发症相关的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,需要提高对新生儿(报告不足的年龄组)登革热的认识和 监测,并强调早期 监测 和干预的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于单个 病例报告,这限制了研究结果的普遍性。然而,由于详细的临床文件和通过 NS1 抗原检测确认登革热,证据是强有力的。

Transmisión vertical de Dengue: Reporte de un caso neonatal en un centro de alta complejidad en Guayaquil, Ecuador.

Authors: Andrés Roberto González Cabrera, Joyce Katherine Andrade Velasquez, Mildred Patricia Zambrano Leal, Juan Gregorio Chang Asinc
Original abstract (English)
Vertical transmission of neonatal dengue presents a diagnostic challenge in hyperendemic areas such as Guayaquil. We report the case of a female neonate, the daughter of a pregnant woman who died from severe peripartum dengue. The patient was admitted with severe perinatal asphyxia, renal injury, and myocardial damage. On the fifth day, she developed fever and a rash, and the diagnosis was confirmed by a positive NS1 antigen test. During the critical phase, she developed hemoconcentration, thrombocytopenia, and pulmonary edema due to capillary leak. Grade 2 intraventricular hemorrhage was also documented. Dynamic fluid management allowed for stabilization without the need for blood products. The patient was discharged in stable condition after 14 days with multidisciplinary follow-up. Publicizing this case raises suspicion of dengue in an underreported age group; timely perinatal detection is crucial to prevent mortality associated with severe complications.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Vertical transmission of neonatal dengue presents a diagnostic challenge in hyperendemic areas such as Guayaquil. The study aimed to document a case of vertical dengue transmission and highlight its clinical implications.
Design and population
The study reports a case of a female neonate, the daughter of a pregnant woman who died from severe peripartum dengue, admitted to a high-complexity center in Guayaquil, Ecuador. The patient was observed over 14 days with multidisciplinary follow-up.
Core methods
The patient was admitted with severe perinatal asphyxia, renal injury, and myocardial damage. Dynamic fluid management was employed to stabilize the patient without the need for blood products. The diagnosis was confirmed by a positive NS1 antigen test on the fifth day.
Main results
The neonate developed fever and a rash on the fifth day, and the diagnosis was confirmed by a positive NS1 antigen test. Grade 2 intraventricular hemorrhage was also documented.
Interpretation and novelty
This case highlights the potential for vertical dengue transmission and the importance of timely perinatal detection to prevent mortality associated with severe complications.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings emphasize the need for increased awareness and surveillance of dengue in neonates, an underreported age group, and underscore the importance of early diagnosis and intervention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a single case report, which limits the generalizability of the findings. However, the evidence is strong due to the detailed clinical documentation and confirmation of dengue through NS1 antigen testing.

刚果安哥拉边境存在登革热 传播 和多种伊蚊病毒组的证据

Evidence of dengue transmission and a diverse Aedes mosquito virome on the Congo’s Angola border
作者： Wenqiao He, Thierry Bobanga, Anne Piantadosi, Zachary R. Popkin-Hall, Fabien Vulu, Matthew H. Collins, Melchior M. Kashamuka, Antoinette K. Tshefu, Jonathan J. Juliano, Jonathan B. Parr
摘要中文翻译摘要 伊蚊广泛分布于刚果民主共和国 (DRC),是登革热 (DENV)、寨卡病毒、基孔肯雅热 (CHIKV) 和黄热病 (YFV) 病毒的主要 媒介。虽然刚果民主共和国的疟疾负担受到相当大的关注,但虫媒病毒的研究仍然不足。在刚果民主共和国西南部最近发生 CHIKV 和 YFV 暴发 的背景下,我们在刚果金佩斯靠近安哥拉边境的三个地区收集了伊蚊,以调查其病毒组。对 8 个随机选择的野外蚊子库(包括来自三个收集地点的 155 只蚊子)进行宏基因组和靶向测序,分别确认了 6 个(75%)和 8 个(100%)个库中的高置信度 DENV 读数和人类血粉。我们发现了多种蚊子病毒组,包括其他已知和推定的人类和动物病毒。我们的研究结果提供了刚果民主共和国-安哥拉边境沿线流行登革热病毒 传播 的有力证据,并说明了野生捕获的蚊子在新兴病原体异种监测方面的潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管存在传播登革热的伊蚊,但刚果民主共和国对登革热的研究仍然不足,而且最近该地区其他虫媒病毒的 暴发 突显了有关当地登革热 传播 动态的知识差距。
设计与对象
该研究在刚果金佩斯靠近安哥拉边境的三个地区收集了伊蚊,以在最近的基孔肯雅热和黄热病 暴发 的背景下调查其病毒组。
核心方法
对来自三个收集地点的 8 个随机选择的野外蚊子库(包括 155 只蚊子)进行了宏基因组和靶向测序,以识别病毒读数和血粉。
主要结果
高置信度 登革病毒 读数在八个蚊子池中的六个 (75%) 中得到证实,并且在所有八个池中均检测到人血粉 (100%)。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为刚果民主共和国-安哥拉边境地区流行 登革病毒 传播 的有力证据,并强调了野生捕获的蚊子在新兴病原体异种监测中的用途。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果支持刚果民主共和国西南部登革热的针对性 监测 和控制措施,并展示了利用蚊子病毒组监测虫媒病毒活性的实用方法。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量小和依赖蚊子库而不是个体数据的限制,这限制了普遍性和精确的风险评估,需要对证据有适度的信心。

Evidence of dengue transmission and a diverse Aedes mosquito virome on the Congo’s Angola border

Authors: Wenqiao He, Thierry Bobanga, Anne Piantadosi, Zachary R. Popkin-Hall, Fabien Vulu, Matthew H. Collins, Melchior M. Kashamuka, Antoinette K. Tshefu, Jonathan J. Juliano, Jonathan B. Parr
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Aedes mosquitoes are widely distributed across the Democratic Republic of Congo (DRC), and are major vectors of dengue (DENV), Zika, chikungunya (CHIKV), and yellow fever (YFV) viruses. While the high burden of malaria in the DRC receives considerable attention, arboviruses remain understudied. In the setting of recent CHIKV and YFV outbreaks in southwestern DRC, we collect Aedes mosquitoes in three areas of Kimpese, DRC, near the Angola border, to investigate their virome. Metagenomic and targeted sequencing of eight randomly selected field mosquito pools, comprising 155 mosquitoes from three collection sites, confirm high-confidence DENV reads and human blood meals in six (75%) and eight (100%) pools, respectively. We find diverse mosquito viromes including other known and putative human and animal viruses. Our findings provide strong evidence of endemic DENV transmission along the DRC-Angola border and illustrate the potential of wild-caught mosquitoes for xenosurveillance of emerging pathogens.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue remains understudied in the Democratic Republic of Congo despite the presence of Aedes mosquitoes that transmit it, and recent outbreaks of other arboviruses in the region highlighted a knowledge gap about local dengue transmission dynamics.
Design and population
The study collected Aedes mosquitoes in three areas of Kimpese, DRC, near the Angola border, to investigate their virome in the context of recent chikungunya and yellow fever outbreaks.
Core methods
Metagenomic and targeted sequencing were performed on eight randomly selected field mosquito pools comprising 155 mosquitoes from three collection sites to identify viral reads and blood meals.
Main results
High-confidence dengue virus reads were confirmed in six of eight mosquito pools (75%), and human blood meals were detected in all eight pools (100%).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these findings as strong evidence of endemic dengue virus transmission along the DRC-Angola border and highlight the utility of wild-caught mosquitoes for xenosurveillance of emerging pathogens.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results support targeted surveillance and control measures for dengue in southwestern DRC and demonstrate a practical approach for monitoring arboviral activity using mosquito viromes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its small sample size and reliance on mosquito pools rather than individual-level data, which constrains generalizability and precise risk assessment, warranting moderate confidence in the evidence.

无症状传播登革热模型的定性分析和优化控制

Qualitative analysis and optimal control of a dengue model with asymptomatic transmission
作者： Ibrahim M. Elmojtaba, Maryam Al-Yahyai, Fatma Al-Musalhi, Nasser Al-Salti
摘要中文翻译登革热 仍然是全球范围内传播最迅速的 媒介 传播疾病之一,其驱动因素是 媒介、媒介、环境以及日益增长的人员流动之间复杂的相互作用。尽管数学 流行病学 取得了重大进展,但现有模型通常依赖于简化假设,而忽略了关键的 传播 途径,特别是无症状感染的作用和人口流动的隐含影响。在这项研究中,我们开发了严格的 宿主–媒介 登革热模型,该模型将无症状 传播 纳入机械和分析易处理的框架内,为迈向流动驱动的流行病学建模奠定了基础。我们通过证明正性、有界性和不变区域的存在性来建立模型的适定性。基本复制编号 \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {R}_0$$\end{document} 是使用下一代矩阵方法导出的,并在无症状携带者存在的情况下检查其流行病学解释。进行了全面的定性分析,包括无病平衡的局部和全局稳定性,并通过李雅普诺夫方法建立了全局稳定性。获得了地方病平衡存在的条件,揭示了无症状 传播 在维持疾病持续性中的基本作用。为了量化模型参数的相对影响,对 \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} 进行敏感性分析执行 \begin{document}$$\mathcal {R}_0$$\end{document},确定 传播 的关键驱动因素,并突出 媒介 相关参数和无症状感染性的主导影响。此外,还制定了包含 媒介 控制、传播 减少和 治疗 策略的最优控制框架。利用庞特里亚金的极大值原理,我们推导了必要的最优性条件并描述了最优干预政策的结构。数值模拟说明了组合控制策略的有效性,并证明了单一干预方法的局限性。结果强调了在登革热动力学中考虑无症状 传播 的至关重要性,并为未来纳入空间异质性和人类流动性的扩展提供了严格的数学基线。这项工作弥合了经典区室模型和更现实的流行病学环境之间的差距,为在日益相互联系的人群中设计有效的登革热控制策略提供了理论见解和实际意义。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 仍然是全球范围内传播最迅速的 媒介 传播疾病之一,其驱动因素是 媒介、媒介、环境以及日益增长的人员流动之间复杂的相互作用。尽管数学 流行病学 取得了重大进展,但现有模型通常依赖于简化假设,而忽略了关键的 传播 途径,特别是无症状感染的作用和人口流动的隐含影响。
设计与对象
在这项研究中,我们开发了严格的 宿主–媒介 登革热模型,该模型将无症状 传播 纳入机械和分析易处理的框架内,为迈向流动驱动的流行病学建模奠定了基础。
核心方法
我们通过证明正性、有界性和不变区域的存在性来建立模型的适定性。基本再生数 $$\mathcal {R}_0$$ 是使用下一代矩阵方法得出的,并在无症状携带者存在的情况下检查其流行病学解释。进行了全面的定性分析,包括无病平衡的局部和全局稳定性,并通过李雅普诺夫方法建立了全局稳定性。
主要结果
获得了地方病平衡存在的条件,揭示了无症状 传播 在维持疾病持续性中的基本作用。对 $$\mathcal {R}_0$$ 进行敏感性分析,确定 传播 的关键驱动因素,并强调 媒介 相关参数和无症状感染性的主导影响。
解释与创新
结果强调了在登革热动力学中考虑无症状 传播 的至关重要性,并为未来纳入空间异质性和人类流动性的扩展提供了严格的数学基线。
科研与公卫意义
这项工作弥合了经典区室模型和更现实的流行病学环境之间的差距,为在日益相互联系的人群中设计有效的登革热控制策略提供了理论见解和实际意义。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据并未报告研究设计或样本量的局限性。研究结果的可信度很高,因为该研究提供了严格的数学框架并确定了登革热 传播 的关键驱动因素。

Qualitative analysis and optimal control of a dengue model with asymptomatic transmission

Authors: Ibrahim M. Elmojtaba, Maryam Al-Yahyai, Fatma Al-Musalhi, Nasser Al-Salti
Original abstract (English)
Dengue fever remains one of the most rapidly expanding vector-borne diseases worldwide, driven by complex interactions between host, vector, environment, and increasingly, human mobility. Despite significant advances in mathematical epidemiology, existing models often rely on simplifying assumptions that neglect critical transmission pathways, particularly the role of asymptomatic infections and the implicit influence of population movement. In this study, we develop a rigorous host–vector dengue model that incorporates asymptomatic transmission within a mechanistic and analytically tractable framework, providing a foundational step toward mobility-driven epidemiological modeling. We establish the well-posedness of the model by proving positivity, boundedness, and the existence of invariant regions. The basic reproduction number, \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {R}_0$$\end{document}, is derived using the next-generation matrix approach, and its epidemiological interpretation is examined in the presence of asymptomatic carriers. A comprehensive qualitative analysis is conducted, including the local and global stability of the disease-free equilibrium, with global stability established via Lyapunov methods. Conditions for the existence of endemic equilibria are obtained, revealing the fundamental role of asymptomatic transmission in sustaining disease persistence. To quantify the relative impact of model parameters, a sensitivity analysis of \documentclass[12pt]{minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$\mathcal {R}_0$$\end{document} is performed, identifying key drivers of transmission and highlighting the dominant influence of vector-related parameters and asymptomatic infectivity. Furthermore, an optimal control framework is formulated incorporating vector control, transmission reduction, and treatment strategies. Using Pontryagin’s Maximum Principle, we derive the necessary optimality conditions and characterize the structure of optimal intervention policies. Numerical simulations illustrate the effectiveness of combined control strategies and demonstrate the limitations of single-intervention approaches. The results underscore the critical importance of accounting for asymptomatic transmission in dengue dynamics and provide a mathematically rigorous baseline for future extensions incorporating spatial heterogeneity and human mobility. This work bridges the gap between classical compartmental models and more realistic epidemiological settings, offering both theoretical insights and practical implications for the design of effective dengue control strategies in increasingly interconnected populations.

基于长期短期记忆的登革热多元预测模型:斯里兰卡案例研究

Long Short-Term Memory-Based Multivariate Forecasting Model for Dengue: A Case Study in Sri Lanka
作者： S. Vijitharan, Abdul Raheem Fathima Shafana
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Dengue fever has been a significant health concern in Sri Lanka since the 1960s. The study aimed to evaluate the effectiveness of different LSTM models in predicting dengue occurrences.
设计与对象
这项研究是过去十年在斯里兰卡进行的,重点关注登革热病例并使用天气数据作为预测因素。该研究涉及 LSTM 模型的三种变体:单向 LSTM、双向 LSTM (BiLSTM) 和编码器-解码器 LSTM。
核心方法
天气数据(包括降雨量和平均温度)被用作预测因子,同时使用 RMSE 评估模型的效率。人们提出了不同的统计和经典机器学习模型来预测登革热,以减轻该疾病达到高 传播 率的趋势。
主要结果
过去十年来,案件数量急剧增加。虽然所有三个模型都表现出相对较好的性能,但 BiLSTM 模型显着优于其他两个模型。
解释与创新
这项研究肯定了使用 LSTM 来预测类似登革热的 媒介 传播疾病,这些疾病的特点是与预测因子之间存在复杂的关系。为了了解过去长短期记忆 (LSTM) 模型的优势,这项具体研究评估了 LSTM 模型的三种变体。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为开发更准确的登革热预测模型提供信息,从而有助于公共卫生规划和干预策略。该研究强调了 LSTM 模型在根据环境因素预测登革热发生方面的潜力。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的具体局限性。证据强度中等,因为该研究侧重于模型性能,而没有解决更广泛的局限性或偏见。

Long Short-Term Memory-Based Multivariate Forecasting Model for Dengue: A Case Study in Sri Lanka

Authors: S. Vijitharan, Abdul Raheem Fathima Shafana
Original abstract (English)
Dengue fever has been a significant health concern in Sri Lanka since the 1960s. The number of cases has drastically increased over the last decade. Different statistical and classical machine learning models have been proposed to forecast dengue in order to mitigate the disease from reaching its high transmission rate. More recently, the advent of neural networks has improved prognosis in an efficient manner through time forecasting of dengue data using multiple predictor variables. Understanding the strength of long short-term memory (LSTM) models in the past, this specific research has evaluated three variants of LSTM models: unidirectional LSTM, bidirectional LSTM (BiLSTM), and encoder-decoder LSTM, with the aim of predicting dengue occurrences in Sri Lanka. Weather data, including rainfall and mean temperature, were used as predictors while the efficiency of the models was assessed using RMSE. While all three models exhibited relatively good performance, the BiLSTM model significantly outperformed the other two models. This study affirms the use of LSTM for predicting dengue-like vector-borne diseases that are characterized by complex relationships with the predictors.
View research seven-element analysis

Dengue fever has been a significant health concern in Sri Lanka since the 1960s. The study aimed to evaluate the effectiveness of different LSTM models in predicting dengue occurrences.
Design and population
The study was conducted in Sri Lanka over the last decade, focusing on dengue cases and using weather data as predictors. The research involved three variants of LSTM models: unidirectional LSTM, bidirectional LSTM (BiLSTM), and encoder-decoder LSTM.
Core methods
Weather data, including rainfall and mean temperature, were used as predictors while the efficiency of the models was assessed using RMSE. Different statistical and classical machine learning models have been proposed to forecast dengue in order to mitigate the disease from reaching its high transmission rate.
Main results
The number of cases has drastically increased over the last decade. While all three models exhibited relatively good performance, the BiLSTM model significantly outperformed the other two models.
Interpretation and novelty
This study affirms the use of LSTM for predicting dengue-like vector-borne diseases that are characterized by complex relationships with the predictors. Understanding the strength of long short-term memory (LSTM) models in the past, this specific research has evaluated three variants of LSTM models.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more accurate forecasting models for dengue, which can aid in public health planning and intervention strategies. The study highlights the potential of LSTM models in predicting dengue occurrences based on environmental factors.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study. The evidence strength is moderate as the study focuses on model performance without addressing broader limitations or biases.

阿根廷登革热时空模型(2009-2025):风险因素、空间模式以及对预防规划的影响

Spatio-Temporal Modeling of Dengue in Argentina (2009–2025): Risk Factors, spatial patterns, and implications for prevention planning
作者： J. Piette, C. Linard, C. Morlighem, V. Andreo
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 登革热在阿根廷已显着蔓延,埃及伊蚊现已在温带省份定居,连续的流行病最终在 2024 年达到创纪录的 暴发。将大规模的年际气候变化与当地环境和社会基础设施条件分开对于有针对性的预防至关重要,但很少有研究在全国范围内对阿根廷异质 传播 区域的登革热决定因素进行建模。方法 我们使用综合嵌套拉普拉斯近似 (INLA) 框架内的贝叶斯分层时空模型分析了 2009 年至 2025 年阿根廷北部和中部 473 个省份报告的 817,699 例登革热病例。零膨胀负二项分布解释了过度分散和过多的零;将空间、时间和时空随机效应与气候、环境和社会人口学协变量相结合,并通过前向链时间交叉验证评估预测性能。结果 登革热 发病率 显示出强烈的年际变化,在 2024 年达到顶峰。厄尔尼诺年份的风险明显较高,与季节气候无关。当前夏季较高的温度和相对湿度与风险增加相关,而前春季温度与随后的 传播 呈非线性结转关系。居住在废物处理场附近的人群面临的风险更大。该模型可靠地识别了高风险部门年份,包括 2024 年的流行病,尽管预测 暴发 的严重程度仍然更具挑战性。结论 大规模气候变率、季节性天气和当地环境因素共同影响了阿根廷登革热 传播。贝叶斯风险图可以指导有针对性的预防和季节性预警系统,特别是在不断扩大的南部 传播 边境地区。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热已蔓延至阿根廷温带省份并连续流行,但大规模气候变化与当地环境和社会基础设施因素的相对影响仍不足以在全国范围内进行有针对性的预防。
设计与对象
利用 2009 年至 2025 年阿根廷北部和中部 473 个省份报告的 817,699 例登革热病例进行了全国性生态分析。
核心方法
应用 INLA 框架内的贝叶斯分层时空模型,使用零膨胀负二项分布来处理过度分散和过量零,并将气候、环境和社会人口统计协变量与前向链时间交叉验证相结合以进行预测性能评估。
主要结果
登革热 发病率 表现出强烈的年际变化,在 2024 年达到峰值,当前夏季较高的温度和相对湿度与风险增加相关,而厄尔尼诺年份的风险明显较高,与季节气候无关。
解释与创新
该研究得出的结论是,大范围的气候变化、季节性天气和当地环境因素共同影响了阿根廷的登革热传播,并证明贝叶斯风险图可以识别包括2024年疫情在内的高风险部门年份。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持开发有针对性的预防和季节性预警系统,特别是在埃及伊蚊正在扎根的不断扩大的南部 传播 边境地区。
局限与证据强度
预测 暴发 的震级仍然比识别高风险区域更具挑战性,这表明尽管有可靠的风险映射,但预测精度仍然有限​​。

Spatio-Temporal Modeling of Dengue in Argentina (2009–2025): Risk Factors, spatial patterns, and implications for prevention planning

Authors: J. Piette, C. Linard, C. Morlighem, V. Andreo
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Dengue has expanded markedly in Argentina, with Aedes aegypti now established in temperate provinces and successive epidemics culminating in a record outbreak in 2024. Disentangling large-scale inter-annual climate variability from local environmental and socio-infrastructural conditions is essential for targeted prevention, yet few studies have modelled dengue determinants across Argentina’s heterogeneous transmission zones at national scale. Methods We analysed 817,699 dengue cases reported across 473 departments in northern and central Argentina from 2009 to 2025 using Bayesian hierarchical spatio-temporal models within the integrated nested Laplace approximation (INLA) framework. A zero-inflated negative binomial distribution accounted for overdispersion and excess zeros; spatial, temporal and space–time random effects were combined with climatic, environmental and sociodemographic covariates, and predictive performance was assessed through forward-chaining temporal cross-validation. Results Dengue incidence showed strong inter-annual variability, peaking in 2024. El Niño years carried markedly higher risk independent of seasonal climate. Higher current-summer temperature and relative humidity were associated with increased risk, while previous-spring temperature showed a non-linear carry-over relationship with subsequent transmission. Populations living near waste-disposal sites were at greater risk. The model reliably identified high-risk department-years, including the 2024 epidemic, although forecasting outbreak magnitude remained more challenging. Conclusions Large-scale climate variability, seasonal weather and local environmental factors jointly shape dengue transmission in Argentina. Bayesian risk mapping can guide targeted prevention and seasonal early-warning systems, particularly along the expanding southern transmission frontier.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue has expanded into temperate provinces of Argentina with successive epidemics, yet the relative contributions of large-scale climate variability versus local environmental and socio-infrastructural factors remain insufficiently disentangled for targeted prevention at national scale.
Design and population
A nationwide ecological analysis was conducted using 817,699 dengue cases reported across 473 departments in northern and central Argentina from 2009 to 2025.
Core methods
Bayesian hierarchical spatio-temporal models within the INLA framework were applied, using a zero-inflated negative binomial distribution to handle overdispersion and excess zeros, and incorporating climatic, environmental and sociodemographic covariates with forward-chaining temporal cross-validation for predictive performance assessment.
Main results
Dengue incidence exhibited strong inter-annual variability with a peak in 2024, higher current-summer temperature and relative humidity were associated with increased risk, and El Niño years carried markedly higher risk independent of seasonal climate.
Interpretation and novelty
The study concludes that large-scale climate variability, seasonal weather and local environmental factors jointly shape dengue transmission in Argentina, and demonstrates that Bayesian risk mapping can identify high-risk department-years including the 2024 epidemic.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the development of targeted prevention and seasonal early-warning systems, particularly along the expanding southern transmission frontier where Aedes aegypti is becoming established.
Limitations and evidence strength
Forecasting outbreak magnitude remained more challenging than identifying high-risk areas, indicating a limitation in predictive precision despite reliable risk mapping.

巴西东北部某市居民对 登革热 的认知和了解

Perception and knowledge of the population about dengue fever in a municipality in Northeast of Brazil
作者： Laiza Carvalho Sousa, Alison do Nascimento Lima, Francisca Juliana do Nascimento Sousa, Elainne Silva Freire, Aline do Carmo Silva, Valéria Cristina Soares Pinheiro, Juliete Lima Viana, Juliana Maria Trindade Bezerra
摘要中文翻译目的:评估巴西马拉尼昂州 Lago do Junco 市居民对登革热教育活动的知识、习惯和参与情况。方法:这是一项观察性横断面研究,随机选择了 264 名 18 岁以上的个体。采用结构化调查问卷,包括社会人口统计学变量以及与病因、传播、症状、治疗、预防和家庭实践相关的问题。使用描述性统计和卡方检验对数据进行分析,显着性水平为5%。结果:共有 264 名受访者,其中 62.1% 为女性,37.0% 年龄在 35 岁至 59 岁之间,26.1% 高中毕业。大多数参与者对登革热的了解令人满意:91.7% 的人正确识别了主要体征和症状,80.3% 的人充分识别了预防方法,64.0% 的人通过蚊虫叮咬正确指示了 传播。然而,59.1% 的人表示家里有盛水的容器,这有利于 媒介 的扩散。此外,54.2%的人表示曾参加过教育活动。较高的教育水平与对该疾病的更多了解之间存在统计学上显着的关联(p < 0.05)。结论:尽管人们的知识水平令人满意,但危险的家庭做法仍然存在。因此,有必要扩大和加强持续的教育行动,特别是针对更多的弱势群体,旨在减少本市的危险行为,有效控制登革热。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估巴西马拉尼昂州 Lago do Junco 市居民对登革热教育活动的知识、习惯和参与情况。这项研究的动机是需要了解公众对 登革热 的看法和认识,登革热 是该地区的一个重大公共卫生问题。
设计与对象
这是一项观察性横断面研究,研究对象为 264 名 18 岁以上的个体,这些个体是从巴西马拉尼昂州 Lago do Junco 市随机抽取的。该研究于2024年至2025年间进行。
核心方法
采用结构化调查问卷,包括社会人口统计学变量以及与病因、传播、症状、治疗、预防和家庭实践相关的问题。使用描述性统计和卡方检验对数据进行分析,显着性水平为5%。
主要结果
共有 264 人接受采访,其中 62.1% 为女性,37.0% 年龄在 35 岁至 59 岁之间,26.1% 完成高中学业。大多数参与者对登革热的了解令人满意:91.7% 的人正确识别了主要体征和症状,80.3% 的人充分识别了预防方法,64.0% 的人通过蚊虫叮咬正确指示了 传播。然而,59.1% 的人表示家里有盛水的容器,这有利于 媒介 的扩散。此外,54.2%的人表示曾参加过教育活动。较高的教育水平与对该疾病的更多了解之间存在统计学上显着的关联(p < 0.05)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,尽管人们的知识水平令人满意,但危险的家庭做法仍然存在。这项研究为教育水平与登革热知识之间的关系提供了新颖的见解,强调了有针对性的教育干预措施的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以通过强调教育活动的重要性来为公共卫生战略提供信息,以提高知识并改变与登革热预防相关的危险行为。这有助于制定更有效的基于社区的干预措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横截面设计的限制,不允许进行因果推断。由于依赖自我报告的数据和潜在的回忆偏差,证据的强度中等。

Perception and knowledge of the population about dengue fever in a municipality in Northeast of Brazil

Authors: Laiza Carvalho Sousa, Alison do Nascimento Lima, Francisca Juliana do Nascimento Sousa, Elainne Silva Freire, Aline do Carmo Silva, Valéria Cristina Soares Pinheiro, Juliete Lima Viana, Juliana Maria Trindade Bezerra
Original abstract (English)
Objective: to evaluate knowledge, habits, and participation in educational activities on dengue among residents of the municipality of Lago do Junco, Maranhão, Brazil. Methodology: this is an observational, cross-sectional study conducted with 264 individuals over 18 years of age, randomly selected. A structured questionnaire was applied, including sociodemographic variables and questions related to the etiological agent, transmission, symptoms, treatment, prevention, and household practices. Data were analyzed using descriptive statistics and the Chi-square test, with a significance level of 5%. Results: a total of 264 individuals were interviewed, of whom 62.1% were female, 37.0% were aged between 35 and 59 years, and 26.1% had completed high school. Most participants demonstrated satisfactory knowledge about dengue: 91.7% correctly recognized the main signs and symptoms, 80.3% adequately identified prevention methods, and 64.0% correctly indicated transmission through mosquito bites. However, 59.1% reported the presence of water-holding containers in their households, favoring vector proliferation. In addition, 54.2% reported having participated in educational activities. A statistically significant association was observed between higher education level and greater knowledge about the disease (p < 0.05). Conclusion: although the population's knowledge is satisfactory, risky household practices still persist. Therefore, it is necessary to expand and strengthen continuous educational actions, especially targeting more vulnerable groups, aiming to reduce risk behaviors and effectively control dengue in the municipality.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate knowledge, habits, and participation in educational activities on dengue among residents of the municipality of Lago do Junco, Maranhão, Brazil. The research is motivated by the need to understand public perception and awareness of dengue fever, which is a significant public health problem in the region.
Design and population
This is an observational, cross-sectional study conducted with 264 individuals over 18 years of age, randomly selected from the municipality of Lago do Junco, Maranhão, Brazil. The study was carried out between 2024 and 2025.
Core methods
A structured questionnaire was applied, including sociodemographic variables and questions related to the etiological agent, transmission, symptoms, treatment, prevention, and household practices. Data were analyzed using descriptive statistics and the Chi-square test, with a significance level of 5%.
Main results
A total of 264 individuals were interviewed, of whom 62.1% were female, 37.0% were aged between 35 and 59 years, and 26.1% had completed high school. Most participants demonstrated satisfactory knowledge about dengue: 91.7% correctly recognized the main signs and symptoms, 80.3% adequately identified prevention methods, and 64.0% correctly indicated transmission through mosquito bites. However, 59.1% reported the presence of water-holding containers in their households, favoring vector proliferation. In addition, 54.2% reported having participated in educational activities. A statistically significant association was observed between higher education level and greater knowledge about the disease (p < 0.05).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that although the population's knowledge is satisfactory, risky household practices still persist. This study provides novel insights into the relationship between education level and dengue knowledge, highlighting the need for targeted educational interventions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform public health strategies by emphasizing the importance of educational campaigns to improve knowledge and modify risky behaviors related to dengue prevention. This can contribute to the development of more effective community-based interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which does not allow for causal inferences. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the reliance on self-reported data and the potential for recall bias.

2024年登革热登革热和登革出血热的临床和生化特征

Clinical and Biochemical Characteristics of Dengue Fever and Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever During the 2024 Dengue Outbreak
作者： Arifa Akram, Tasnim Nafisa, Tania Islam Resma, Md Nazmul Huda, Md. Ashiqur Rahman
摘要中文翻译背景:登革热是一种 媒介 传播的病毒感染,对热带和亚热带地区的公共卫生构成重大威胁,从轻度发烧到 DHF 和 DSS 等严重形式。性别和血小板计数被认为会影响疾病的严重程度,但它们的作用仍不清楚。目的:本研究旨在探讨登革热患者的人口统计学、临床和实验室特征,重点关注性别差异和血小板计数的关联。方法:这是一项于2024年7月至11月在孟加拉国达卡NILMRC病毒学系进行的前瞻性研究。研究人群包括研究期间入住AMZ医院内科病房的确诊登革热患者,以及符合纳入和排除标准的NILMRC室外患者。进行血清学和RT PCR。结果:男性占57.9%,女性占42.1%,其中男性登革热患病率较高。大多数患者年龄在 16-25 岁(30.1%)和 26-35 岁(29.5%)年龄段。临床特征存在性别差异:男性更常报告咳嗽、头痛、肌痛、疲劳、关节痛和血小板计数低,而女性则更常报告皮疹和恶心/呕吐。较高比例的男性 (8.2%) 经历过 DHF/DSS 等严重并发症。84.2% 的血小板计数 >100,000 个细胞/μL。分别有 42.5% 和 37% 的患者出现肝酶(AST 和 ALT)升高。DENV-2 是主要血清型 (61%)。较低的血小板计数(≤100,000 个细胞/μL)与较高的 发病率 严重并发症、AST 和 ALT 水平升高以及血细胞比容升高 >20% 相关。结论:本研究强调了登革热临床表现和严重程度的显着性别差异,男性更常出现肌肉骨骼疼痛、疲劳以及 DHF 和 DSS 等严重并发症。血小板计数成为预测严重后果的关键标志。莫诺医学院学报,2025 年 12 月;11(2):113-120

问题与背景
登革热是一种由 媒介 传播的病毒感染,对热带和亚热带地区的公共卫生构成重大威胁,从轻度发烧到 DHF 和 DSS 等严重形式。性别和血小板计数被认为会影响疾病的严重程度,但它们的作用仍不清楚。
设计与对象
这是2024年7月至11月在孟加拉国达卡NILMRC病毒学系进行的一项前瞻性研究。研究人群包括研究期间入住AMZ医院内科病房的确诊登革热患者,以及符合纳入和排除标准的来自NILMRC的户外患者。

57.9%为男性,42.1%为女性,其中男性登革热患病率较高。临床特征存在性别差异:男性更常报告咳嗽、头痛、肌痛、疲劳、关节痛和血小板计数低,而女性则更常报告皮疹和恶心/呕吐。较高比例的男性 (8.2%) 经历过 DHF/DSS 等严重并发症。84.2% 的血小板计数 >100,000 个细胞/μL。分别有 42.5% 和 37% 的患者出现肝酶(AST 和 ALT)升高。DENV-2 是主要血清型 (61%)。较低的血小板计数(≤100,000 个细胞/μL)与较高的 发病率 严重并发症、AST 和 ALT 水平升高以及血细胞比容升高 >20% 相关。
解释与创新
这项研究强调了登革热临床表现和严重程度的显着性别差异,男性更容易出现肌肉骨骼疼痛、疲劳以及 DHF 和 DSS 等严重并发症。血小板计数成为预测严重后果的关键标志。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为登革热的针对性 监测 和管理策略提供信息,特别是在识别严重疾病的危险因素并指导基于性别和血小板计数的临床决策方面。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在孟加拉国的一个中心进行的,这可能会限制普遍性。证据是中等的,因为它基于特定 暴发 时期的观察数据。

Clinical and Biochemical Characteristics of Dengue Fever and Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever During the 2024 Dengue Outbreak

Authors: Arifa Akram, Tasnim Nafisa, Tania Islam Resma, Md Nazmul Huda, Md. Ashiqur Rahman
Original abstract (English)
Background: Dengue is a vector-borne viral infection that poses a significant public health threat in tropical and subtropical regions, ranging from mild fever to severe forms like DHF and DSS. Gender and platelet count are thought to influence disease severity, but their roles remain unclear. Objective: This study aims to explore the demographic, clinical, and laboratory characteristics of dengue patients, focusing on gender differences and platelet count’s association. Methodology: This was a prospective study carried out in the Department of Virology in NILMRC, Dhaka, Bangladesh from July to November 2024. The study population comprised confirmed dengue patients admitted to the Medicine Ward of AMZ Hospital during the study period, as well as outdoor patients from NILMRC, who met the inclusion and exclusion criteria. Serology and RT PCR were performed. Results: 57.9% were male and 42.1% were female, with a higher prevalence of dengue among males. The majority of patients were in the 16-25 (30.1%) and 26-35 (29.5%) age groups. Gender differences were observed in clinical features: males more frequently reported cough, headache, myalgia, fatigue, joint pain, and low platelet count, while females had a higher incidence of rash and nausea/vomiting. A higher proportion of males (8.2%) experienced severe complications like DHF/DSS.84.2% having platelet counts >100,000 cells/μL. Elevated liver enzymes (AST and ALT) were observed in 42.5% and 37% of patients, respectively. DENV-2 was the predominant serotype (61%). Lower platelet counts (≤100,000 cells/μL) were associated with higher incidences of severe complications, elevated AST and ALT levels, and hematocrit rise >20%. Conclusion: This study highlights significant gender differences in the clinical presentation and severity of dengue, with males more frequently experiencing musculoskeletal pain, fatigue, and severe complications like DHF and DSS. Platelet count emerged as a key marker for predicting severe outcomes. Journal of Monno Medical College December, 2025;11(2):113-120
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue is a vector-borne viral infection that poses a significant public health threat in tropical and subtropical regions, ranging from mild fever to severe forms like DHF and DSS. Gender and platelet count are thought to influence disease severity, but their roles remain unclear.
Design and population
This was a prospective study carried out in the Department of Virology in NILMRC, Dhaka, Bangladesh from July to November 2024. The study population comprised confirmed dengue patients admitted to the Medicine Ward of AMZ Hospital during the study period, as well as outdoor patients from NILMRC, who met the inclusion and exclusion criteria.
Core methods
Serology and RT PCR were performed. The majority of patients were in the 16-25 (30.1%) and 26-35 (29.5%) age groups.
Main results
57.9% were male and 42.1% were female, with a higher prevalence of dengue among males. Gender differences were observed in clinical features: males more frequently reported cough, headache, myalgia, fatigue, joint pain, and low platelet count, while females had a higher incidence of rash and nausea/vomiting. A higher proportion of males (8.2%) experienced severe complications like DHF/DSS. 84.2% having platelet counts >100,000 cells/μL. Elevated liver enzymes (AST and ALT) were observed in 42.5% and 37% of patients, respectively. DENV-2 was the predominant serotype (61%). Lower platelet counts (≤100,000 cells/μL) were associated with higher incidences of severe complications, elevated AST and ALT levels, and hematocrit rise >20%.
Interpretation and novelty
This study highlights significant gender differences in the clinical presentation and severity of dengue, with males more frequently experiencing musculoskeletal pain, fatigue, and severe complications like DHF and DSS. Platelet count emerged as a key marker for predicting severe outcomes.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted surveillance and management strategies for dengue, particularly in identifying risk factors for severe disease and guiding clinical decision-making based on gender and platelet count.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in a single center in Bangladesh, which may limit generalizability. The evidence is moderate as it is based on observational data from a specific outbreak period.

马来西亚城市地区登革热时空联系网络的社会网络分析和指数随机图建模

Social network analysis and exponential random graph modelling of dengue spatiotemporal linkage networks in an urban Malaysian district
作者： Siti Najiha Md Asari, Fatimah Abdul Razak, Mohd Rahim Sulong, Wan Ming Keong, Nazarudin Safian
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热负担日益集中在快速城市化的亚洲城市,但常规的 监测 很少捕捉到精细尺度的住宅时空共现。
设计与对象
我们使用社交网络分析和指数随机图模型(ERGM)对马来西亚八打灵区的案例级联系网络进行了描述,并确定了联系形成的社会人口和居住相关性。
核心方法
当居住地在 200 m 以内且症状出现在 14 天内时,病例就会被关联起来。
主要结果
在 2023 年报告的 19,549 例实验室确诊的登革热病例中,12,978 例至少有一个关联,在高度分散的网络中形成了 21,424 个关联。
解释与创新
链接代表住宅共现,而不是确认的 传播。
科研与公卫意义
将关联分析集成到常规 监测 中可以支持在运营能力受到限制时对高层建筑和优先社区中的 媒介 控制进行有针对性的优先排序。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性或证据强度。

Social network analysis and exponential random graph modelling of dengue spatiotemporal linkage networks in an urban Malaysian district

Authors: Siti Najiha Md Asari, Fatimah Abdul Razak, Mohd Rahim Sulong, Wan Ming Keong, Nazarudin Safian
Original abstract (English)
Dengue burden is increasingly concentrated in rapidly urbanising Asian cities, yet routine surveillance rarely captures fine-scale residential spatiotemporal co-occurrence. We characterised a case-level linkage network and identified sociodemographic and residential correlates of linkage formation in Petaling District, Malaysia, using social network analysis and exponential random graph models (ERGMs). Cases were linked when their residences were within 200 m and symptom onset occurred within 14 days. Among 19,549 laboratory-confirmed dengue cases reported in 2023, 12,978 had at least one linkage, forming 21,424 linkages in a highly fragmented network. Linkages represent residential co-occurrence rather than confirmed transmission. Among connected cases, the conditional odds of linkage were elevated for pairs of high-rise residents (OR 6.66, 95% CI 6.40–6.93), low-rise residents (OR 4.34, 95% CI 4.15–4.54) and non-citizen cases (OR 3.96, 95% CI 3.70–4.22). Student status was modestly associated with linkage formation (OR 1.07, 95% CI 1.05–1.10). Degree-related structural dependence was not supported. Attribute associations were more stable than structural dependence across evaluated network definitions. Integrating linkage analysis into routine surveillance could support targeted prioritisation of vector control in high-rise buildings and priority communities when operational capacity is constrained.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue burden is increasingly concentrated in rapidly urbanising Asian cities, yet routine surveillance rarely captures fine-scale residential spatiotemporal co-occurrence.
Design and population
We characterised a case-level linkage network and identified sociodemographic and residential correlates of linkage formation in Petaling District, Malaysia, using social network analysis and exponential random graph models (ERGMs).
Core methods
Cases were linked when their residences were within 200 m and symptom onset occurred within 14 days.
Main results
Among 19,549 laboratory-confirmed dengue cases reported in 2023, 12,978 had at least one linkage, forming 21,424 linkages in a highly fragmented network.
Interpretation and novelty
Linkages represent residential co-occurrence rather than confirmed transmission.
Scientific and public-health significance
Integrating linkage analysis into routine surveillance could support targeted prioritisation of vector control in high-rise buildings and priority communities when operational capacity is constrained.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength.

用于 媒介 控制的数字 监测:哥伦比亚基于网络的登革热监测系统的部署和评估

Digital Surveillance for Vector Control: Deployment and Evaluation of a Web-Based Dengue Monitoring System in Colombia
作者： Susana Koo Chong, Felix Wu, Tatiana Rivera Ramírez, Rocío Cárdenas Sánchez, María Angélica Carrillo, Axel Kroeger, Rocio Cardenas Sanchez
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 数字技术越来越多地应用于公共卫生领域,以改善数据管理并加强疾病控制行动。本研究描述了基于网络的软件系统(媒介 Control (DSVC) 的数字 监测)的设计、开发和现场测试,以监视和评估 媒介 控制程序。该系统是在大规模干预期间部署的,使用一种新型杀幼虫/杀成虫保护涂层(PC)应用于家用水容器,这是许多热带城市传播登革热的伊蚊的主要幼虫栖息地。在哥伦比亚库库塔和洛斯帕蒂奥斯进行了一项实地研究,以评估 DSVC 在减少数据处理时间、提高准确性和支持及时决策方面的潜力。方法:DSVC 的开发是为了优化数据处理和监控 媒介 控制活动。该系统通过整合社区志愿者走访约 8,000 个家庭期间收集的数据,在实际操作条件下进行了测试。比较了两种数据收集方法:传统纸质表格和 Epicollect5 移动应用程序。首席研究员通过 DSVC 监控实时数据处理、分析和可视化。对市政数据管理工程师的深入采访评估了该系统融入当地卫生服务的潜力。与社区志愿者和 媒介 控制技术人员进行的焦点小组讨论探讨了 Epicollect5 的接受度、可用性以及感知的优点和局限性。结果 与纸质工作流程相比,DSVC 减少了数据处理时间并提高了准确性。该系统有效管理大量数据,并实现数据的快速聚合和可视化。市政 媒介 控制技术人员报告称,DSVC 满足监测 媒介 控制活动的关键要求。访谈和焦点小组表明对数字工具的积极接受,强调了它们在常规项目中采用的潜力。Epicollect5 促进了纸质数据和数字数据的集成,减少了错误并加速了数据捕获。然而,实地调查显示,一些社区存在设备被盗的风险以及对纸质表格的持续偏好。结论 DSVC 和 Epicollect5 提高了数据收集和分析的效率,从而实现更快、更有针对性的响应。数字工具代表了优化 媒介 控制程序的重大进步。市政部门确定了该系统未来整合的潜力,有待行政决策。用户接受度、操作可行性和清晰的工作流程对于最大化 媒介 控制中数字工具的影响至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估基于网络的登革热监测系统在提高 媒介 控制程序中的数据处理效率和准确性方面的潜力。
设计与对象
在哥伦比亚库库塔和洛斯帕蒂奥斯进行了实地研究,以评估系统在实际操作条件下的性能。
核心方法
该系统的测试方法是整合社区志愿者在走访约 8,000 个家庭期间收集的数据,并将传统纸质表格与 Epicollect5 移动应用程序进行比较。
主要结果
与纸质工作流程相比,DSVC 减少了数据处理时间并提高了准确性,而现场调查揭示了设备被盗的风险以及某些社区对纸质表格的持续偏好。
解释与创新
作者解释说,DSVC 和 Epicollect5 提高了数据收集和分析的效率,从而能够更快、更有针对性地响应 媒介 控制程序。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,数字工具可以改善登革热 监测 控制计划的管理,支持更有效的 监测 和决策。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到设备盗窃的潜在风险和某些地区对纸质表格的持续偏好的限制,这可能会影响结果的普遍性。证据是中等的。

Digital Surveillance for Vector Control: Deployment and Evaluation of a Web-Based Dengue Monitoring System in Colombia

Authors: Susana Koo Chong, Felix Wu, Tatiana Rivera Ramírez, Rocío Cárdenas Sánchez, María Angélica Carrillo, Axel Kroeger, Rocio Cardenas Sanchez
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Digital technologies are increasingly used in public health to improve data management and strengthen disease-control operations. This study describes the design, development, and field testing of a web-based software system—Digital Surveillance of Vector Control (DSVC)—to monitor and evaluate vector control programs. The system was deployed during a large-scale intervention using a novel larvicidal/adulticidal protective coating (PC) applied to domestic water containers, the main larval habitat of dengue-transmitting Aedes mosquitoes in many tropical cities. A field study was conducted in Cúcuta and Los Patios, Colombia, to assess the potential of DSVC to reduce data processing time, improve accuracy, and support timely decision-making. Methodology: DSVC was developed to optimize data processing and monitoring vector control activities. It was tested under real operational conditions by integrating data collected by community volunteers during visits to approximately 8,000 households. Two data-collection methods were compared: traditional paper forms and the Epicollect5 mobile application. The principal investigator monitored real-time data processing, analysis, and visualization through DSVC. In-depth interviews with municipal data management engineers assessed the system’s potential integration into local health services. Focus group discussions with community volunteers and vector control technicians explored acceptance, usability, and perceived advantages and limitations of Epicollect5. Results DSVC reduced data processing time and improved accuracy compared to paper-based workflows. The system efficiently managed large data volumes and enabled rapid aggregation and visualization of data. Municipal vector control technicians reported that DSVC met key requirements for monitoring vector control activities. Interviews and focus groups indicated positive acceptance of digital tools, highlighting their potential for adoption in routine programs. Epicollect5 facilitated integration of paper-based and digital data, reduced errors, and accelerated data capture. However, fieldwork revealed risks of device theft and persistent preference for paper forms in some neighborhoods. Conclusion DSVC and Epicollect5 enhanced the efficiency of data collection and analysis, enabling faster and more targeted responses. Digital tools represent a significant advancement for optimizing vector control programs. Municipal departments identified the system’s potential for future integration, pending administrative decisions. User acceptance, operational feasibility, and clear workflows are essential for maximizing the impact of digital tools in vector control.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the potential of a web-based dengue monitoring system to improve data processing efficiency and accuracy in vector control programs.
Design and population
A field study was conducted in Cúcuta and Los Patios, Colombia, to assess the system's performance in real operational conditions.
Core methods
The system was tested by integrating data collected by community volunteers during visits to approximately 8,000 households, comparing traditional paper forms with the Epicollect5 mobile application.
Main results
DSVC reduced data processing time and improved accuracy compared to paper-based workflows, while fieldwork revealed risks of device theft and persistent preference for paper forms in some neighborhoods.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that DSVC and Epicollect5 enhanced the efficiency of data collection and analysis, enabling faster and more targeted responses to vector control programs.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that digital tools can improve the management of dengue vector control programs, supporting more effective surveillance and decision-making.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited by the potential risks of device theft and persistent preference for paper forms in some areas, which may affect the generalizability of the results. The evidence is moderate.

孟加拉国城市高流行和新兴沿海人均登革热风险,2021-2025 年:全国时空生态分析。

Urban Hyperendemicity and Emerging Coastal Per-Capita Risk of Dengue in Bangladesh, 2021-2025: A Nationwide Spatiotemporal Ecological Analysis.
作者： Pratyay Hasan, Tazdin Delwar Khan, Mohammad Saiful Islam, Mohd Arifuzzaman, Mohammad Emdadul Haque
摘要中文翻译背景:登革热已从达卡中心转移到孟加拉国全国范围。目的:为了量化时空模式并识别 2021 年至 2025 年所有 64 个地区的高风险集群,并利用 2025 年的数据扩展之前的报告,本研究旨在观察人均风险热点的持续变化。方法:卫生服务总局 (DGHS) 的回顾性生态研究汇总了疑似和实验室确诊的登革热入院病例的年度数据(n = 637,626 例)。每 10 万人口的 发病率 是根据 2022 年人口普查预测计算得出的,年增长率为 1.12%。应用全局(Moran's I)和局部(Getis-Ord Gi*)空间自相关和前瞻性时空扫描统计(SaTScan)。结果:全国 发病率:76.4/100,000/年。2025 年利率调整后的莫兰 I = 0.328 (p = 0.001)。原始病例热点:大达卡;人口调整热点:贾洛卡蒂、巴尔古纳(沿海)。高速率时空星团(2024-2025):达卡、马尼克甘杰(RR = 3.13,p = 0.001)。达卡 O/E = 4.31 (RR = 6.38)。两个低速率集群(北部、东南部)。结论:城市高流行的特点是病例数量高,加上沿海人均风险不断出现,需要采取有针对性的干预措施。在达卡,加强 媒介 控制至关重要,而在沿海地区则应实施 监测 预警。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热已从达卡中心转移到孟加拉国全国范围。本研究旨在量化时空模式并识别 2021 年至 2025 年所有 64 个地区的高风险集群,利用 2025 年的数据扩展之前的报告,以观察人均风险热点的持续变化。
设计与对象
使用 2021 年至 2025 年间孟加拉国所有 64 个地区卫生服务总局 (DGHS) 收集的疑似和实验室确诊登革热病例(n = 637,626 例)的年度汇总数据进行了一项回顾性生态研究。
核心方法
发病率 每 100,000 人口的发病率采用 2022 年人口普查预测数据(年增长率为 1.12%)计算。研究应用了全局(Moran's I)和局部(Getis-Ord Gi∗)空间自相关分析以及前瞻性时空扫描统计(SaTScan)来分析时空模式。
主要结果
全国 发病率 率为 76.4/100,000/年。2025 年,利率调整后的 Moran's I 为 0.328 (p = 0.001)。在大达卡发现了原始病例热点,而经过人口调整的热点则在贾洛卡蒂和巴尔古纳(沿海)。在达卡和马尼克甘杰发现了一个高速率时空星团(2024-2025)(RR = 3.13,p = 0.001)。达卡的 O/E 比率为 4.31 (RR = 6.38)。在北部和东南部地区发现了两个低率集群。
解释与创新
该研究强调了达卡的城市高流行和新兴的沿海人均风险,表明登革热 传播 模式发生了转变,需要量身定制的干预措施。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,达卡需要加强 媒介 控制,沿海地区需要 监测 预警,以应对不断变化的登革热风险形势。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于汇总数据,这可能会限制推断个体风险因素的能力。由于使用了生态分析和潜在的空间自相关偏差,证据是中等的。

Urban Hyperendemicity and Emerging Coastal Per-Capita Risk of Dengue in Bangladesh, 2021-2025: A Nationwide Spatiotemporal Ecological Analysis.

Authors: Pratyay Hasan, Tazdin Delwar Khan, Mohammad Saiful Islam, Mohd Arifuzzaman, Mohammad Emdadul Haque
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Dengue has shifted from Dhaka-centric to nationwide in Bangladesh. AIM: To quantify spatiotemporal patterns and identify high-risk clusters across all 64 districts from 2021 to 2025, and, extending previous reports with data from 2025, this study aims to observe an ongoing shift in per-capita risk hotspots. METHODS: Retrospective ecological study of the Directorate General of Health Services (DGHS) aggregate yearly data on suspected and laboratory-confirmed dengue admissions (n = 637,626 cases). Incidence per 100,000 population was calculated using 2022 census projections with 1.12% annual growth. Global (Moran's I) and local (Getis-Ord Gi∗) spatial autocorrelation and prospective space-time scan statistics (SaTScan) were applied. RESULTS: National incidence: 76.4/100,000/year. 2025 rate-adjusted Moran's I = 0.328 (p = 0.001). Raw-case hotspots: Greater Dhaka; population-adjusted hotspots: Jhalokathi, Barguna (coastal). High-rate space-time cluster (2024-2025): Dhaka, Manikganj (RR = 3.13, p = 0.001). Dhaka O/E = 4.31 (RR = 6.38). Two low-rate clusters (north, southeast). CONCLUSION: Urban hyperendemicity, characterized by a high case volume, coupled with the emerging coastal per-capita risk, necessitates tailored interventions. Intensive vector control is essential in Dhaka, while early-warning surveillance should be implemented in coastal districts.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue has shifted from Dhaka-centric to nationwide in Bangladesh. This study aims to quantify spatiotemporal patterns and identify high-risk clusters across all 64 districts from 2021 to 2025, extending previous reports with data from 2025 to observe an ongoing shift in per-capita risk hotspots.
Design and population
A retrospective ecological study was conducted using aggregate yearly data on suspected and laboratory-confirmed dengue admissions (n = 637,626 cases) from the Directorate General of Health Services (DGHS) across all 64 districts in Bangladesh from 2021 to 2025.
Core methods
Incidence per 100,000 population was calculated using 2022 census projections with 1.12% annual growth. Global (Moran's I) and local (Getis-Ord Gi∗) spatial autocorrelation and prospective space-time scan statistics (SaTScan) were applied to analyze spatiotemporal patterns.
Main results
The national incidence rate was 76.4/100,000/year. In 2025, the rate-adjusted Moran's I was 0.328 (p = 0.001). Raw-case hotspots were identified in Greater Dhaka, while population-adjusted hotspots were in Jhalokathi and Barguna (coastal). A high-rate space-time cluster (2024-2025) was found in Dhaka and Manikganj (RR = 3.13, p = 0.001). Dhaka had an O/E ratio of 4.31 (RR = 6.38). Two low-rate clusters were identified in the north and southeast regions.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights urban hyperendemicity in Dhaka and the emerging coastal per-capita risk, indicating a shift in dengue transmission patterns that requires tailored interventions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest the need for intensive vector control in Dhaka and early-warning surveillance in coastal districts to address the changing risk landscape of dengue.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on aggregate data, which may limit the ability to infer individual-level risk factors. The evidence is moderate due to the use of ecological analysis and the potential for spatial autocorrelation bias.
