社论:美洲新兴虫媒病毒:流行病学、公共卫生影响和未来准备

Editorial: Emerging arboviruses in the Americas: epidemiology, public health impact, and future preparedness
作者： André Ricardo Ribas Freitas, Marta Giovanetti, Luciano Pamplona de Góes Cavalcanti, Pedro María Alarcón-Elbal
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这篇社论强调了虫媒病毒疾病日益严重的全球负担,特别是登革热、基孔肯雅热、黄热病和奥罗普切病毒,这些疾病正在地理和生态上扩展到传统流行地区之外。它强调需要解决有关低估疾病负担和 传播 驱动因素的持续知识差距。
设计与对象
该社论综合了美洲和全球多项研究和 监测 报告的结果,重点关注虫媒病毒疾病及其 媒介。它整合了来自国家级分析、基因组研究和生态研究的数据,但没有指定单一的研究设计或人群。
核心方法
该社论依靠国家 监测 数据、基因组测序、昆虫学调查和生态模型来评估虫媒病毒 监测 动态和疾病负担。它还纳入了对 媒介 能力、阻力 突变 以及影响 传播 的人类行为因素的研究。
主要结果
社论报道称,巴西的登革热 传播 在 2024 年呈现出显着的精细尺度异质性,在地理空间上聚集在各个城市。报告还指出,基孔肯雅热造成了反复的流行浪潮,其中包括 2025 年在留尼汪岛发生的一次重大 暴发,估计有 208 名老年人死亡,病死率为 2.4%。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明由于 监测 系统的漏报,虫媒病毒疾病(尤其是基孔肯雅热)的真正负担一直被低估。他们强调了记录黄热病扩展到包括圣保罗州在内的新地区以及在白纹伊蚊中检测到黄热病病毒的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,迫切需要改进 监测、有针对性的 媒介 控制和增强 疫苗接种 覆盖范围,以减轻虫媒病毒疾病日益扩大的威胁。他们还强调解决 疫苗接种 覆盖次优问题和驱动 传播 的生态因素的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该社论基于多种研究和 监测 报告的综合,这可能会导致数据质量和可比性方面的差异。证据强度中等,因为它依赖于汇总数据和建模而不是对照研究,并且一些发现来自摘要或初步报告。

Editorial: Emerging arboviruses in the Americas: epidemiology, public health impact, and future preparedness

Authors: André Ricardo Ribas Freitas, Marta Giovanetti, Luciano Pamplona de Góes Cavalcanti, Pedro María Alarcón-Elbal
Original abstract (English)
The burden of arboviral diseases is intensifying rather than stabilizing. Dengue continues to reach unprecedented levels across the Americas, while geospatial clustering across Brazilian municipalities in 2024 highlights substantial fine-scale heterogeneity in transmission, underscoring the need for targeted and locally tailored control measures. This challenge is not confined to endemic regions: screening of blood donors in Shenzhen, China, illustrates how arboviruses can silently move through global travel networks and establish risks far beyond their traditional areas of circulation. Chikungunya provides a compelling example of the recurring epidemic waves that may characterize the coming decades. Twenty years after the devastating 2005-2006 epidemic, Réunion Island experienced another major outbreak in 2025. Our analysis estimated roughly 208 excess deaths among older adults during a three-month period; the age-specific excess mortality rate reached 145.3 per 100,000 (95% CI: 125.5-165.0) with a case fatality rate (CFR) of 2.4%, substantially exceeding officially reported fatalities (1). National-level analyses from Brazil similarly demonstrate a significant burden of hospitalization and mortality associated with chikungunya infection (2). Together, these findings suggest that the true impact of chikungunya has been consistently underestimated. The Réunion experience serves as a warning for the Americas, where more than a decade has passed since the virus became established following its introduction in 2014-2015. Conditions favorable for a comparable resurgence may already be in place. A recurring lesson emerges across arboviral diseases: surveillance systems based primarily on laboratory-confirmed cases frequently underestimate the true burden of infection and disease.Vectors remain central to this intensification. Several contributions in this Research Topic highlight the increasingly complex ecological and epidemiological factors shaping arboviral transmission. Studies range from the growing prevalence of pyrethroid resistance (kdr) mutations in Aedes aegypti populations in northeastern Argentina-threatening the long-term effectiveness of conventional vector control-to transcriptomic investigations of Wolbachia in this mosquito species, which reveal promising avenues for biological control interventions. Human factors also contribute substantially. A study assessing knowledge and attitudes toward chikungunya among healthcare workers in Chengdu, China, identified important awareness gaps that may delay recognition and diagnosis. Against this backdrop, climate variability-including the prospect of future El Niño events-is likely to amplify transmission risks and contribute to increasingly severe dengue seasons, alongside renewed activity of chikungunya, yellow fever, and Oropouche virus.The most concerning trend emerging from this collection is the continued geographic and ecological expansion of arboviruses. Yellow fever activity has intensified markedly, with 2025 recording the highest annual case count since 2020 and transmission extending into areas without a historical record of disease occurrence, including São Paulo State (3). Genomic investigation demonstrates the virus re-emerging across forest and periurban environments while advancing along forest edges toward densely populated, and often under-vaccinated, communities (4). Importantly, the prospect of yellow fever re-urbanization is no longer merely theoretical. Yellow fever virus has been detected and isolated from Aedes albopictus in an urban green area of São Paulo State (5). At the same time, Aedes vittatus-first recorded in the Americas in the Dominican Republic in 2019 (6) and now expanding across both insular and continental Caribbean regions-represents another potential bridge vector connecting sylvatic and urban transmission cycles (7). In this context, suboptimal vaccination coverage in newly affected areas remains the most important modifiable vulnerability.Oropouche virus presents a parallel narrative of geographic expansion. Once considered largely confined to the Amazon Basin, the virus has increasingly demonstrated its capacity to spread beyond its historical range. Surveillance efforts in wild birds at rehabilitation centers in Florida are now evaluating whether competent vertebrate hosts exist in North America, an increasingly relevant question given the growing number of imported human infections. Within Brazil, genomic surveillance recently documented the first imported case in Rio Grande do Sul, alongside enzootic circulation of Culicoides paraensis, signaling a tangible outbreak risk in one of the southernmost regions of the country (8). Additional studies further emphasize the importance of vigilance at the margins of known transmission zones. The detection of alphavirus circulation in Costa Rica, together with the description of the first case of West Nile virus meningoencephalitis in southwest Michigan, illustrates how arboviruses continue to establish themselves in new ecological settings, host populations, and vulnerable patient groups. Early recognition of such transitions remains critical for effective public health response.As arboviruses expand into new territories, their clinical spectrum is also being redefined. Oropouche virus, historically regarded as a self-limiting febrile illness, has increasingly emerged as a pathogen capable of causing severe neurological disease and adverse congenital outcomes. Experimental evidence demonstrating enhanced virulence and neuroinvasive capacity of contemporary Oropouche virus strains in the AG129 mouse model provides biological support for these observations. Clinical reports describing neurological manifestations-including Guillain-Barré syndrome accompanied by bilateral visual impairment associated with an emerging reassortant lineage in Ceará ( 9)-further expand the recognized disease phenotype. The possibility of congenital Oropouche syndrome has also gained increasing attention. A hypothesis initially raised within this Research Topic has since been reinforced by reports of newborns presenting with microcephaly following maternal infection in Brazil (10). These findings suggest that the public health implications of Oropouche virus may be substantially broader than previously appreciated. More broadly, mechanistic studies included in this collection contribute valuable insights into arboviral pathogenesis. For example, evidence demonstrating that disruption of a glycosylation site in the Zika virus envelope protein attenuates viral replication provides important clues regarding determinants of flaviviral virulence and identifies potential targets for future intervention strategies.Chikungunya reinforces this theme from a different perspective. Rather than revealing a new clinical syndrome, recent evidence highlights a long-recognized but consistently underreported outcome: mortality. The findings from Réunion Island and Brazil clearly indicate that chikungunya-associated deaths remain substantially underestimated by conventional surveillance systems (1,2).Several contributions in this Research Topic point toward the strategies needed to address future arboviral threats. Reviews examining current and emerging vector-borne disease risks, together with analyses of knowledge gaps surrounding Caribbean arboviral vectors, collectively support the adoption of integrated One Health approaches that recognize the interconnected roles of humans, animals, vectors, and the environment. As recently emphasized, closing both geographical and methodological gaps in arbovirus surveillance is essential for strengthening preparedness in an era of accelerating environmental change (11). Because case-based surveillance consistently underestimates the true burden of disease, complementary approaches are urgently needed. These include excess-mortality analyses and syndromic surveillance to identify otherwise unrecognized fatalities; integrated entomological and virological surveillance; monitoring of non-human primate mortality as an early warning system for yellow fever circulation; blood-bank screening as a sentinel tool for silent transmission; and minimally invasive tissue sampling to improve the investigation of arboviral deaths, an approach shown to be broadly acceptable in Brazil (12). These surveillance efforts must be accompanied by strengthened vaccination strategies. For yellow fever, as urgently called for by PAHO (3), vaccination coverage must be expanded across all populations exposed to sylvatic and rural transmission settings and increased until it attains at least the population-level protective threshold capable of preventing re-urbanization in newly affected areas. Likewise, broader implementation of available vaccines against dengue and chikungunya will be essential to reduce future disease burden. Together, enhanced surveillance, improved diagnostics, expanded vaccination, strengthened postmortem investigations, and timely public health interventions constitute the foundation of effective preparedness. Emerging arboviruses in the Americas-and increasingly worldwide-do not represent a single problem with a single solution. Rather, they comprise a complex and evolving constellation of threats that require tailored responses. Recognizing the distinct epidemiological, ecological, and clinical dimensions of each risk is the first step toward building the preparedness that the region urgently needs.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The editorial highlights the intensifying global burden of arboviral diseases, particularly dengue, chikungunya, yellow fever, and Oropouche virus, which are expanding geographically and ecologically beyond traditional endemic regions. It emphasizes the need to address persistent knowledge gaps regarding underestimated disease burden and the drivers of transmission.
Design and population
The editorial synthesizes findings from multiple studies and surveillance reports across the Americas and globally, focusing on arboviral diseases and their vectors. It integrates data from national-level analyses, genomic investigations, and ecological studies without specifying a single study design or population.
Core methods
The editorial relies on national surveillance data, genomic sequencing, entomological surveys, and ecological modeling to assess arboviral transmission dynamics and disease burden. It also incorporates studies on vector competence, resistance mutations, and human behavioral factors influencing transmission.
Main results
The editorial reports that dengue transmission in Brazil shows substantial fine-scale heterogeneity, with geospatial clustering across municipalities in 2024. It also notes that chikungunya has caused recurring epidemic waves, including a major outbreak in Réunion Island in 2025 with an estimated 208 excess deaths among older adults and a case fatality rate of 2.4%.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these findings as evidence that the true burden of arboviral diseases, particularly chikungunya, has been consistently underestimated due to underreporting in surveillance systems. They highlight the novelty of documenting the expansion of yellow fever into new regions, including São Paulo State, and the detection of yellow fever virus in Aedes albopictus.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the urgent need for improved surveillance, targeted vector control, and enhanced vaccination coverage to mitigate the expanding threat of arboviral diseases. They also emphasize the importance of addressing suboptimal vaccination coverage and ecological factors driving transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The editorial is based on a synthesis of diverse studies and surveillance reports, which may introduce variability in data quality and comparability. The evidence strength is moderate, as it relies on aggregated data and modeling rather than controlled studies, and some findings are derived from abstracts or preliminary reports.

使用免疫信息学和结构评估开发针对登革热和基孔肯雅病毒混合感染的潜在 疫苗

Development of a potential vaccine against dengue and chikungunya virus co-infection using immunoinformatics and structural evaluation
作者： Sadeeq Ullah, Junhong Xie, Caixia Guo, Hizbullah Khan, Xiao Zou, Tianwen Lai, Abdullah Shah, Rehmat Islam, Jiahui Luo, Wenqi Xu
摘要中文翻译登革病毒 (DENV) 和基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 共同感染在两种虫媒病毒共同传播的地区构成了越来越大的挑战,这凸显了采取综合 疫苗 策略的必要性。在这里,我们使用基于免疫信息学的方法设计了针对 DENV NS1 蛋白和 CHIKV NSP1 蛋白保守区域的多表位亚基 疫苗 候选物。根据抗原性、安全相关筛选和群体覆盖度选择保守的 CTL、HTL 和 B 细胞表位,然后用免疫学上合适的接头和 N 端人 β-防御素-2 佐剂进行组装。经过三维建模和验证后,该结构显示出良好的物理化学特征和可接受的结构质量。对接分析表明与人 TLR4 具有稳定的相互作用,并通过正常模式分析和在 AMBER v24 中使用 ff19SB 力场进行的 100 ns 全原子分子动力学模拟进一步检查。轨迹分析支持 疫苗-TLR4 复合物的构象稳定性,MM/GBSA 计算表明良好的结合能量。密码子优化支持在大肠杆菌 pET-28a(+) 系统中表达的可行性。在计算机免疫模拟中,进一步预测了体液和细胞 免疫应答 的诱导,并增加了抗体的产生和免疫记忆的发展。这些结果支持所提出的构建体作为针对 DENV-CHIKV 联合感染的 疫苗 策略的进一步实验评估的合理候选者。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革病毒 (DENV) 和基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 共同感染在两种虫媒病毒共同传播的地区构成了越来越大的挑战,这凸显了采取综合 疫苗 策略的必要性。

在这里,我们使用基于免疫信息学的方法设计了针对 DENV NS1 蛋白和 CHIKV NSP1 蛋白保守区域的多表位亚基 疫苗 候选物。
核心方法
根据抗原性、安全相关筛选和群体覆盖度选择保守的 CTL、HTL 和 B 细胞表位,然后用免疫学上合适的接头和 N 端人 β-防御素-2 佐剂进行组装。
主要结果
经过三维建模和验证后,该结构显示出良好的物理化学特征和可接受的结构质量。
解释与创新
对接分析表明与人 TLR4 具有稳定的相互作用,并通过正常模式分析和在 AMBER v24 中使用 ff19SB 力场进行的 100 ns 全原子分子动力学模拟进一步检查。
科研与公卫意义
轨迹分析支持 疫苗-TLR4 复合物的构象稳定性,MM/GBSA 计算表明良好的结合能量。
局限与证据强度
密码子优化支持在大肠杆菌 pET-28a(+) 系统中表达的可行性。

Development of a potential vaccine against dengue and chikungunya virus co-infection using immunoinformatics and structural evaluation

Authors: Sadeeq Ullah, Junhong Xie, Caixia Guo, Hizbullah Khan, Xiao Zou, Tianwen Lai, Abdullah Shah, Rehmat Islam, Jiahui Luo, Wenqi Xu
Original abstract (English)
Dengue virus (DENV) and chikungunya virus (CHIKV) co-infection poses a growing challenge in regions where both arboviruses co-circulate, highlighting the need for integrated vaccine strategies. Here, we designed a multi-epitope subunit vaccine candidate targeting conserved regions of the DENV NS1 protein and the CHIKV NSP1 protein using an immunoinformatics-based approach. Conserved CTL, HTL, and B-cell epitopes were selected on the basis of antigenicity, safety-related screening, and population coverage, then assembled with immunologically appropriate linkers and an N-terminal human β-defensin-2 adjuvant. The construct displayed favourable physicochemical features and acceptable structural quality after three-dimensional modeling and validation. Docking analysis indicated stable interaction with human TLR4, which was further examined by normal-mode analysis and 100-ns all-atom molecular dynamics simulations performed in AMBER v24 with the ff19SB force field. Trajectory analyses supported conformational stability of the vaccine-TLR4 complex, and MM/GBSA calculations suggested favourable binding energetics. Codon optimization supported expression feasibility in the E. coli pET-28a(+) system. In silico immune simulation further predicted induction of both humoral and cellular immune responses, with increased antibody production and immune-memory development. These results support the proposed construct as a rational candidate for further experimental evaluation toward a vaccine strategy against DENV-CHIKV co-infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus (DENV) and chikungunya virus (CHIKV) co-infection poses a growing challenge in regions where both arboviruses co-circulate, highlighting the need for integrated vaccine strategies.
Design and population
Here, we designed a multi-epitope subunit vaccine candidate targeting conserved regions of the DENV NS1 protein and the CHIKV NSP1 protein using an immunoinformatics-based approach.
Core methods
Conserved CTL, HTL, and B-cell epitopes were selected on the basis of antigenicity, safety-related screening, and population coverage, then assembled with immunologically appropriate linkers and an N-terminal human β-defensin-2 adjuvant.
Main results
The construct displayed favourable physicochemical features and acceptable structural quality after three-dimensional modeling and validation.
Interpretation and novelty
Docking analysis indicated stable interaction with human TLR4, which was further examined by normal-mode analysis and 100-ns all-atom molecular dynamics simulations performed in AMBER v24 with the ff19SB force field.
Scientific and public-health significance
Trajectory analyses supported conformational stability of the vaccine-TLR4 complex, and MM/GBSA calculations suggested favourable binding energetics.
Limitations and evidence strength
Codon optimization supported expression feasibility in the E. coli pET-28a(+) system.

使用嵌套时间序列交叉验证评估网格搜索优化在支持 媒介 回归登革热预测中的统计意义

Evaluating the Statistical Significance of Grid Search Optimization in Support Vector Regression for Dengue Forecasting Using Nested Time-Series Cross-Validation
作者： Ovien Yoga Caesarizky, Ahmad Ilham, Laelatul Khikmah, Faiq Madani
摘要中文翻译背景支持 媒介 回归 (SVR) 广泛用于流行病预测,因为核方法可以有效地模拟非线性、多变量和滞后的气候与疾病关系。虽然从业者通常应用详尽的网格搜索超参数优化,但这种计算成本高昂的调整是否在严格、无泄漏的时间序列验证下产生统计上显着的预测增强仍然是一个悬而未决的经验问题。目的 严格评估基于 SVR 的每周登革热病例预测的网格搜索超参数优化的统计意义和实用性。方法 本研究利用 1990 年至 2013 年波多黎各圣胡安和秘鲁伊基托斯公开的 DengAI 基准(N = 1,872 个城市周)构建了一条严格无泄漏的管道。该管道包含中值插补、训练专用的 Z 分数异常值缓解、标准化和主成分分析。我们将默认的 SVR 配置与针对正则化常数、ε 不敏感管和核系数进行优化的网格调整 SVR 进行了比较。应用了三种验证协议:时间顺序 80:20 保留、滚动源时间序列交叉验证以及利用五个外折叠的嵌套时间序列交叉验证。统计推断依赖于应用于外折叠误差的成对 Wilcoxon 符号秩检验,并辅以 Cohen 的 dz 效应大小、百分位引导 95% 置信区间和 Holm-Bonferroni 多重校正。通过系统地改变保留主成分的数量来检查方法的稳健性。结果 在按时间顺序保留的评估中,网格搜索将伊基托斯的平均绝对误差 (MAE) 减少了 0.30%,在圣胡安减少了 0.59%。在嵌套交叉验证下,调整模型和默认模型之间的 MAE 平均差异表明,伊基托斯的案例减少了 0.024 个案例(p = 1.0000),而圣胡安的案例增加了 0.494 个案例(p = 0.1875)。经过多重校正后,这两个结果均未达到统计显着性。此外,所选的超参数在外部验证折叠中表现出严重的不稳定,特别是对于圣胡安。优化过程还分别导致每个城市额外增加 11.48 秒和 14.80 秒的计算挂钟开销,但没有带来相应的准确性优势。结论 在 DengAI 基准测试和强大的无泄漏预处理管道的背景下,默认的 SVR 配置在统计上与其网格调整的对应配置没有区别。这些发现表明,流行病预测中的超参数优化不应自动执行。相反,它必须由预先注册的最小利益效应大小和明确的计算停止规则进行战略指导。本研究提出的综合方法框架为评估其他低倍时间序列预测领域的优化效用提供了一个潜在的可移植框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
背景支持 媒介 回归 (SVR) 广泛用于流行病预测,因为核方法可以有效地模拟非线性、多变量和滞后的气候与疾病关系。虽然从业者通常应用详尽的网格搜索超参数优化,但这种计算成本高昂的调整是否在严格、无泄漏的时间序列验证下产生统计上显着的预测增强仍然是一个悬而未决的经验问题。
设计与对象
本研究利用 1990 年至 2013 年(N = 1,872 个城市周)波多黎各圣胡安和秘鲁伊基托斯公开的 DengAI 基准构建了一条严格无泄漏的管道。该管道包含中值插补、训练专用的 Z 分数异常值缓解、标准化和主成分分析。
核心方法
该研究将默认 SVR 配置与针对正则化常数、ε 不敏感管和核系数进行优化的网格调整 SVR 进行了比较。应用了三种验证协议:时间顺序 80:20 保留、滚动源时间序列交叉验证以及利用五个外折叠的嵌套时间序列交叉验证。统计推断依赖于应用于外折叠误差的成对 Wilcoxon 符号秩检验,并辅以 Cohen 的 dz 效应大小、百分位引导 95% 置信区间和 Holm-Bonferroni 多重校正。
主要结果
在按时间顺序保留的评估中,网格搜索将伊基托斯的平均绝对误差 (MAE) 减少了 0.30%,在圣胡安减少了 0.59%。在嵌套交叉验证下,调整模型和默认模型之间的 MAE 平均差异表明,伊基托斯的案例减少了 0.024 个案例(p = 1.0000),而圣胡安的案例增加了 0.494 个案例(p = 0.1875)。经过多重校正后,这两个结果均未达到统计显着性。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,流行病预测中的超参数优化不应自动执行。相反,它必须由预先注册的最小利益效应大小和明确的计算停止规则进行战略指导。
科研与公卫意义
本研究提出的综合方法框架为评估其他低倍时间序列预测领域的优化效用提供了一个潜在的可移植框架。
局限与证据强度
优化过程分别导致每个城市额外增加 11.48 秒和 14.80 秒的计算挂钟开销,但没有带来相应的准确性优势。所选的超参数在外部验证折叠中表现出严重的不稳定,特别是对于圣胡安。

Evaluating the Statistical Significance of Grid Search Optimization in Support Vector Regression for Dengue Forecasting Using Nested Time-Series Cross-Validation

Authors: Ovien Yoga Caesarizky, Ahmad Ilham, Laelatul Khikmah, Faiq Madani
Original abstract (English)
Background Support Vector Regression (SVR) is widely employed for epidemic forecasting because kernel methods effectively model nonlinear, multivariate, and lagged climate-disease relationships. While practitioners routinely apply exhaustive grid-search hyperparameter optimization, whether such computationally expensive tuning produces statistically significant predictive enhancements under strict, leakage-free time-series validation remains an open empirical question. Objective To rigorously evaluate the statistical significance and practical utility of grid-search hyperparameter optimization for SVR-based weekly dengue case forecasting. Methods Utilizing the publicly available DengAI benchmark for San Juan, Puerto Rico, and Iquitos, Peru, from 1990 to 2013 (N = 1,872 city-weeks), this study constructs a strictly leakage-free pipeline. This pipeline incorporates median imputation, training-exclusive Z-score outlier mitigation, standardization, and Principal Component Analysis. We compared the default SVR configuration against a grid-tuned SVR optimized over the regularization constant, epsilon-insensitive tube, and kernel coefficient. Three validation protocols were applied: a chronological 80:20 hold-out, rolling-origin time-series cross-validation, and nested time-series cross-validation utilizing five outer folds. Statistical inference relied on paired Wilcoxon signed-rank tests applied to outer-fold errors, complemented by Cohen’s dz effect sizes, percentile-bootstrap 95% confidence intervals, and Holm-Bonferroni multiplicity correction. Methodological robustness was examined by systematically varying the number of retained principal components. Results On the chronological hold-out evaluation, the grid search reduced the Mean Absolute Error (MAE) by a marginal 0.30% in Iquitos and 0.59% in San Juan. Under nested cross-validation, the mean difference in MAE between the tuned and default models demonstrated a 0.024-case reduction in Iquitos (p = 1.0000) and a 0.494-case increase in San Juan (p = 0.1875). Neither result achieved statistical significance following multiplicity correction. Furthermore, the selected hyperparameters exhibited severe instability across the outer validation folds, particularly for San Juan. The optimization process also incurred an additional 11.48 seconds and 14.80 seconds of computational wall-clock overhead per city, respectively, without delivering a corresponding accuracy benefit. Conclusion Within the context of the DengAI benchmark and a robust leakage-free preprocessing pipeline, the default SVR configuration is statistically indistinguishable from its grid-tuned counterpart. These findings suggest that hyperparameter optimization in epidemic forecasting should not be executed automatically. Instead, it must be strategically guided by a pre-registered Smallest Effect Size of Interest and an explicit computational stopping rule. The comprehensive methodological framework presented in this study offers a potentially transferable framework for evaluating optimization utility in other low-fold time-series forecasting domains.

登革热 并发脑出血、急性呼吸衰竭和院内肺炎:A 病例报告

Dengue Fever Complicated by Intracerebral Haemorrhage, Acute Respiratory Failure and Nosocomial Pneumonia: A Case Report
作者： Ronak Shah, Parth Shah, Mittal Sindhav
摘要中文翻译登革热 是一种由 登革病毒 引起的蚊媒病毒性疾病。大多数病例具有自限性病程,极少数情况下重症登革热会导致致命的神经系统并发症,如脑炎和出血。这是一名 38 岁男性的 病例报告,他出现三天高烧、一天感觉改变、头痛和全身无力。血清学检测证实登革热 IgM 呈阳性。格拉斯哥昏迷评分 (GCS) 从 15 分快速下降至 11 分,导致临床病程变得复杂,在肺部分泌物带血的情况下,需要在第二天进行气管插管。胸部高分辨率计算机断层扫描(CT)提示合并肺水肿。脑部CT显示3.9×3.8×3.5厘米的脑实质出血(IPH),周围水肿,造成明显的占位效应。实验室参数记录了严重的血小板减少症(最低 0.6 lac/cumm)、凝血病、肝细胞损伤(SGOT/SGPT 峰值为 227/276 IU/L)以及 C 反应蛋白升高(85.6 mg/L)。第五天,气管内抽吸培养物中出现了广泛耐药的鲍曼不动杆菌,需要将抗菌治疗升级为美罗培南和多粘菌素。支持性治疗包括血小板和新鲜冰冻血浆(FFP)输注。患者在第九天成功拔管,并于第十二天出院回家。值得注意的是,在免疫功能正常的年轻成人中同时发生登革热相关脑出血 (ICH)、急性低氧血症性呼吸衰竭和多重耐药 (MDR) 院内肺炎是已发表文献中很少记录的三联征。早期识别神经功能恶化、及时进行神经影像学检查、积极的血液制品支持以及针对 ICH 的保守治疗的靶向抗菌治疗仍然是良好结果的关键决定因素。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了 登革热 罕见但严重的神经和肺部并发症,突显了免疫功能正常的成人同时发生脑出血、急性呼吸衰竭和医院肺炎的知识差距。
设计与对象
这是一名 38 岁男性的 病例报告,他出现三天高烧、感觉改变、头痛和全身无力,并在临床环境中接受治疗。
核心方法
该患者接受了登革热 IgM 血清学检测、实验室监测(包括血小板计数、凝血特征、肝酶和 C 反应蛋白),以及胸部和大脑的高分辨率 CT 成像。
主要结果
头颅CT显示3.9×3.8×3.5 cm脑实质出血,周围水肿,造成明显占位效应,胸部CT显示实变伴肺水肿。
解释与创新
作者将登革热相关脑出血、急性低氧血症性呼吸衰竭和耐多药院内肺炎同时发生解释为免疫功能健全的年轻成人中罕见记录的三联征。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了早期神经系统监测、及时神经影像学检查、积极的血液制品支持和靶向抗菌治疗在管理 重症登革热 并发症中的重要性。
局限与证据强度
作为单个病例报告,证据的普遍性有限,无法建立因果关系;由于缺乏比较数据或更广泛的 队列研究 验证,证据强度较低。

Dengue Fever Complicated by Intracerebral Haemorrhage, Acute Respiratory Failure and Nosocomial Pneumonia: A Case Report

Authors: Ronak Shah, Parth Shah, Mittal Sindhav
Original abstract (English)
Dengue fever is a mosquito-borne viral illness caused by the dengue virus. Majority of cases have self-limiting course, rarely severe dengue causes fatal neurological complications like encephalitis and haemorrhage. This is case report of a 38-year-old male who presented with three days of high-grade fever, one day of altered sensorium, headache, and generalised weakness. Serological testing confirmed dengue IgM positivity. The clinical course was complicated by a rapid decline in Glasgow Coma Scale (GCS) from 15 to 11, necessitating endotracheal intubation on day two in the setting of blood-stained pulmonary secretions. Highresolution Computed Tomography (CT) of the thorax suggested consolidation with pulmonary oedema. CT of the brain revealed a 3.9×3.8×3.5 cm Intracerebral Parenchymal Haemorrhage (IPH) with surrounding oedema causing significant mass effect. Laboratory parameters documented severe thrombocytopaenia (nadir 0.6 lac/cumm), coagulopathy, hepatocellular injury with peak SGOT/ SGPT of 227/276 IU/L, and elevated C-reactive protein at 85.6 mg/L. Endotracheal aspirate cultures grew extensively drug-resistant Acinetobacter baumannii on day five, requiring escalation of antimicrobial therapy to meropenem and polymyxin. Supportive management included platelet and Fresh Frozen Plasma (FFP) transfusion. The patient was successfully extubated on day nine and discharged home on day twelve. Notably, the simultaneous occurrence of dengue-associated Intracerebral Haemorrhage (ICH), acute hypoxaemic respiratory failure, and Multi-Drug Resistant (MDR) nosocomial pneumonia in a young immunocompetent adult represents a triad that is rarely documented in the published literature. Early recognition of neurological deterioration, timely neuroimaging, aggressive blood product support, and targeted antimicrobial therapy with conservative management of ICH remains critical determinants of a favourable outcome.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the rare but severe neurological and pulmonary complications of dengue fever, highlighting a knowledge gap regarding the simultaneous occurrence of intracerebral haemorrhage, acute respiratory failure, and nosocomial pneumonia in immunocompetent adults.
Design and population
This is a case report of a 38-year-old male who presented with three days of high-grade fever, altered sensorium, headache, and generalised weakness, and was managed in a clinical setting.
Core methods
The patient underwent serological testing for dengue IgM, laboratory monitoring including platelet counts, coagulation profiles, liver enzymes, and C-reactive protein, as well as imaging with high-resolution CT of the thorax and brain.
Main results
The CT of the brain revealed a 3.9×3.8×3.5 cm intracerebral parenchymal haemorrhage with surrounding oedema causing significant mass effect, while the thorax CT showed consolidation with pulmonary oedema.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the simultaneous occurrence of dengue-associated intracerebral haemorrhage, acute hypoxaemic respiratory failure, and multi-drug resistant nosocomial pneumonia as a rarely documented triad in a young immunocompetent adult.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the importance of early neurological monitoring, timely neuroimaging, aggressive blood product support, and targeted antimicrobial therapy in managing severe dengue complications.
Limitations and evidence strength
As a single case report, the evidence is limited in generalisability and cannot establish causality; the strength of evidence is low due to the absence of comparative data or broader cohort validation.

登革热感染儿科儿童肾功能不足:多米尼加共和国中心的布雷查斯诊断 登革热感染儿科患者的急性肾功能衰竭:多米尼加共和国中心的诊断差距

Insuficiencia renal aguda en pacientes pediátricos con infección por dengue: brechas diagnósticas en un centro de República Dominicana Acute renal failure in pediatric patients with dengue infection: diagnostic gaps at a center in the Dominican Republic
作者： Nicolle Marie Batlle-Núñez, Yanely López-Bencosme, Allyson Rodríguez-Román, Lisbeth Fernández-Méndez, Lisa José Domínguez-Rodríguez, Yokasta Elizabeth Méndez-Méndez, Luis López-Zabala, Anthony Gutiérrez-Martínez
摘要中文翻译简介:登革热是多米尼加共和国的一种地方病,可能与急性肾衰竭等并发症有关,特别是在儿科人群中。关于这种关系的证据有限且异构。本研究旨在评估多米尼加共和国中心的登革热临床 诊断 儿科患者的临床、实验室和肾功能特征。方法:进行了一项横断面、单中心研究。这些患者包括多米尼加共和国圣地亚哥洛斯卡巴列罗斯阿图罗·格鲁隆地区大学儿童医院出现登革热症状和体征的患者。样本由 44 名患者组成。主要结果变量是使用 pRIFLE 量表评估的肾损伤风险,以及使用登革热严重程度评分 (DSS) 测量的登革热临床严重程度。结果:根据 DSS,结果显示 35 名患者 (79.5%) 患有轻度登革热,其余 9 名患者 (20.5%) 患有中度登革热。血尿的发生率为 9.09%。最常见的临床症状是肌痛(15.9%)。血小板计数的中位数为 41,500 个细胞/mm3。血小板计数与登革热严重程度之间的关系具有统计学意义(p = 0.027)。结论:这项研究是制定多米尼加共和国登革热儿童患者综合全国特征的起点。此外,它还解决了儿科登革热患者诊断过程中的一个关键差距,凸显了改善准确诊断方法的紧迫性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热在多米尼加共和国流行,可能会导致儿童急性肾衰竭等并发症,但将登革热与儿童急性肾衰竭联系起来的证据仍然有限且异质,造成了本研究试图解决的知识差距。
设计与对象
多米尼加共和国圣地亚哥洛斯卡巴列罗斯的 Arturo Grullón 博士地区大学儿童医院开展了一项横断面、单中心研究,招募了 44 名具有登革热临床体征和症状的儿童患者。
核心方法
该研究使用 pRIFLE 量表评估肾损伤的风险,并使用登革热严重程度评分 (DSS) 以及临床和实验室变量评估登革热严重程度。
主要结果
在44名患者中,35名(79.5%)根据登革出血热严重程度评分(DSS)诊断为轻症登革热,9名(20.5%)为中度登革热;9.09%的患者记录有血尿,15.9%的患者出现肌痛,血小板计数中位数为41,500个/mm3,且血小板计数越低与登革热严重程度呈显著相关(p = 0.027)。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为全国儿科登革热特征描述的起点,并强调需要改进诊断方法,强调了该人群准确 诊断 方面的关键差距。
科研与公卫意义
该研究强调了加强儿科登革热诊断能力的紧迫性,以预防急性肾衰竭等并发症,并为流行病环境中的 监测 和临床管理提供信息。
局限与证据强度
单中心、横断面设计和小样本限制了普遍性和因果推断,需要中等强度的证据,并强调需要进行更大规模的多中心研究。

Insuficiencia renal aguda en pacientes pediátricos con infección por dengue: brechas diagnósticas en un centro de República Dominicana Acute renal failure in pediatric patients with dengue infection: diagnostic gaps at a center in the Dominican Republic

Authors: Nicolle Marie Batlle-Núñez, Yanely López-Bencosme, Allyson Rodríguez-Román, Lisbeth Fernández-Méndez, Lisa José Domínguez-Rodríguez, Yokasta Elizabeth Méndez-Méndez, Luis López-Zabala, Anthony Gutiérrez-Martínez
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: Dengue is an endemic disease in the Dominican Republic and may be associated with complications such as acute renal failure, especially in the pediatric population. Evidence on this relationship is limited and heterogeneous. This study aims to evaluate the clinical, laboratory, and renal function characteristics in pediatric patients with a clinical diagnosis of dengue at a center in the Dominican Republic. Methods: A cross-sectional, single-center study was conducted. The population consisted of patients with signs and symptoms of dengue at the Dr. Arturo Grullón Regional University Children's Hospital in Santiago de los Caballeros, Dominican Republic. The sample consisted of 44 patients. The main outcome variables were the risk of kidney injury, assessed using the pRIFLE scale, and the clinical severity of dengue, measured using the Dengue Severity Score (DSS). Results: The findings revealed a diagnosis of mild dengue in 35 patients (79.5%) and moderate disease in the remaining 9 (20.5%), according to the DSS. Hematuria was documented in 9.09%. The most frequent clinical symptom was myalgia (15.9%). The platelet count had a median of 41,500 cells/mm3. The relationship between platelet count and dengue severity was statistically significant (p = 0.027). Conclusions: This study is a starting point for developing a comprehensive national characterization of pediatric patients with dengue in the Dominican Republic. In addition, it addresses a critical gap in the diagnostic process for pediatric patients with dengue, highlighting the urgency of improving access to accurate diagnostic methods.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue is endemic in the Dominican Republic and may cause complications such as acute renal failure in children, yet evidence linking dengue to pediatric acute renal failure remains limited and heterogeneous, creating a knowledge gap that this study sought to address.
Design and population
A cross-sectional, single-center study was conducted at the Dr. Arturo Grullón Regional University Children's Hospital in Santiago de los Caballeros, Dominican Republic, enrolling 44 pediatric patients with clinical signs and symptoms of dengue.
Core methods
The study assessed the risk of kidney injury using the pRIFLE scale and dengue severity with the Dengue Severity Score (DSS) alongside clinical and laboratory variables.
Main results
Among 44 patients, 35 (79.5%) had mild dengue and 9 (20.5%) had moderate dengue according to the DSS; hematuria was documented in 9.09% and myalgia in 15.9%, with a median platelet count of 41,500 cells/mm3 and a statistically significant association between lower platelet count and greater dengue severity (p = 0.027).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as a starting point for national characterization of pediatric dengue and emphasize the need to improve diagnostic methods, highlighting a critical gap in accurate diagnosis for this population.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study underscores the urgency of strengthening diagnostic capacity for pediatric dengue to prevent complications such as acute renal failure and to inform surveillance and clinical management in endemic settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The single-center, cross-sectional design and small sample limit generalizability and causal inference, warranting a moderate strength of evidence and highlighting the need for larger, multi-center studies.

靶向 登革病毒 NS3 解旋酶:茶树儿茶素作为潜在治疗先导药物的生化和计算评估

Targeting Dengue Virus NS3 Helicase: Biochemical and Computational Evaluation of Catechins from Camellia sinensis as Potential Therapeutic Leads
作者： Milla K. Wojciechowski, L. D. Goyzueta-Mamani, M. Chávez-Fumagalli, Edward L. D’Antonio
摘要中文翻译登革病毒 血清型 2 是一种人类致病性黄病毒,编码非结构蛋白 3 (DEN2-NS3),一种超家族 2 病毒解旋酶。DEN2-NS3 包含 N 端丝氨酸蛋白酶结构域和 C 端 RNA 解旋酶/核苷 5'-三磷酸酶 (NTPase) 结构域,这对于病毒复制至关重要。在解旋酶/NTPase 结构域中,DEN2-NS3 利用 NTP 水解产生的能量,并具有从 3' 端到 5' 端的易位极性,优先选择双链 RNA 底物。先前报道,没食子酰化儿茶素 (-)-表没食子儿茶素没食子酸酯 (EGCG) 对寨卡病毒 NS3 解旋酶/NTPase 非常有效,观察到的 IC50 值为 295.7 nM。这促使人们进行一项调查,以确定三种儿茶素,即 EGCG、(−)-表儿茶素没食子酸酯 (ECG) 和 (−)-表没食子儿茶素 (EGC),是否可以作为 DEN2-NS3 的有效抑制剂。本研究中进行的酶抑制测定表明,解旋酶/NTPase 催化结构域 DEN2-NS3(S171-K618) 受到这些没食子酰化儿茶素的强烈抑制。在我们的酶联验证测定中,在测试浓度下,化合物观察到的微不足道的影响证实了这种抑制作用。观察到的测试化合物的抑制常数 (Ki) 值和表观抑制模式(相对于 NTPase 活性)为 EGCG(混合模式抑制)Ki = 400 ± 86.6 nM,ECG(非竞争性抑制)Ki = 550 ± 250 nM,EGC(混合模式抑制)Ki = 18.3 ± 4.2 μM。冠状病毒 nsp13 解旋酶参考抑制剂 SSYA10-001 还显示出对 DEN2-NS3 NTPase 活性的抑制作用,Ki = 10.2 ± 0.3 μM(混合模式抑制)。此外,使用从 SiteMap 开始的计算工作流程,我们提供了证据,表明 RNA 结合腔内存在高度可成药的口袋,涉及残基 ASP290、ARG387、ASP409、MET429、HIS487、ASP541、ARG599 和 ASP603。这些儿茶素均通过 200 纳秒分子动力学 (MD) 模拟进行分析,以评估目标 DEN2-NS3 结合袋内的结合稳定性。计算结果显示,EGCG 和 ECG 保持高度稳定性,与 ASP603、ARG599、ASP541 和 ARG387 形成共享、高度持久的氨基酸接触(>45% 占用率)。总之,我们的生化和计算数据支持 EGCG 和 ECG 作为没食子酰化儿茶素先导,并提出了一个合理的结合模型,涉及与 DEN2-NS3 核酸结合区相关的两亲口袋。我们建议,未来将这些化合物结构优化为稳定的前药衍生物,可能会产生有前途的 抗病毒药物 候选药物。图解摘要要点 EGCG 和 ECG 是 DEN2-NS3 解旋酶的高效抑制剂。这些没食子酰化儿茶素与 RNA 结合腔内的可成药口袋结合。MD 模拟(200 纳秒轨迹)揭示了连续的结合稳定性。关键相互作用残基包括 ASP603、ARG599、ASP541 和 ARG387。实验酶抑制证实了计算结果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革病毒 血清型 2 (DENV-2) 编码病毒复制必需的非结构蛋白 3 (DEN2-NS3) 解旋酶/NTPase,代表潜在的治疗靶点。先前的证据表明没食子酰化儿茶素可抑制黄病毒解旋酶,从而激发了针对 DEN2-NS3 的 EGCG、ECG 和 EGC 评估,从而推动了这项研究。
设计与对象
该研究采用生化酶抑制测定和计算模型来评估儿茶素作为 DEN2-NS3 解旋酶/NTPase 结构域的抑制剂。目标是 DEN2-NS3(S171-K618) 催化结构域,设计测定法来测量 NTPase 活性抑制。
核心方法
酶抑制测定评估了 EGCG、ECG 和 EGC 对 DEN2-NS3 NTPase 活性的影响,并确定了相对于 NTPase 活性的抑制常数 (Ki)。包括 SiteMap 在内的计算工作流程确定了 RNA 结合腔内的可药物口袋,200 纳秒分子动力学 (MD) 模拟评估了儿茶素与该口袋的结合稳定性。
主要结果
没食子酰化儿茶素 EGCG 和 ECG 强烈抑制 DEN2-NS3 NTPase 活性,Ki 值分别为 400 ± 86.6 nM(混合模式抑制)和 550 ± 250 nM(非竞争性抑制)。EGC 显示较弱的抑制(Ki = 18.3 ± 4.2 μM,混合模式)。MD 模拟显示 EGCG 和 ECG 具有高结合稳定性,与残基 ASP603、ARG599、ASP541 和 ARG387 形成持久接触。
解释与创新
作者将 EGCG 和 ECG 解释为针对 DEN2-NS3 RNA 结合腔内可药物口袋的有效先导化合物,并得到生化和计算证据的支持。新颖之处在于识别了特定的两亲性口袋并证明了这些儿茶素的稳定结合,这表明了一种合理的 抗病毒药物 机制。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持没食子酰化儿茶素(特别是 EGCG 和 ECG)作为针对 DEN2-NS3 解旋酶/NTPase 活性的 抗病毒药物 候选物的潜力。这可以为用于登革热治疗的儿茶素衍生物的结构引导优化的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外生化测定和计算模型,未经体内验证,因此对人类的治疗功效和安全性留下了不确定性。证据是中等的,因为它建立了生化抑制和结合稳定性,但缺乏相关模型中直接的 抗病毒药物 功效数据。

Targeting Dengue Virus NS3 Helicase: Biochemical and Computational Evaluation of Catechins from Camellia sinensis as Potential Therapeutic Leads

Authors: Milla K. Wojciechowski, L. D. Goyzueta-Mamani, M. Chávez-Fumagalli, Edward L. D’Antonio
Original abstract (English)
Dengue virus serotype 2 is a human pathogenic flavivirus that encodes a non-structural protein 3 (DEN2-NS3), a superfamily-2 viral helicase. DEN2-NS3 contains an N-terminal serine protease domain and a C-terminal RNA helicase/nucleoside 5’-triphosphatase (NTPase) domain that is essential for viral replication. In the helicase/NTPase domain, DEN2-NS3 utilizes energy derived from NTP hydrolysis and has a translocation polarity from the 3’-to-5’ end with preference of a duplex RNA substrate. A galloylated catechin, (−)-epigallocatechin gallate (EGCG), was previously reported to be highly potent against the Zika virus NS3 helicase/NTPase, with an IC50 value observed at 295.7 nM. This prompted an investigation to determine if three catechins, namely, EGCG, (−)-epicatechin gallate (ECG), and (−)-epigallocatechin (EGC), would act as potent inhibitors of DEN2-NS3. Enzyme-inhibition assays performed in this study revealed that the helicase/NTPase catalytic domain, DEN2-NS3(S171-K618), was strongly inhibited by these galloylated catechins. This inhibition was verified by the insignificant impact observed by the compounds on our enzyme-coupled confirmatory assay, at the concentrations tested. The observed inhibition constant (Ki) values and apparent modes of inhibition (relative to NTPase activity) of the tested compounds were Ki = 400 ± 86.6 nM for EGCG (mixed-mode inhibition), Ki = 550 ± 250 nM for ECG (uncompetitive inhibition), and Ki = 18.3 ± 4.2 μM for EGC (mixed-mode inhibition). The coronavirus nsp13 helicase reference inhibitor SSYA10-001 also revealed inhibition against DEN2-NS3 NTPase activity with a Ki = 10.2 ± 0.3 μM (mixed-mode inhibition). Furthermore, using a computational workflow starting with SiteMap, we provide evidence that a highly druggable pocket exists within the RNA-binding cavity, involving residues ASP290, ARG387, ASP409, MET429, HIS487, ASP541, ARG599, and ASP603. These catechins were each analyzed through 200-ns molecular dynamics (MD) simulations to evaluate the binding stability within the target DEN2-NS3 binding pocket. Computational results revealed that EGCG and ECG maintained high stability, forming shared, highly persistent amino acid contacts (>45% occupancy) with ASP603, ARG599, ASP541, and ARG387. In conclusion, our biochemical and computational data support EGCG and ECG as galloylated catechin leads and suggest a plausible binding model involving an amphipathic pocket associated with the nucleic acid binding region of DEN2-NS3. We suggest that future structural optimization of these compounds into stable prodrug derivatives could yield promising antiviral candidates. Graphical Abstract Highlights EGCG and ECG are highly potent inhibitors of the DEN2-NS3 helicase. These galloylated catechins bind to a druggable pocket within the RNA-binding cavity. MD simulations (200-ns trajectories) revealed continuous binding stability. Key interaction residues include ASP603, ARG599, ASP541, and ARG387. Experimental enzyme-inhibition corroborated with computational findings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus serotype 2 (DENV-2) encodes a non-structural protein 3 (DEN2-NS3) helicase/NTPase essential for viral replication, representing a potential therapeutic target. The study was prompted by prior evidence that galloylated catechins inhibit flavivirus helicases, motivating evaluation of EGCG, ECG, and EGC against DEN2-NS3.
Design and population
The study employed biochemical enzyme-inhibition assays and computational modeling to evaluate catechins as inhibitors of the DEN2-NS3 helicase/NTPase domain. The target was the DEN2-NS3(S171-K618) catalytic domain, and assays were designed to measure NTPase activity inhibition.
Core methods
Enzyme-inhibition assays assessed the impact of EGCG, ECG, and EGC on DEN2-NS3 NTPase activity, with inhibition constants (Ki) determined relative to NTPase activity. A computational workflow including SiteMap identified a druggable pocket within the RNA-binding cavity, and 200-ns molecular dynamics (MD) simulations evaluated binding stability of the catechins to this pocket.
Main results
Galloylated catechins EGCG and ECG strongly inhibited DEN2-NS3 NTPase activity with Ki values of 400 ± 86.6 nM (mixed-mode inhibition) and 550 ± 250 nM (uncompetitive inhibition), respectively. EGC showed weaker inhibition (Ki = 18.3 ± 4.2 μM, mixed-mode). MD simulations revealed high binding stability for EGCG and ECG, forming persistent contacts with residues ASP603, ARG599, ASP541, and ARG387.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret EGCG and ECG as potent leads targeting a druggable pocket within the DEN2-NS3 RNA-binding cavity, supported by both biochemical and computational evidence. The novelty lies in identifying a specific amphipathic pocket and demonstrating stable binding of these catechins, suggesting a plausible antiviral mechanism.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support the potential of galloylated catechins, particularly EGCG and ECG, as antiviral candidates targeting DEN2-NS3 helicase/NTPase activity. This could inform the development of structure-guided optimization of catechin derivatives for dengue therapy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro biochemical assays and computational modeling without in vivo validation, leaving uncertainty about therapeutic efficacy and safety in humans. The evidence is moderate, as it establishes biochemical inhibition and binding stability but lacks direct antiviral efficacy data in relevant models.

麦拉哈社区健康中心管辖区域内居民登革热知识水平与行为同登革热发病率的关系

Hubungan Tingkat Pengetahuan dan Perilaku Masyarakat Dengan Angka Kejadian Demam Berdarah Dengue di Wilayah Kerja Puskesmas Meuraxa Banda Aceh
作者： Zilla Nabila Aulia, Suriatu Laila, Fia Dewi Auliani
摘要中文翻译Demam Berdarah 登革热 (DBD) ialah penyakit infeksi 病毒 akut yang disebabkan oleh 病毒登革热 yang tergolong 节肢动物传播的病毒,黄病毒属,黄病毒科。印度尼西亚 Angka kejadian DBD 2023 年 11 月 hingga Maret 2024 dilaporkan kasus DBD 印度尼西亚 mencapai 53.131 orang dengan angka kematian sebesar 404 orang。Penyakit DBD 可能会影响使用效果和性能。请注意,本产品可能会影响 DBD 的性能。Jenis Penelitian ini adalah metode kuantitatif dengan pendekatan cross sections. Teknik pengambilan sampel dalam penelitian ini adalah 分层随机抽样。Sampel pada penelitian ini sebanyak 100 orang。分析数据分为单变量和双变量。Hasil uji uji univariat menunjukkan distribusi frekuensi tingkat pengetahuan masyarakat didominasi oleh kategori baik yaitu sebanyak 79 orang (79%) and perilaku masyarakat didominasi oleh kategori baik yaitu sebanyak 54 orang (54%)。Hasil uji bivariat menggunakan uji statistik chi-square didapatkan nilai p-value tingkat pengetahuan 0,000
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革病毒 在印度尼西亚引起发病率很高的急性发热性疾病,而 死亡率 则在印度尼西亚引起,但社区知识和行为在形成疾病 发病率 中的作用仍未得到充分了解。该研究旨在确定班达亚齐 Meuraxa 分区居民的知识和行为水平是否与登革热 发病率 相关。
设计与对象
在班达亚齐的 Meuraxa 分区进行了一项横断面定量研究,以评估社区知识、行为和登革热 发病率 之间的关系。采用分层随机抽样的方式总共招募了 100 名居民,并收集了他们的知识和行为水平的数据。
核心方法
使用结构化问卷收集数据,并使用单变量和双变量(卡方)检验进行分析,以评估知识、行为和登革热 发病率 之间的关联。抽样采用分层随机技术以确保代表性。
主要结果
单变量分析显示,79% 的参与者对登革热有良好的了解,54% 的参与者表现出良好的行为。使用卡方检验的双变量分析显示,知识水平与登革热 发病率 之间存在统计显着相关性 (p = 0.000)。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明较高的社区对登革热的了解与较低的疾病 发病率 相关,这表明有针对性的健康教育可以减少 传播。新颖之处在于使用横截面设计来量化 Meuraxa 分区的这种关联。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,通过教育活动提高社区知识可能会减少班达亚齐的登革热 发病率,从而支持有针对性的公共卫生干预措施。这强调了行为改变策略在登革热控制计划中的价值。
局限与证据强度
横截面设计限制了因果推断,小样本量 (n=100) 限制了 Meuraxa 之外的普遍性。由于潜在的混淆以及对自我报告的知识和行为数据的依赖,证据强度中等。

Hubungan Tingkat Pengetahuan dan Perilaku Masyarakat Dengan Angka Kejadian Demam Berdarah Dengue di Wilayah Kerja Puskesmas Meuraxa Banda Aceh

Authors: Zilla Nabila Aulia, Suriatu Laila, Fia Dewi Auliani
Original abstract (English)
Demam Berdarah Dengue (DBD) ialah penyakit infeksi virus akut yang disebabkan oleh virus dengue yang tergolong Arthropod-Borne virus, genus flavivirus, famili Flaviviridae. Angka kejadian DBD di Indonesia november 2023 hingga Maret 2024 dilaporkan kasus DBD di Indonesia mencapai 53.131 orang dengan angka kematian sebesar 404 orang. Penyakit DBD berkaitan dengan kondisi lingkungan dan perilaku masyarakat. Tujuan pada penelitian ini untuk mengetahui hubungan tingkat pengetahuan dan perilaku masyarakat dengan angka kejadian DBD. Jenis penelitian ini adalah metode kuantitatif dengan pendekatan cross sectional. Teknik pengambilan sampel dalam penelitian ini adalah stratified random sampling. Sampel pada penelitian ini sebanyak 100 orang. Analisis data menggunakan uji univariat dan bivariat. Hasil uji uji univariat menunjukkan distribusi frekuensi tingkat pengetahuan masyarakat didominasi oleh kategori baik yaitu sebanyak 79 orang (79%) dan perilaku masyarakat didominasi oleh kategori baik yaitu sebanyak 54 orang (54%). Hasil uji bivariat menggunakan uji statistik chi-square didapatkan nilai p-value tingkat pengetahuan 0,000
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus causes acute febrile illness with significant morbidity and mortality in Indonesia, yet the role of community knowledge and behaviour in shaping disease incidence remains insufficiently understood. The study aimed to determine whether levels of knowledge and behaviour among residents are associated with dengue incidence in the Meuraxa sub-district of Banda Aceh.
Design and population
A cross-sectional quantitative study was conducted in the Meuraxa sub-district of Banda Aceh to assess the relationship between community knowledge, behaviour, and dengue incidence. A total of 100 residents were enrolled using stratified random sampling, and data were collected on their knowledge and behaviour levels.
Core methods
Data were collected using structured questionnaires and analysed with univariate and bivariate (chi-square) tests to evaluate associations between knowledge, behaviour, and dengue incidence. Sampling employed stratified random techniques to ensure representativeness.
Main results
Univariate analysis showed that 79% of participants had good knowledge and 54% exhibited good behaviour regarding dengue. Bivariate analysis using the chi-square test revealed a statistically significant association between knowledge levels and dengue incidence (p = 0.000).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as evidence that higher community knowledge about dengue is associated with lower disease incidence, suggesting that targeted health education could reduce transmission. The novelty lies in quantifying this association in the Meuraxa sub-district using a cross-sectional design.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings imply that improving community knowledge through education campaigns may reduce dengue incidence in Banda Aceh, supporting targeted public health interventions. This underscores the value of behaviour-change strategies in dengue control programs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The cross-sectional design limits causal inference, and the small sample size (n=100) restricts generalisability beyond Meuraxa. The evidence strength is moderate due to potential confounding and reliance on self-reported knowledge and behaviour data.

探索马尼拉社区民众应对登革热挑战的生活经验 暴发

Exploring The Lived Experience in Challenges Among Community People in Manila Towards Dengue Outbreak
作者： Glenda Sison, Joel John Dela Merced
摘要中文翻译这项现象学研究探讨了马尼拉社区成员在登革热 暴发 期间的生活经历、挑战、教育遭遇、预防实践和应对反应。特意选择了九名在过去五年内亲自经历过登革热或有家庭成员被诊断出登革热的成年参与者。通过半结构化访谈指南收集数据,涵盖三个领域:登革热 暴发 期间的挑战、登革热教育和预防的经验和看法,以及通过教育和预防措施的应对措施。主题分析揭示了三组主要发现。首先,登革热通过工作中断、家庭责任转移、分离、缺课、恐惧、身体痛苦、经济紧张和护理困难等方式扰乱日常生活。其次,参与者对登革热的了解参差不齐,这些知识来自医院讲座、社区活动、个人经验和基本意识,而教育则鼓励警惕、清理活动、雾化和早期咨询。然而,排水不畅、积水、附近水道、户外暴露、社区参与不一致以及相互竞争的责任限制了持续的预防。第三,参与者通过家庭清洁、清除积水、使用驱虫剂和杀虫剂、穿防护服、早期咨询、补水、家庭支持和社区参与来应对。该研究得出的结论是,登革热是一个多层面的家庭和社区问题,需要临床、教育、环境、情感和社会反应。它建议持续、可及且包容儿童的健康教育;协调社区清理和 媒介 控制活动;改善排水和监测;以家庭为中心的医疗保健;及时转介和监测;居民、医护人员、学校、描笼涯官员、地方政府单位和卫生部之间加强合作以实现可持续发展。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了马尼拉社区成员在登革热 暴发 期间面临的多维挑战,旨在了解生活经历、教育经历、预防措施和应对措施,为公共卫生策略提供信息。
设计与对象
一项现象学定性研究有目的地对马尼拉的九名成年参与者进行了抽样调查,这些参与者在过去五年内亲自经历过登革热或有家庭成员被诊断出登革热。
核心方法
使用半结构化访谈指南收集数据,涵盖 暴发 期间的挑战、登革热教育和预防经验,以及通过教育和预防措施应对的反应。
主要结果
专题分析确定了三个主要发现:登革热通过工作中断、家庭责任、恐惧、身体痛苦、经济压力和护理困难扰乱了日常生活;参与者对来自多个来源的登革热的了解参差不齐;应对策略包括家庭清洁、驱虫剂、防护服、早期咨询和社区参与。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,登革热是一个多层面的家庭和社区问题,需要综合的临床、教育、环境、情感和社会应对措施,强调需要持续、可及和儿童包容性的健康教育和协调的 媒介 控制活动。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持以下建议:改善排水和 监测、以家庭为中心的医疗保健、及时转诊和监测以及加强居民、医护人员、学校、描笼涯官员、地方政府单位和卫生部之间的合作,以减轻登革热的影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究的小目标样本和定性设计限制了普遍性,并且对自我报告经历的依赖可能会引入回忆和社会期望偏差,因此需要谨慎地将证据强度解释为中等。

Exploring The Lived Experience in Challenges Among Community People in Manila Towards Dengue Outbreak

Authors: Glenda Sison, Joel John Dela Merced
Original abstract (English)
This phenomenological study explored the lived experiences, challenges, educational encounters, preventive practices, and coping responses of community members in Manila during dengue outbreaks. Nine adult participants who had personally experienced dengue or had a family member diagnosed within the past five years were purposively selected. Data were gathered through a semi-structured interview guide covering three domains: challenges during dengue outbreaks, experiences and perceptions of dengue education and prevention, and coping responses through education and preventive measures. Thematic analysis revealed three major sets of findings. First, dengue disrupted daily life through work interruption, shifting family responsibilities, separation, school absence, fear, physical suffering, financial strain, and caregiving difficulties. Second, participants displayed uneven knowledge of dengue, drawn from hospital lectures, community activities, personal experience, and basic awareness, while education encouraged vigilance, cleanup drives, fogging, and early consultation. However, poor drainage, stagnant water, nearby waterways, outdoor exposure, inconsistent neighborhood participation, and competing responsibilities limited sustained prevention. Third, participants coped through household cleaning, removal of stagnant water, use of repellents and insecticides, protective clothing, early consultation, hydration, family support, and community participation. The study concluded that dengue is a multidimensional household and community concern requiring clinical, educational, environmental, emotional, and social responses. It recommends sustained, accessible, and child-inclusive health education; coordinated community clean-up and vector-control activities; improved drainage and surveillance; family-centered healthcare; timely referral and monitoring; and stronger collaboration among residents, healthcare workers, schools, barangay officials, local government units, and the Department of Health for sustainability.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the multidimensional challenges faced by community members in Manila during dengue outbreaks, aiming to understand lived experiences, educational encounters, preventive practices, and coping responses to inform public health strategies.
Design and population
A phenomenological qualitative study purposively sampled nine adult participants in Manila who had personally experienced dengue or had a family member diagnosed within the past five years.
Core methods
Data were collected using a semi-structured interview guide covering challenges during outbreaks, dengue education and prevention experiences, and coping responses through education and preventive measures.
Main results
Thematic analysis identified three major findings: dengue disrupted daily life through work interruption, family responsibilities, fear, physical suffering, financial strain, and caregiving difficulties; participants had uneven knowledge of dengue drawn from multiple sources; and coping strategies included household cleaning, repellents, protective clothing, early consultation, and community participation.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that dengue is a multidimensional household and community concern requiring integrated clinical, educational, environmental, emotional, and social responses, highlighting the need for sustained, accessible, and child-inclusive health education and coordinated vector-control activities.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support recommendations for improved drainage and surveillance, family-centered healthcare, timely referral and monitoring, and stronger collaboration among residents, healthcare workers, schools, barangay officials, local government units, and the Department of Health to mitigate dengue impact.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's small purposive sample and qualitative design limit generalizability, and reliance on self-reported experiences may introduce recall and social desirability biases, warranting cautious interpretation of the evidence strength as moderate.

斯里兰卡登革热仪表板:斯里兰卡登革热 监测 基于网络的决策支持工具

Sri Lanka Dengue Dashboard: A Web-Based Decision Support Tool for Dengue Surveillance in Sri Lanka
作者： Y. M. Amali P. Rajapaksha
摘要中文翻译这些天,斯里兰卡正面临着重症登革热 暴发。在这种情况下,全面了解斯里兰卡登革热历史趋势以及当前登革热形势非常有用。仪表板是向公众传达大量信息的简单有效的方式。因此,斯里兰卡登革热仪表板是提供有关斯里兰卡登革热状况的全面信息以及气候因素与登革热病例之间关系的初步尝试。关键词:数据可视化、热图、气候-登革热关系、树图、时间序列分析、四开本、仪表板、登革热

斯里兰卡正在经历 重症登革热 暴发,因此需要总结历史趋势和当前状况的工具。

核心方法
提供的证据没有报告用于构建仪表板的具体方法、数据源或分析程序。
主要结果
提供的证据并未报告仪表板中的任何定量结果或发现。

作者将斯里兰卡登革热仪表板作为提供全面登革热信息和探索气候与登革热关系的初步尝试。
科研与公卫意义
提供易于使用的可视化工具可以帮助公众认识斯里兰卡的登革热 监测 并支持决策。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据不报告研究局限性或证据强度评估。

Sri Lanka Dengue Dashboard: A Web-Based Decision Support Tool for Dengue Surveillance in Sri Lanka

Authors: Y. M. Amali P. Rajapaksha
Original abstract (English)
These days, Sri Lanka is facing a severe dengue outbreak. In this situation, it is very useful to provide an overall understanding of Sri Lanka's historical dengue trends as well as the current dengue situation. A dashboard is an easy and effective way to convey a large amount of information to the public. Therefore, the Sri Lanka Dengue Dashboard is an initial attempt to provide comprehensive information about the dengue situation in Sri Lanka, together with the relationship between climate factors and dengue cases. Keywords: Data visualization, Heat map, Climate-dengue relationship, Tree map, Time series analysis, Quarto, Dashboard, Dengue fever
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Sri Lanka is experiencing a severe dengue outbreak, creating a need for tools that summarize historical trends and the current situation.
Design and population
The supplied evidence does not report the study design, setting, dates, or population.
Core methods
The supplied evidence does not report the specific methods, data sources, or analytical procedures used to build the dashboard.
Main results
The supplied evidence does not report any quantitative results or findings from the dashboard.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors present the Sri Lanka Dengue Dashboard as an initial attempt to deliver comprehensive dengue information and explore climate‐dengue relationships.
Scientific and public-health significance
Providing an easy‐to‐use visual tool may aid public awareness and support decision‐making for dengue surveillance in Sri Lanka.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report study limitations or an assessment of evidence strength.

TỔN THƯƠNG GAN Ở BỆNH NHI SỐC SỐT XUẤT HUYẾT DENGUE TẠI BỆNH VIỆN NHI ĐỒNG 1

TỔN THƯƠNG GAN Ở BỆNH NHI SỐC SỐT XUẤT HUYẾT DENGUE TẠI BỆNH VIỆN NHI ĐỒNG 1
作者： Phước Truyền Lê, Tấn Phương Đinh, Nguyễn Thế Nguyên Phùng
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 (SXHD) 与登革热 (SXHD) 相关联。Tuy nhiên,giá trị tiên lượng của diễn tiến động học tổn thương gan trong giai đoạn sốc ở trẻ sốc SXHD vẫn chưa được làm rõ。
设计与对象
Nghiên cứu theo dõi dọc tiến cứu được thực hiện tại Khoa Cấp cứu và Hồi sức Tích cực – Chống độc, Bệnh viện Nhi Đồng 1 từ 01/01/2021 đến 31/12/2023. Trẻ từ 1 tháng đến 15 tuổi được chẩn đoán sốc SXHD có xét nghiệm dengue dương tính được thu nhận.
核心方法
Tổn thương gan được theo dõi trong quá trình điều trị kể từ thời điểm vào sốc。重要的是,ROC 的物流是从物流开始的。
主要结果
Nghiên cứu được thực hiện ở 113 bệnh nhi; tuổi trung vị 8,9 (6,4–12,1) tuổi; nam chiếm 61%. Tỷ lệ tử vong là 15%. Tại thời điểm vào sốc, AST trung vị 332 IU/L; 24,8% có AST >1000 IU/L. AST ở nhóm tử vong cao hơn có ý nghĩa so với nhóm sống tại tất cả các thời điểm (p < 0,001). AST >1000 IU/L là các yếu tố tiên lượng độc lập tử vong.
解释与创新
首个48小时AST动态变化对登革热休克综合征患儿死亡具有显著预后价值,有助于结合临床因素改善风险分层。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为登革热休克综合征患儿死亡风险监测与评估提供支持,从而改善干预措施与医疗护理。
局限与证据强度
研究可能受限于样本量与纵向观察性质,但结果由统计分析支持且预后价值可靠。

TỔN THƯƠNG GAN Ở BỆNH NHI SỐC SỐT XUẤT HUYẾT DENGUE TẠI BỆNH VIỆN NHI ĐỒNG 1

Authors: Phước Truyền Lê, Tấn Phương Đinh, Nguyễn Thế Nguyên Phùng
Original abstract (English)
Đặt vấn đề: Tổn thương gan thường gặp ở sốc sốt xuất huyết dengue (SXHD) và có thể liên quan đến bệnh nặng cũng như tử vong. Tuy nhiên, giá trị tiên lượng của diễn tiến động học tổn thương gan trong giai đoạn sốc ở trẻ sốc SXHD vẫn chưa được làm rõ. Phương pháp: Nghiên cứu theo dõi dọc tiến cứu thực hiện tại Khoa Cấp cứu và Hồi sức Tích cực – Chống độc, Bệnh viện Nhi Đồng 1 từ 01/01/2021 đến 31/12/2023. Trẻ từ 1 tháng đến 15 tuổi được chẩn đoán sốc SXHD có xét nghiệm dengue dương tính được thu nhận. Tổn thương gan được theo dõi trong quá trình điều trị kể từ thời điểm vào sốc. Phân tích đường cong ROC và hồi quy logistic đa biến được sử dụng để đánh giá giá trị tiên lượng tử vong. Kết quả: Nghiên cứu được thực hiện ở113 bệnh nhi; tuổi trung vị 8,9 (6,4–12,1) tuổi; nam chiếm 61%. Tỷ lệ tử vong là 15%. Tại thời điểm vào sốc, AST trung vị 332 IU/L; 24,8% có AST >1000 IU/L. AST ở nhóm tử vong cao hơn có ý nghĩa so với nhóm sống tại tất cả các thời điểm (p 1000 IU/L là các yếu tố tiên lượng độc lập tử vong. Kết luận: Động học AST trong 48 giờ đầu có giá trị tiên lượng tử vong rõ rệt ở trẻ em sốc SXHD, góp phần cải thiện phân tầng nguy cơ khi kết hợp với các yếu tố lâm sàng.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Tổn thương gan thường gặp ở sốc sốt xuất huyết dengue (SXHD) và có thể liên quan đến bệnh nặng cũng như tử vong. Tuy nhiên, giá trị tiên lượng của diễn tiến động học tổn thương gan trong giai đoạn sốc ở trẻ sốc SXHD vẫn chưa được làm rõ.
Design and population
Nghiên cứu theo dõi dọc tiến cứu được thực hiện tại Khoa Cấp cứu và Hồi sức Tích cực – Chống độc, Bệnh viện Nhi Đồng 1 từ 01/01/2021 đến 31/12/2023. Trẻ từ 1 tháng đến 15 tuổi được chẩn đoán sốc SXHD có xét nghiệm dengue dương tính được thu nhận.
Core methods
Tổn thương gan được theo dõi trong quá trình điều trị kể từ thời điểm vào sốc. Phân tích đường cong ROC và hồi quy logistic đa biến được sử dụng để đánh giá giá trị tiên lượng tử vong.
Main results
Nghiên cứu được thực hiện ở 113 bệnh nhi; tuổi trung vị 8,9 (6,4–12,1) tuổi; nam chiếm 61%. Tỷ lệ tử vong là 15%. Tại thời điểm vào sốc, AST trung vị 332 IU/L; 24,8% có AST >1000 IU/L. AST ở nhóm tử vong cao hơn có ý nghĩa so với nhóm sống tại tất cả các thời điểm (p < 0,001). AST >1000 IU/L là các yếu tố tiên lượng độc lập tử vong.
Interpretation and novelty
Động học AST trong 48 giờ đầu có giá trị tiên lượng tử vong rõ rệt ở trẻ em sốc SXHD, góp phần cải thiện phân tầng nguy cơ khi kết hợp với các yếu tố lâm sàng.
Scientific and public-health significance
Kết quả nghiên cứu có thể hỗ trợ trong việc theo dõi và đánh giá nguy cơ tử vong ở trẻ em bị sốc SXHD, từ đó cải thiện các biện pháp can thiệp và chăm sóc y tế.
Limitations and evidence strength
Nghiên cứu có thể bị giới hạn bởi quy mô mẫu và tính chất theo dõi dọc, nhưng các kết quả được hỗ trợ bởi phân tích thống kê và có giá trị tiên lượng đáng tin cậy.

老年登革热住院患者的临床与实验室特征

ĐẶC ĐIỂM LÂM SÀNG VÀ CẬN LÂM SÀNG CỦA SỐT XUẤT HUYẾT DENGUE Ở NGƯỜI CAO TUỔI ĐIỀU TRỊ NỘI TRÚ
作者： Thị Bích Đặng, Thị Hoàn Trần, Xuân Hưng Lê, Kim Thư Nguyễn
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了老年住院患者与年轻人相比登革热的临床和亚临床特征,旨在确定年龄特异性的表现和结果差异。
设计与对象
越南国立热带病医院于 2024 年至 2025 年进行了一项前瞻性观察研究,纳入了 444 名住院登革热患者,分为两个年龄组:60 岁以下 (n=217) 和 60 岁以上 (n=227)。
核心方法
记录临床和实验室变量,包括一般特征、表现症状、血小板计数、天冬氨酸转氨酶 (AST) 水平和 治疗 干预措施,以便在年龄组之间进行比较。
主要结果
老年患者的高血压(38.8% vs. 19.4%)和糖尿病(21.1% vs. 10.6%)发生率显着较高,高热≥39°C的频率较低(4.4% vs. 19.4%),入院时血小板计数显着较低(30.0 vs. 92.0 G/L)和最低点(15.0 vs. 15.0 G/L)。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,老年登革热患者的初始症状不太典型,但表现出更严重的血小板减少和更高的转氨酶升高,因此需要更密切的实验室监测来评估疾病的严重程度。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要针对特定​​年龄的登革热管理策略,特别是加强对老年患者的血小板计数和肝酶的监测,以预防严重并发症。
局限与证据强度
单中心设计和缺乏随机性限制了普遍性,并且缺乏病毒学确认或血清型数据限制了因果推断;总体证据强度中等。

ĐẶC ĐIỂM LÂM SÀNG VÀ CẬN LÂM SÀNG CỦA SỐT XUẤT HUYẾT DENGUE Ở NGƯỜI CAO TUỔI ĐIỀU TRỊ NỘI TRÚ

Authors: Thị Bích Đặng, Thị Hoàn Trần, Xuân Hưng Lê, Kim Thư Nguyễn
Original abstract (English)
Mục tiêu: Mô tả đặc điểm lâm sàng, cận lâm sàng của bệnh nhân sốt xuất huyết Dengue (Dengue) ở người cao tuổi và so sánh với nhóm dưới 60 tuổi. Đối tượng và phương pháp: Nghiên cứu quan sát tiến cứu trên 444 bệnh nhân Dengue điều trị nội trú tại Bệnh viện Bệnh Nhiệt đới Trung ương giai đoạn 2024-2025. Người bệnh được chia thành hai nhóm tuổi < 60 (n=217) và ≥ 60 (n=227). Các biến số gồm đặc điểm chung, triệu chứng lâm sàng khi nhập viện, xét nghiệm (tiểu cầu, AST) và can thiệp điều trị. So sánh giữa hai nhóm với p<0,05. Kết quả: Người cao tuổi có tỷ lệ tăng huyết áp (38,8%) và đái tháo đường (21,1%) cao hơn rõ rệt. Tỷ lệ sốt cao ≥39°C thấp hơn ở nhóm ≥60 tuổi (4,4% so với 19,4%). Số lượng tiểu cầu thấp hơn tại nhập viện (30,0 so với 92,0 G/L) và nadir thấp hơn (15,0 so với 42,0 G/L; p<0,001). Tỷ lệ tăng men gan, truyền tiểu cầu và sử dụng kháng sinh cao hơn ở nhóm người cao tuổi. Kết luận: Người cao tuổi mắc Dengue có biểu hiện lâm sàng ban đầu ít điển hình nhưng giảm tiểu cầu sâu và tăng men gan rõ rệt hơn, cần theo dõi sát xét nghiệm để đánh giá mức độ bệnh.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the clinical and subclinical characteristics of dengue in elderly inpatients compared with younger adults, aiming to identify age-specific differences in presentation and outcomes.
Design and population
A prospective observational study was conducted at the National Hospital for Tropical Diseases in Vietnam from 2024 to 2025, enrolling 444 hospitalized dengue patients divided into two age groups: under 60 years (n=217) and 60 years or older (n=227).
Core methods
Clinical and laboratory variables including general characteristics, presenting symptoms, platelet counts, aspartate aminotransferase (AST) levels, and treatment interventions were recorded for comparison between age groups.
Main results
Elderly patients had significantly higher rates of hypertension (38.8% vs. 19.4%) and diabetes (21.1% vs. 10.6%), lower frequency of high fever ≥39°C (4.4% vs. 19.4%), and markedly lower platelet counts at admission (30.0 vs. 92.0 G/L) and nadir (15.0 vs.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that elderly dengue patients present with less typical initial symptoms but exhibit deeper thrombocytopenia and higher transaminase elevations, necessitating closer laboratory monitoring to assess disease severity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for age-specific dengue management strategies, particularly intensified monitoring of platelet counts and liver enzymes in elderly patients to prevent severe complications.
Limitations and evidence strength
The single-center design and lack of randomization limit generalizability, and the absence of virological confirmation or serotype data constrains causal inference; overall evidence strength is moderate.

2026年西冷巴西公共卫生中心辖区登革热患者家庭环境卫生状况调查

KONDISI SANITASI LINGKUNGAN RUMAH PENDERITA DEMAM BERDARAH DENGUE DI WILAYAH KERJA PUSKESMAS KEBUN TEBU LAMPUNG BARAT TAHUN 2026
作者： Meisya Rahma Syifa, Dina Dwi Nuryani, Fitri Ekasari
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Demam Berdarah 登革热 (DBD) 仍然是印度尼西亚的一个公共卫生问题,其中包括西楠榜县,该地区的环境卫生条件疑似影响其 发病率。
设计与对象
本研究于 2026 年在西楠榜省 Puskesmas Kebun Tebu 服务区采用定量分析横断面设计,纳入了通过有目的抽样选出的 79 名受访者。
核心方法
使用结构化问卷收集数据,并以频率分布形式进行单变量分析,并使用显着性水平为 95% 的卡方检验进行双变量分析。
主要结果
单变量分析显示,25名受访者(31.6%)罹患登革热,18名(22.8%)的储水条件可能成为蚊虫孳生地,29名(36.7%)的住宅通风不良,36名(45.6%)的垃圾管理不善。双变量分析显示,储水条件与登革热发生存在显著关联(p=0.000;OR=25.5),住宅通风与登革热发生存在显著关联(p=0.008;OR=4.286),垃圾管理与登革热发生存在显著关联(p=0.003;OR=5.143)。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明不良的环境卫生因素与研究区域的 DBD 发病率 显着相关,强调了有针对性的干预措施的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,改善家庭储水、通风和废物管理可以降低 DBD 风险,为当地健康促进和环境 监测 战略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
横截面设计限制了因果推断,有目的的抽样可能会引入选择偏差,降低普遍性;证据强度中等。

KONDISI SANITASI LINGKUNGAN RUMAH PENDERITA DEMAM BERDARAH DENGUE DI WILAYAH KERJA PUSKESMAS KEBUN TEBU LAMPUNG BARAT TAHUN 2026

Authors: Meisya Rahma Syifa, Dina Dwi Nuryani, Fitri Ekasari
Original abstract (English)
Demam Berdarah Dengue (DBD) masih menjadi masalah kesehatan masyarakat di Indonesia, termasuk di Kabupaten Lampung Barat. Kondisi sanitasi lingkungan rumah, khususnya penampungan air, ventilasi rumah, dan pengelolaan sampah diduga berhubungan dengan kejadian DBD. Penelitian ini bertujuan untuk mengetahui hubungan faktor-faktor tersebut dengan kejadian DBD di wilayah kerja Puskesmas Kebun Tebu Lampung Barat tahun 2026. Penelitian ini menggunakan desain kuantitatif analitik dengan pendekatan cross sectional. Sampel penelitian sebanyak 79 responden diperoleh melalui teknik purposive sampling. Data dikumpulkan menggunakan kuesioner terstruktur, kemudian dianalisis secara univariat dalam bentuk distribusi frekuensi, serta bivariat menggunakan uji chi- square dengan tingkat signifikansi 95%. Hasil analisis univariat menunjukkan sebanyak 25 responden (31,6%) mengalami DBD, 18 responden (22,8%) memiliki kondisi penampungan air berpotensi menjadi tempat perindukan nyamuk, 29 responden (36,7%) memiliki ventilasi rumah tidak baik, dan 36 responden (45,6%) memiliki pengelolaan sampah yang tidak baik. Analisis bivariat menunjukkan ada hubungan signifikan kondisi penampungan air dengan kejadian DBD (p=0,000; OR=25,5), kondisi ventilasi rumah dengan kejadian DBD (p=0,008; OR=4,286), dan pengelolaan sampah dengan kejadian DBD (p=0,003; OR=5,143). Disarankan Puskesmas meningkatkan promosi kesehatan serta pengawasan lingkungan, masyarakat diharapkan aktif melakukan 3M Plus (menguras, menutup, mengubur) dan menjaga kebersihan lingkungan, sementara institusi pendidikan dan peneliti selanjutnya perlu memperluas penelitian terkait faktor lain yang mempengaruhi kejadian DBD.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Demam Berdarah Dengue (DBD) remains a public health problem in Indonesia, including West Lampung Regency, where environmental sanitation conditions are suspected to influence its incidence.
Design and population
This study used a quantitative analytic cross-sectional design in the service area of Puskesmas Kebun Tebu, West Lampung, in 2026, enrolling 79 respondents selected by purposive sampling.
Core methods
Data were collected using a structured questionnaire and analyzed univariately as frequency distributions and bivariately using the chi-square test with a 95% significance level.
Main results
Univariate analysis found 25 respondents (31.6%) had DBD, 18 (22.8%) had water storage conditions conducive to mosquito breeding, 29 (36.7%) had poor ventilation, and 36 (45.6%) had poor waste management. Bivariate analysis showed significant associations between water storage conditions and DBD (p=0.000; OR=25.5), ventilation and DBD (p=0.008; OR=4.286), and waste management and DBD (p=0.003; OR=5.143).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these findings as evidence that poor environmental sanitation factors are significantly associated with DBD incidence in the study area, highlighting the need for targeted interventions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that improving household water storage, ventilation, and waste management could reduce DBD risk, informing local health promotion and environmental surveillance strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The cross-sectional design limits causal inference, and purposive sampling may introduce selection bias, reducing generalizability; the evidence strength is moderate.

社交广告中关于 登革热 的说服:系统功能语言方法

PERSUASION IN SOCIAL ADVERTISEMENT ABOUT DENGUE FEVER: A SYSTEMIC-FUNCTIONAL LINGUISTIC APPROACH
作者： Denise Maia Pereira, Fabíola de Jesus Soares Santana, José Haroldo Bandeira Sousa
摘要中文翻译与社会宣传有关的工作,是我们共同讨论的证据,主要是为了说明社会问题,并要求使用各种符号来重现全会的理解。我们正在开展公共调查,以说服政府开展公众宣传活动,以预防登革热和社会问题。在此基础上,我们对图像进行了研究,并利用设计视觉语法和语言系统功能来解释意义的构建过程。社会实践中的共同分析、结果论坛:元功能术语、理念的术语、过程材料的过渡过程;利用语料库进行说服和说服论坛。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查政府如何在社交网络上发布有关登革热的社交广告中进行说服。这项研究的基础是需要了解公共卫生信息传递中使用的语言策略,以应对日益增长的登革热问题,以及有效沟通在疾病预防中的重要性。
设计与对象
该研究是在社交网络环境中进行的,分析了三个广告的数据。它重点关注针对 登革热 的政府公共卫生运动中使用的说服技巧,重点是信息的语言和视觉元素。
核心方法
该研究采用了系统功能语言学方法,研究了图像和词语在有说服力的意义建构中的相互作用。它利用视觉设计语法和功能系统语言学来分析从广告中收集的数据。
主要结果
对这三个广告的分析表明,观念元功能中最常用的传递过程是物质过程。通过诱惑进行说服和通过信念进行说服被认为是语料库中采用的策略。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为在公共卫生传播中细致入微地使用说服策略的证据,强调了语言和视觉元素在塑造公众对登革热的理解方面的重要性。这种方法为社交广告在疾病预防方面的有效性提供了一个新的视角。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为制定更有效的登革热预防公共卫生传播策略提供信息,强调说服性语言和视觉设计在吸引公众和提高健康意识方面的作用。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其仅抽象的性质和三个广告的样本量较小而受到限制,这可能无法完全代表社交媒体活动中使用的说服策略的多样性。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

PERSUASION IN SOCIAL ADVERTISEMENT ABOUT DENGUE FEVER: A SYSTEMIC-FUNCTIONAL LINGUISTIC APPROACH

Authors: Denise Maia Pereira, Fabíola de Jesus Soares Santana, José Haroldo Bandeira Sousa
Original abstract (English)
Este trabalho relata aspectos de propagandas sociais, que são parte de um rico gênero discursivo evidenciado pela sua exposição cada vez mais comum em redes sociais e requer o uso de uma variedade semiótica de recursos para que sejam plenamente compreendidos. O estudo buscou investigar como a persuasão é empregada na publicidade governamental sobre a dengue em uma rede social. Para alcançar esse objetivo, este estudo investiga uma avaliação entre imagens e palavras no processo persuasivo de construção de significado utilizando a Gramática do Design Visual e a Linguística sistêmico Funcional. Através da análise de dados coletados de três anúncios em redes sociais, os resultados foram: em termos de metafunção ideacional, o processo transitivo mais utilizado foi o processo material; a persuasão por sedução e a persuasão por convicção foram utilizadas nos corpora.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate how persuasion is employed in government social advertisements about dengue on a social network. The research is grounded in the need to understand the linguistic strategies used in public health messaging to address the growing prevalence of dengue and the importance of effective communication in disease prevention.
Design and population
The study was conducted in a social network setting, analyzing data from three advertisements. It focused on the persuasive techniques used in government public health campaigns targeting dengue fever, with an emphasis on the linguistic and visual elements of the messages.
Core methods
The study employed a systemic-functional linguistic approach, examining the interplay between images and words in the persuasive construction of meaning. It utilized the Grammar of Visual Design and Functional Systemic Linguistics to analyze the data collected from the advertisements.
Main results
The analysis of the three advertisements revealed that the most frequently used transitive process in the ideational metafunction was the material process. Persuasion through seduction and persuasion through conviction were identified as strategies employed in the corpora.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as evidence of the nuanced use of persuasive strategies in public health communication, highlighting the importance of both linguistic and visual elements in shaping public understanding of dengue. This approach offers a novel perspective on the effectiveness of social advertisements in disease prevention.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more effective public health communication strategies for dengue prevention, emphasizing the role of persuasive language and visual design in engaging the public and promoting health awareness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its abstract-only nature and the small sample size of three advertisements, which may not fully represent the diversity of persuasive strategies used in social media campaigns. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these constraints.

登革热 患者中性粒细胞淋巴细胞比值 (NLR) 与 NS1 抗原(非结构蛋白 1)作为 免疫应答 的关系分析

Analysis of the Relationship between Neutrophil-Lymphocyte Rasio (NLR) and NS1 Antigen (Non-Structural Protein 1) as an Immune Response in Dengue Fever Patients
作者： Kinanti Defin Nurani, Syahrul Ardiansyah

查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热出血热 (DHF) 仍然是印度尼西亚的一个重大公共卫生问题,需要确定反映登革热感染早期 免疫应答 的可用生物标志物。该研究旨在探讨中性粒细胞-淋巴细胞比率 (NLR) 与 NS1 抗原水平之间的关系,作为 DHF 患者 免疫应答 的潜在指标。
设计与对象
本研究采用横断面观察设计,使用 2026 年 4 月至 6 月泗水哈吉地区综合医院 (RSUD) 60 名 DHF 患者的医疗记录。
核心方法
Spearman相关分析用于评估NLR值与NS1抗原水平之间的关系,并使用p值评估统计显着性。
主要结果
NLR 值和 NS1 抗原水平之间观察到统计学上显着的弱正相关(r = 0.287;p = 0.026)。
解释与创新
作者认为,NLR 可以作为评估 DHF 患者 免疫应答 的支持性参数,尽管它应该与其他临床参数一起解释。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,NLR 可能有助于登革热患者的早期风险分层或 免疫应答 监测,支持临床决策。
局限与证据强度
横断面设计和小样本量限制了因果推断和普遍性,并且对医疗记录的依赖可能会引入选择或信息偏差,需要谨慎解释。

Analysis of the Relationship between Neutrophil-Lymphocyte Rasio (NLR) and NS1 Antigen (Non-Structural Protein 1) as an Immune Response in Dengue Fever Patients

Authors: Kinanti Defin Nurani, Syahrul Ardiansyah
Original abstract (English)
This study employed a cross-sectional observational design, utilizing medical records from 60 DHF patients at Haji Regional General Hospital (RSUD) Surabaya during the period of April–June 2026. These findings suggest the potential utility of NLR as a supportive parameter for assessing immune response, although its interpretation should be combined with other clinical parameters. Analysis of the Relationship between Neutrophil-Lymphocyte Rasio (NLR) and NS1 Antigen (Non-Structural Protein 1) as an Immune Response in Dengue Fever Patients Analisis Hubungan Neutrofil-Limfosit Rasio (NLR) dengan Antigen NS1 (Protein Non-Struktural 1) sebagai Respons Imun pada Pasien Demam Berdarah Dengue DOI: https://doi.org/10.21070/ups.11494 Keywords: Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever;, Neutrophil Lymphocyte Rasio (NLR);, Immune Response. Abstract Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever (DHF) remains a significant public health issue in Indonesia. The Non-Structural Protein 1 (NS1) antigen is a biomarker indicative of early-stage dengue infection that can be detected in circulation prior to the formation of specific antibodies, whereas the Neutrophil-Lymphocyte Ratio (NLR) reflects the balance between inflammatory and immune responses to viral infection. Spearman correlation analysis revealed a statistically significant relationship between NLR values and NS1 antigen levels (r = 0.287; p = 0.026), characterized by a weak, positive correlation. Downloads References T. Alaf Athallah, “Pelatihan Pembuatan Ovitrap Nyamuk Aedes sp . dan Atraktan Fermentasi sebagai Upaya Pengendalian Demam Berdarah Dengue ( DBD ),” Madaniya, vol. 797–803, 2024, doi: https://doi.org/10.53696/27214834.828. Shafwan, Nirwana, and Deni, “Analisis Faktor Risiko Penyebaran Demam Berdarah Dengue (DBD) di Wilayah Kerja Puskesmas Lalowaru Tah...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever (DHF) remains a significant public health issue in Indonesia, and there is a need to identify accessible biomarkers that reflect early immune responses to dengue infection. The study aimed to examine the relationship between the Neutrophil-Lymphocyte Ratio (NLR) and NS1 antigen levels as potential indicators of immune response in DHF patients.
Design and population
This study employed a cross-sectional observational design using medical records from 60 DHF patients at Haji Regional General Hospital (RSUD) Surabaya between April and June 2026.
Core methods
Spearman correlation analysis was used to assess the relationship between NLR values and NS1 antigen levels, with statistical significance evaluated using p-values.
Main results
A statistically significant weak positive correlation was observed between NLR values and NS1 antigen levels (r = 0.287; p = 0.026).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors suggest that NLR may serve as a supportive parameter for assessing immune response in DHF patients, though it should be interpreted alongside other clinical parameters.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings indicate that NLR could potentially aid in early risk stratification or monitoring of immune response in dengue patients, supporting clinical decision-making.
Limitations and evidence strength
The cross-sectional design and small sample size limit causal inference and generalizability, and reliance on medical records may introduce selection or information bias, warranting cautious interpretation.
