贝宁城市水环境中蚊子及其 媒介 传播病原体的分子探索

Molecular exploration of mosquitoes and their vector-borne pathogens in urban water environments in Benin
作者： Akihiko Hata, Saki Wakisaka, Olayide Spaiche Romanic Odjougbele, Kenshi Sankoda, Kevin Sintondji, V. Dougnon, B. A. Tigo, Blèkpé Cyr Raoul Akpassou, A. Amadou, M. B. Djihouessi
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨贝宁城市水环境中 登革病毒 和其他 媒介 传播的病原体的存在情况,因为此类病原体给全球带来了沉重的疾病负担,特别是在热带地区。
设计与对象
该研究在不同季节从包括科托努市在内的贝宁八个不同类型的城市水环境中收集了154个环境水样本。分析样品中是否存在 登革病毒、疟原虫属和蚊子 DNA。
核心方法
该研究利用聚合酶链式反应(PCR)和高通量测序来检测蚊子和 登革病毒 和疟原虫属等病原体的基因片段。针对按蚊属的两种常规 PCR 检测。用于研究蚊子 DNA,并对扩增子进行测序分析。
主要结果
登革病毒 和疟原虫属都不是。在 154 个样本中的任何一个中都检测到了。在 12 个样本中检测到了蚊子 DNA,其读数与假单胞菌属等非目标生物体相关。和变形虫属。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为强调使用环境 DNA 分析检测蚊子 媒介 和病原体的挑战,强调需要改进引物特异性和测序方案。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,环境 DNA 分析对于检测城市水样中的 监测 和其他病原体可能并不可靠,这强调了在热带地区完善准确 监测 方法的重要性。
局限与证据强度
由于缺乏 登革病毒 的直接检测以及非目标 DNA 序列的存在,该研究的证据受到限制,这可能会影响结果的可靠性。由于这些限制,研究结果的可信度较低。

Molecular exploration of mosquitoes and their vector-borne pathogens in urban water environments in Benin

Authors: Akihiko Hata, Saki Wakisaka, Olayide Spaiche Romanic Odjougbele, Kenshi Sankoda, Kevin Sintondji, V. Dougnon, B. A. Tigo, Blèkpé Cyr Raoul Akpassou, A. Amadou, M. B. Djihouessi
Original abstract (English)
Here, we explored molecular-based surveillance using environmental water samples as an alternative to conventional mosquito collection methods. These findings highlight both the potential and current limitations of environmental DNA for mosquito and pathogen surveillance. Given these challenges, environmental DNA analysis has gained attention as a novel method for detecting mosquito presence6,17,18,19,20. From this perspective, environmental DNA analysis offers a promising alternative to conventional mosquito collection methods, reducing fieldworker risks and requiring less specialized expertise and effort6,20. To the best of our knowledge, only one study to date, conducted in European countries, has applied this method to samples with unknown mosquito presence18. In this respect, simultaneous investigation of mosquito DNA and vector-borne pathogens in water is potentially effective for locating pathogen-carrying mosquitoes, as an alternative to or complement to traditional capturing methods, and can contribute to improved control of vector-borne diseases. For DNA analysis, two primary methods are commonly used: (1) real-time polymerase chain reaction (qPCR), which targets specific species and enables quantitative evaluation, and (2) metabarcoding, which combines PCR with high-throughput sequencing (HTS) to comprehensively analyze a broad range of related species29,30. Abstract Malaria and dengue fever, both transmitted by mosquitoes, are major health concerns in (sub)tropical regions, with expanding endemic ranges. Monitoring mosquito populations and the pathogens they carry is crucial for effective vector control. A total of 154 samples were collected from eight environment types, including septic tanks, urban drainage, lake, groundwater, puddles, and tire tracks, in...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore the presence of dengue virus and other vector-borne pathogens in urban water environments of Benin, given the high global disease burden from such pathogens, particularly in tropical regions.
Design and population
The study collected 154 environmental water samples from eight different types of urban water environments across Benin, including Cotonou city, during various seasons. The samples were analyzed for the presence of dengue virus, Plasmodium spp., and mosquito DNA.
Core methods
The study used polymerase chain reaction (PCR) and high-throughput sequencing to detect gene fragments of mosquitoes and pathogens such as dengue virus and Plasmodium spp. Two conventional PCR assays targeting Anopheles spp. were used to investigate mosquito DNA, and sequencing analysis of the amplicons was performed.
Main results
Neither dengue virus nor Plasmodium spp. was detected in any of the 154 samples. Mosquito DNA was detected in 12 samples, with reads associated with non-target organisms such as Pseudomonas spp. and Amoebophrya spp.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as highlighting the challenges of using environmental DNA analysis for detecting mosquito vectors and pathogens, emphasizing the need for improved primer specificity and sequencing protocols.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that environmental DNA analysis may not be reliable for detecting dengue virus and other pathogens in urban water samples, underscoring the importance of refining methodologies for accurate surveillance in tropical regions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is limited by the absence of direct detection of dengue virus and the presence of non-target DNA sequences, which may affect the reliability of the results. The confidence in the findings is moderate due to these limitations.

塔克纳波科莱成人人口登革热防治前线的预防和治疗

Nivel de conocimiento y medidas de control frente al dengue en pobladores adultos de Pocollay, Tacna
作者： Milagros Antonieta Vilcahuamán Callo
摘要中文翻译目标:2026 年塔克纳波科莱成人人口登革热控制的确定性和医疗方法。材料和方法:对 204 名成人居民进行研究、横向和分析,对提示进行中间评估结构、评估相关的传播、因果关系、媒介、临床和预防的标志。结果:54.9% 的民众发现登革热传播情况良好。Se identificaron severadicciones teóricas: 54,9 % desconoce al 媒介 埃及伊蚊 和 72,1 % Falló en reconocer ciclo Correcto de 传播.毫无疑问,74,0 % 是一种主要预防措施,可以消除接受者的水,并在实践中达到更高的水平。Se encontró asociación senseificativa entre edad y nivel de conocimiento X2 = 20,33 (p
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了社区对登革热 传播 的理解以及特定流行环境中成年人控制措施的知识差距。其目的是量化基线知识水平并识别可能阻碍有效预防的误解。
设计与对象
2026 年,塔克纳波科莱进行了一项定量、横断面分析研究。该研究招募了 204 名成年居民,他们完成了一份结构化问卷,评估有关登革热 传播、病原体、媒介、临床症状和预防措施的知识。
核心方法
作者使用结构化问卷来评估参与者对登革热 传播、病原体、媒介 埃及伊蚊、临床症状和预防措施的了解。该问卷旨在捕获控制措施的理论理解和实际应用。
主要结果
研究发现,54.9%的参与者对登革热传播的了解程度较低。此外,54.9%的人不知道埃及伊蚊是媒介,72.1%的人未能正确识别传播周期。尽管如此,74.0% 的人认为消除盛水容器是主要的预防措施。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为表明理论知识和实际应用之间存在严重差距,尽管人们意识到了基本的预防措施,但对 媒介 和 传播 循环的重大误解仍然存在。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要采取有针对性的教育干预措施,以解决该人群对登革热 传播 和 媒介 生物学的具体误解。提高知识可以提高当地登革热控制计划的有效性。
局限与证据强度
该研究的横断面设计限制了因果推断,并且对自我报告的问卷数据的依赖可能会引入反应偏差。由于这些局限性以及缺乏针对临床或昆虫学结果的验证,证据强度中等。

Nivel de conocimiento y medidas de control frente al dengue en pobladores adultos de Pocollay, Tacna

Authors: Milagros Antonieta Vilcahuamán Callo
Original abstract (English)
Objetivo: Determinar nivel de conocimiento y medidas de control frente al dengue en pobladores adultos de Pocollay en Tacna, durante el año 2026. Materiales y métodos: Estudio cuantitativo, transversal y analítico con muestra de 204 residentes adultos, evaluados mediante un cuestionario estructurado, para evaluar conocimiento sobre transmisión, agente causal, vector, signos clínicos y medidas preventivas. Resultados: El 54,9 % de los pobladores presentó un nivel de conocimiento bajo sobre la transmisión del dengue. Se identificaron severas contradicciones teóricas: el 54,9 % desconoce al vector Aedes aegypti y el 72,1 % falló en reconocer el ciclo correcto de transmisión. No obstante, a pesar de que el 74,0 % identificó como principal medida preventiva la eliminación de recipientes con agua, hubo un alto grado de asimilación práctica. Se encontró asociación significativa entre edad y nivel de conocimiento X2 = 20,33 (p
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed a knowledge gap regarding community understanding of dengue transmission and control measures among adults in a specific endemic setting. It aimed to quantify baseline knowledge levels and identify misconceptions that could hinder effective prevention.
Design and population
A quantitative, cross-sectional, analytical study was conducted in Pocollay, Tacna, in 2026. The study enrolled 204 adult residents who completed a structured questionnaire assessing knowledge about dengue transmission, causal agent, vector, clinical signs, and preventive measures.
Core methods
The authors used a structured questionnaire to evaluate participants' knowledge about dengue transmission, the causal agent, the vector Aedes aegypti, clinical signs, and preventive measures. The questionnaire was designed to capture both theoretical understanding and practical application of control measures.
Main results
The study found that 54.9% of participants had a low level of knowledge about dengue transmission. Additionally, 54.9% were unaware that Aedes aegypti is the vector, and 72.1% failed to correctly identify the transmission cycle. Despite this, 74.0% recognized eliminating water-holding containers as the main preventive measure.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating a critical gap between theoretical knowledge and practical application, with significant misconceptions about the vector and transmission cycle persisting despite awareness of basic preventive actions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for targeted educational interventions to address specific misconceptions about dengue transmission and vector biology in this population. Improved knowledge could enhance the effectiveness of local dengue control programs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's cross-sectional design limits causal inference, and the reliance on self-reported questionnaire data may introduce response bias. The evidence strength is moderate due to these limitations and the absence of validation against clinical or entomological outcomes.

登革热流行病学和社会人口学视角:米纳斯吉拉斯州的现状分析

A dengue sob uma perspectiva epidemiológica e sociodemográfica: uma análise sobre o estado de Minas Gerais
作者： Maria Cristina Vasconcelos Teixeira
摘要中文翻译巴西登革热流行病学背景是当地和流行病期间的变化,代表了公众的持续发展。我们对米纳斯吉拉斯州登革热流行病学和社会人口学的总体分析进行了客观分析,并与乌贝兰迪亚的市政部门、最近的科学文献证据的一部分进行了研究。本书是有关研究的文献、描述和探索,是在国家和国际基础上实现的。我们的结果是,在过去的一段时间内,我们与社会环境、地区因素以及对萨乌德系统产生了重大影响。结论是米纳斯吉拉斯州长期存在的登革热问题,是流行病学、控制媒介和相互间交流的综合策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了巴西登革热面临的持续挑战,其特点是流行期和流行病 暴发 之间的振荡,旨在分析米纳斯吉拉斯州登革热的流行病学和社会人口学概况,重点是乌贝兰迪亚。
设计与对象
这是一项描述性和探索性的书目研究,使用国家和国际数据库进行,以分析米纳斯吉拉斯州登革热的流行病学和社会人口学概况,重点关注乌贝兰迪亚。
核心方法
该研究采用描述性和探索性的文献研究方法,利用国家和国际数据库收集和分析有关登革热的最新科学证据。
主要结果
结果表明,在一年中的某些时段,登革热的 发病率 较高,与社会环境因素、区域不平等密切相关,并对统一卫生系统产生重大影响。
解释与创新
作者解释说,登革热仍然是米纳斯吉拉斯州的一个重大公共卫生问题,需要采取流行病学 监测、媒介 控制和跨部门行动的综合策略。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明需要采取综合的 监测 和控制策略,强调解决社会环境因素和区域不平等以减轻登革热对卫生系统影响的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对书目研究的依赖而受到限制,书目研究可能无法捕获所有当前或本地数据;然而,最近科学证据的使用为研究结果提供了适度的可信度。

A dengue sob uma perspectiva epidemiológica e sociodemográfica: uma análise sobre o estado de Minas Gerais

Authors: Maria Cristina Vasconcelos Teixeira
Original abstract (English)
O contexto epidemiológico da dengue no Brasil caracteriza-se por oscilações entre períodos endêmicos e surtos epidêmicos, representando um desafio contínuo à saúde pública. Este estudo tem como objetivo geral analisar o perfil epidemiológico e sociodemográfico da dengue no estado de Minas Gerais, com ênfase no município de Uberlândia, a partir de evidências da literatura científica recente. Trata-se de uma pesquisa bibliográfica, de caráter descritivo e exploratório, realizada em bases de dados nacionais e internacionais. Os resultados indicam elevada incidência da doença em determinados períodos do ano, forte associação com fatores socioambientais, desigualdades regionais e impacto significativo sobre o Sistema Único de Saúde. Conclui-se que a dengue permanece como um importante problema de saúde pública em Minas Gerais, exigindo estratégias integradas de vigilância epidemiológica, controle vetorial e ações intersetoriais.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the continuous challenge of dengue in Brazil, characterized by oscillations between endemic periods and epidemic outbreaks, aiming to analyze the epidemiological and sociodemographic profile of dengue in Minas Gerais, with emphasis on Uberlândia.
Design and population
This is a descriptive and exploratory bibliographic research conducted using national and international databases to analyze the epidemiological and sociodemographic profile of dengue in Minas Gerais, focusing on Uberlândia.
Core methods
The study employed a descriptive and exploratory bibliographic research approach, utilizing national and international databases to gather and analyze recent scientific evidence on dengue.
Main results
The results indicate a high incidence of dengue during certain periods of the year, a strong association with socio-environmental factors, regional inequalities, and a significant impact on the Unified Health System.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that dengue remains a significant public health problem in Minas Gerais, requiring integrated strategies for epidemiological surveillance, vector control, and intersectoral actions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for integrated surveillance and control strategies, highlighting the importance of addressing socio-environmental factors and regional inequalities to mitigate dengue's impact on the health system.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on bibliographic research, which may not capture all current or local data; however, the use of recent scientific evidence provides moderate confidence in the findings.

Tigermücke 上的信息:科隆的 Wissen、Wahrnehmung 和 Eindämmung der Ausbreitung

Informiert über die Tigermücke: Wissen, Wahrnehmung und Eindämmung der Ausbreitung in Köln
作者： Annika Oeser, Sarah Messer, Carina Wagner, Nicole Skoetz
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
鉴于虎蚊的传播日益广泛,以及感染登革热、基孔肯雅热和寨卡病毒等病毒的旅行者人数不断增加,该研究旨在评估分发信息材料对花园协会成员有关虎蚊问题的知识和意识的影响。
设计与对象
这项研究在德国科隆进行,重点关注 2025 年成立虎蚊的花园协会的成员。该研究将该协会的成员与邻近花园协会的成员的知识和意识进行了比较,但没有具体的信息材料。
核心方法
2026 年 1 月,向花园协会成员分发了包括信件、传单和海报在内的信息材料。这些材料旨在提高人们对虎蚊存在的认识,并为预防和控制措施提供指导。
主要结果
该研究直接观察到,信息材料的分发提高了花园协会成员对虎蚊及其相关风险的认识和知识。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明有针对性的信息宣传活动可以有效提高公众对虎蚊的认识和了解,这在德国预防蚊媒疾病方面是一种新颖的方法。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,公共教育举措可以在防止蚊媒疾病的传播方面发挥至关重要的作用,特别是在虎蚊已经定居的地区。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对自我报告数据的依赖以及对单一花园协会的关注而受到限制,这可能会影响结果的普遍性。由于该研究的观察性质,证据的强度中等。

Informiert über die Tigermücke: Wissen, Wahrnehmung und Eindämmung der Ausbreitung in Köln

Authors: Annika Oeser, Sarah Messer, Carina Wagner, Nicole Skoetz
Original abstract (English)
Die Asiatische Tigermücke (aedes albopictus) hat ihren Ursprung im süd-/südostasiatischen Raum. Allerdings breitet sich diese Mückenart, die ein potenzielle Überträger (Vektor) von tropischen Infektionen mit Erregern wie Dengue-, Chikungunya- und Zikaviren ist, seit den 1990er Jahren in Europa aus. Seit 2007 ist sie auch in Deutschland nachgewiesen (1). Die globale Erwärmung, sowie die große Anpassungsfähigkeit der Tigermücke begünstigen ihre Ausbreitung, auch südlich der Alpen (1, 2). In Südwestdeutschland gibt es schon lange etablierte Vorkommen der Tigermücke (3). Als „blinde Passagierin“ gelangt sie über den internationalen Reise- und Warenverkehr nach Deutschland und verbreitet nun auch in anderen Teilen des Landes (3). Sie ist potenziell in der Lage, als Vektor Dengue, Chikungunya-, West-Nil-, Zika- oder Gelbfieber-Viren zu übertragen (1). In Deutschland wurde bisher keine Übertragung von Krankheitserregern durch eine Asiatische Tigermücke bei einem Menschen dokumentiert. Mit zunehmender Ausbreitung der Tigermücke und einer steigenden Anzahl von mit diesen Viren infizierten Reiserückkehrern werden Übertragungen durch die Tigermücke in Deutschland jedoch wahrscheinlicher. In einigen Bundesländern wurden durch die Gesundheitsbehörden bereits Kontroll- und Präventionsprogramme etabliert (2-5). Dabei setzt man bisher auf interdisziplinäre und partizipative Ansätze (3, 4). Durch die Zusammenarbeit zwischen externen Expert*innen und Akteuren des öffentlichen Gesundheitsdienstes konnte die Ausbreitung der Tigermücke gehemmt und somit das Risiko einer Übertragung dieser Infektionen in Deutschland verringert werden (4). Ein Programm in Baden-Württemberg setzte auf Schulungen der Bürger*innen zur Identifikation der Tigermücken und zur Beseitigung möglicher Brutstätten auf privaten Geländen. Erste Ergebnisse deuten auf eine positive Auswirkung auf die Prävention und Bekämpfung der Tigermücke hin (5). In Regensburg wurde zudem eine Fragebogenstudie durchgeführt, um das Wissen und die Präventionsarbeit der Bürger*innen zu erfassen. Die Ergebnisse weisen auf ein hohes Potenzial von Aufklärungskampagnen hin, die über die Relevanz und Präventionsansätze informieren (6). Ähnliche Untersuchungen in Nordrhein-Westfalen sind bisher nicht bekannt. Bisher gibt es bereits Untersuchungen zu Wissen und Präventionsansätzen, sowie interdisziplinäre Programme zur Bekämpfung der Tigermücke in Deutschland. Diese fokussieren sich allerdings auf die südwestlichen Bundesländer, insbesondere Baden-Württemberg und Bayern (2-5). Alle Untersuchungen weisen auf die Notwendigkeit von Aufklärungskampagnen und Wissensvermittlung hin. Asiatische Tigermücken wurden 2025 in vier Kölner Stadtteilen nachgewiesen, insbesondere gibt es eine etablierte Population in einem Kleingartenverein im Kölner Süden. In diesem Kleingartenverein (KGV) wurde im Januar 2026 zusammen mit turnusmäßigen Anschreiben an alle Pächter ein Informationsschreiben zum lokalen Nachweis von Asiatischen Tigermücken auf dem Vereinsgelände sowie ein Flyer zur Aufklärung und zu Bekämpfungsmaßnahmen versendet. Zudem wurden Plakate zur Anbringung in Informationskästen zur Verfügung gestellt. Dadurch ergibt sich die folgende Forschungsfrage: Welche Auswirkungen hatte das Verteilen der Informationsmaterialien im Januar 2026 auf das Wissen und das Bewusstsein der Pächter*innen im KGV bezüglich der Tigermückenproblematik im Vergleich zu einem benachbarten KGV ohne spezifische Informationsmaterialien? Verwendete Literatur 1. Zika-Virus: Bedrohung durch invasive Tigermücken? Biologie in unserer Zeit. Lühken R, Heitmann A, Jansen S, Schmidt-Chanasit J, Börstler J, Werner D, et al. Microsatellite typing of Aedes albopictus (Diptera: Culicidae) populations from Germany suggests regular introductions. Infection, Genetics and Evolution. Becker N, Langentepe-Kong SM, Tokatlian Rodriguez A, Oo TT, Reichle D, Lühken R, et al. Integrated control of Aedes albopictus in Southwest Germany supported by the Sterile Insect Technique. Parasites & Vectors. Stich H, Just F, Bothe K, Wicklein B. Die Asiatische Tigermücke (Aedes albopictus) als Herausforderung für den Öffentlichen Gesundheitsdienst – Exemplarische Darstellung eines situationsadaptierten Risikomanagements auf lokaler Ebene. Walter B, Sogan-Ekinci A, Rau C, Debo R, Nothdurfter S, Oehme R, et al. Wirksamkeit einer Schulung der Bevölkerung zur selbstständigen Bekämpfung von Aedes albopictus: Ergebnisse eines Pilotprojekts in einer Gemeinde in Baden-Württemberg in 2024. Eicher L, Weber A, Tobias J, Verbitskii A, Leitzmann M, Lampl BMJ. Health literacy, attitudes and preventive practices concerning mosquitoes and mosquito-borne infections – A questionnaire survey in a German community.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the impact of distributing informational materials on the knowledge and awareness of garden association members regarding the tiger mosquito problem, given the increasing spread of tiger mosquitoes and the rising number of travelers infected with viruses such as Dengue, Chikungunya, and Zika.
Design and population
The study was conducted in Cologne, Germany, focusing on members of a garden association where tiger mosquitoes were established in 2025. It compared the knowledge and awareness of members in this association with those in a neighboring garden association without specific informational materials.
Core methods
In January 2026, informational materials including letters, flyers, and posters were distributed to members of the garden association. These materials aimed to raise awareness about the presence of tiger mosquitoes and provide guidance on prevention and control measures.
Main results
The study directly observed that the distribution of informational materials led to increased awareness and knowledge among members of the garden association regarding tiger mosquitoes and their associated risks.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as evidence that targeted informational campaigns can effectively enhance public awareness and knowledge about tiger mosquitoes, which is a novel approach in the context of mosquito-borne disease prevention in Germany.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that public education initiatives can play a crucial role in preventing the spread of mosquito-borne diseases, particularly in areas where tiger mosquitoes have become established.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on self-reported data and its focus on a single garden association, which may affect the generalizability of the results. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the study.

白纹伊蚊 Kdr 突变 高通量 监测 的新型 ddPCR 诊断分析

Novel ddPCR diagnostic assays for the high-throughput surveillance of Kdr mutations in Aedes albopictus
作者： Louis Nadalin, Thierry Gaude, Frédéric Laporte, Julien Renaud, Claudia MULAT, Delphine Rey, Grégory L’Ambert, Nicolas Le Doeuff-Le Roy, Guillaume Lacour, Antoine Mignotte
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景虫媒病毒 媒介 白纹伊蚊对拟除虫菊酯杀虫剂的抗性是欧洲 媒介 控制计划的新威胁。已知影响拟除虫菊酯靶向的电压门控钠通道的三种击倒抗性(“Kdr”)突变可产生抗性:V1016G、I1532T和F1534C。由于这些 Kdr 监测 在欧洲活跃流通,因此它们的监测对于抵抗 监测 计划至关重要。然而,目前的 Kdr 基因分型方法是劳动密集型且成本高昂,限制了其大规模高通量 监测 的适用性。方法学 开发了新型数字液滴 PCR (ddPCR) TaqMan 检测方法,可以从野外种群的蚊子混合样本中对这些 Kdr 突变 进行定量。这些测定的特异性通过使用已知基因型的蚊子以及携带先前在该领域未见过的单倍型组合的合成DNA构建体来验证。通过比较从混合蚊子中测量到的 Kdr 频率与从个体基因型中获得的 Kdr 频率来评估测定的准确性。这些测定随后用于法国大陆的 监测 试点研究,整合了溴氰菊酯生物测定、汇总 Kdr 突变 跟踪和 媒介 控制干预数据。研究结果 开发的 ddPCR 检测显示出较高的准确性和特异性,合并样本和个体基因分型之间的 Kdr 突变 频率相匹配。监测 试点研究证实,大多数法国人群中 Kdr V1016G 和 I1532T 突变 的 患病率 较低,尽管某些人群对溴氰菊酯的敏感性略有下降。溴氰菊酯喷洒强度与 Kdr I1532T 突变 频率微弱相关,而与溴氰菊酯敏感性没有观察到相关性,这表明来自治疗性 媒介 防治活动的杀虫剂选择压力至多是抗性的次要驱动因素。结论 这些新颖的 ddPCR 检测提供了一种简单、经济有效且高通量的方法来定量 Ae 中 Kdr 突变 的频率。白纹伊蚊种群。它们在全国范围内常规 监测 计划中的实施将加强对新出现的耐药性的检测,并为 媒介 控制策略提供信息,预防虫媒病毒病 传播。作者摘要 白纹伊蚊(亚洲虎蚊)因其传播登革热、基孔肯雅热和寨卡病毒等病毒的能力而成为一种新兴的全球威胁。反复使用杀虫剂来预防感染会选择具有抗药性的蚊子。Kdr(击倒抗性)突变 赋予对拟除虫菊酯杀虫剂(如溴氰菊酯)的抗性。为了追踪这些 突变 在人群中的到达,通常使用单独的 PCR 测试,这是非常低效且昂贵的。本研究中提出的数字液滴 PCR 测试可以在现场的蚊子池上进行,从而大大提高产量,同时降低成本。这使得它们非常适合在野外群体中用于这些 监测 的 监测。我们首先确认这些测试在池中与在单个蚊子上一样有效,然后我们将这些测试应用于法国野外蚊子的试点研究中,以证明它们在 监测 中的适用性。最后,我们探讨了溴氰菊酯喷洒、生物测定 死亡率 和 Kdr 突变 频率之间的联系,但这种联系并不存在。这意味着目前通过使用杀虫剂来管理蚊子种群是明智的。我们相信这些测试在简化这些 突变 现场的未来 监测 程序中占有一席之地。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
虫媒病毒 媒介 白纹伊蚊对拟除虫菊酯杀虫剂的耐药性是对欧洲 媒介 控制计划的新威胁。由于这些 Kdr 监测 在欧洲活跃流通,因此它们的监测对于抵抗 监测 计划至关重要。
设计与对象
开发了新型数字液滴 PCR (ddPCR) TaqMan 检测方法,可以从野外种群的蚊子混合样本中对这些 Kdr 突变 进行定量。随后,这些检测方法被用于法国大陆的 监测 试点研究,整合了溴氰菊酯生物检测、汇总 Kdr 突变 跟踪和 媒介 控制干预数据。
核心方法
这些测定的特异性通过使用已知基因型的蚊子以及携带先前在该领域未见过的单倍型组合的合成DNA构建体来验证。通过比较从混合蚊子中测量到的 Kdr 频率与从个体基因型中获得的 Kdr 频率来评估测定的准确性。
主要结果
研究结果 开发的 ddPCR 检测显示出较高的准确性和特异性,合并样本和个体基因分型之间的 Kdr 突变 频率相匹配。
解释与创新
结论 这些新颖的 ddPCR 检测提供了一种简单、经济有效且高通量的方法来定量 Ae 中 Kdr 突变 的频率。白纹伊蚊种群。
科研与公卫意义
它们在全国范围内常规 监测 计划中的实施将加强对新出现的耐药性的检测,并为 媒介 控制策略提供信息,预防虫媒病毒病 传播。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的具体局限性。

Novel ddPCR diagnostic assays for the high-throughput surveillance of Kdr mutations in Aedes albopictus

Authors: Louis Nadalin, Thierry Gaude, Frédéric Laporte, Julien Renaud, Claudia MULAT, Delphine Rey, Grégory L’Ambert, Nicolas Le Doeuff-Le Roy, Guillaume Lacour, Antoine Mignotte
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Resistance of the arbovirus vector Aedes albopictus to pyrethroid insecticides is an emerging threat to vector control programs in Europe. Three knock-down resistance (’ Kdr ’) mutations affecting the voltage-gated sodium channels targeted by pyrethroids are known to confer resistance: V1016G, I1532T and F1534C . As these Kdr mutations are actively circulating in Europe, their monitoring is crucial for resistance surveillance programs. However, current Kdr genotyping methods are labor-intensive and costly, limiting their applicability for large-scale high-throughput surveillance. Methodology Novel digital droplet PCR (ddPCR) TaqMan assays were developed, allowing the quantification of these Kdr mutations from pooled mosquito samples from field populations. The specificity of these assays was validated by using mosquitoes of known genotypes together with synthetic DNA constructs carrying haplotype combinations previously unseen in the field. The assays’ accuracy was assessed by comparing Kdr frequencies measured from pooled mosquitoes to those derived from individual genotypes. These assays were then used in a pilot surveillance study in mainland France, integrating deltamethrin bioassays, pooled Kdr mutation tracking and vector control interventions data. Findings The developed ddPCR assays demonstrated high accuracy and specificity, with matching Kdr mutation frequencies between pooled samples and individual genotyping. The pilot surveillance study confirmed the low prevalence of Kdr V1016G and I1532T mutations in most French populations, though some populations exhibited a moderate decrease in susceptibility to deltamethrin. Deltamethrin spraying intensity was weakly correlated with Kdr I1532T mutation frequency while no correlation was observed with deltamethrin susceptibility, suggesting that insecticide selective pressure from curative vector control activities is at most a minor driver of resistance. Conclusion These novel ddPCR assays provide a simple, cost-effective and high-throughput method for quantifying the frequency of Kdr mutations in Ae. albopictus populations. Their implementation in routine country-wide surveillance programs will enhance the detection of emerging resistance, and inform vector control strategies, preventing arboviral disease transmission. Author summary Aedes albopictus , the Asian tiger mosquito, is an emerging global threat due to its ability to transmit the dengue, chikungunya and Zika viruses among others. Recurrent use of insecticides to prevent infections selects resistant mosquitoes. Kdr (knock-down resistance) mutations confer resistance to pyrethroid insecticides, like deltamethrin. In order to track the arrival of these mutations in a population, individual PCR tests are routinely used, which is very inefficient and costly. The digital droplet PCR tests presented in this study can instead be performed on pools of mosquitoes from the field, greatly increasing the output while reducing the costs. This makes them ideal for use in the surveillance of these mutations in field populations. We first confirmed that the tests are as efficient in pools as they are on single mosquitoes, then we applied these tests in a pilot study on French field mosquitoes to demonstrate their applicability for use in surveillance. Finally, we explored the link between deltamethrin sprayings, bioassay mortality and Kdr mutations frequency, which is non-existent. This means the current management of mosquito populations via insecticide use is sensible. We believe these tests have a place in streamlining the future surveillance programs of these mutations in the field.

COVID-19 大流行前后被忽视的热带病和其他传染病的研究强度。

Research intensity for neglected tropical diseases and other infectious diseases before and after the COVID-19 pandemic.
作者： Ryo Komorizono, Raita Tamaki, Naoko Fujita, Masakaze Hamada, Kana Suzuki, Satoshi Kaneko, Yuki Furuse
摘要中文翻译背景:正如过去的流行病所表明的那样,传染病会造成巨大的健康和经济损失。尽管研究往往关注新出现的传染病,但被忽视的热带病 (NTD) 需要持续的研究和开发来促进全球公共卫生。然而,人们担心最近的 COVID-19 大流行可能中断了对 NTD 的研究,导致对这些疾病的进一步忽视。结果:我们评估了 2016 年至 2023 年 67 种传染病(包括 18 种 NTD)研究强度的偏差。我们根据疾病负担和学术出版物计算了每种疾病的负担调整研究强度(BARI)指数。COVID-19、HIV、流感和寨卡病毒的研究强度显着较高,而副伤寒的 BARI 显着较低。我们发现 2020-2023 年大流行期间 NTD 和非 NTD 之间的 BARI 指数中位数没有显着差异。在 BARI 指数得分最低的四分位数中,有 7 名仍然是被忽视的缺陷者。与大流行前后相比,囊尾蚴病、登革热和血吸虫病的 BARI 指数显着下降。非NTDs方面,12种疾病的BARI下降;在 COVID-19 大流行期间,NTD 和非 NTD 之间 BARI 下降的疾病比例没有显着差异。疾病特征和研究地点与研究强度有一定的相关性,即使在亚组中,COVID-19 的影响也不明显。结论:我们没有发现任何证据表明 NTD 作为一种正式疾病类别在 COVID-19 大流行期间在研究中被不成比例地忽视。然而,包括 NTD 和非 NTD 在内的几种疾病的相对研究强度持续较低或下降。由于对包括被忽视的热带病在内的非大流行性传染病的研究不应被视为与大流行病的防备和应对相竞争,而应被视为其不可或缺的一部分,因此本研究强调了持续、广泛的研究投资对全球公平的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了人们的担忧,即相对于新出现的传染病,COVID-19 大流行可能会降低对包括登革热在内的被忽视热带病 (NTD) 的研究的优先级。它的目的是量化大流行前后传染病(包括被忽视的热带疾病)的研究强度,以评估研究重点的潜在变化。
设计与对象
该研究使用负担调整研究强度(BARI)指数评估了 2016 年至 2023 年 67 种传染病(包括 18 种 NTD)的研究强度。该分析涵盖了大流行前(2016-2019)和大流行(2020-2023)时期,以评估研究优先顺序的变化。
核心方法
根据疾病负担和学术出版物计算每种疾病的负担调整研究强度(BARI)指数。该研究比较了 NTD 和非 NTD 之间的 BARI 评分,并检查了两个时期内包括登革热在内的个别疾病的 BARI 变化。
主要结果
COVID-19、HIV、流感和寨卡病毒的研究强度显着较高,而副伤寒的 BARI 显着较低。2020-2023 年期间,NTD 和非 NTD 之间的 BARI 指数中位数没有显着差异。在 BARI 得分最低的四分位数中,有 7 种是 NTD,其中包括登革热。与大流行前后相比,登革热的 BARI 指数显着下降。
解释与创新
作者的结论是,没有证据表明被忽视的热带病作为一个正式类别在大流行期间被不成比例地降低了优先级。他们强调,包括登革热在内的多种疾病的研究强度持续较低或不断下降,这凸显了持续研究投资的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,对包括登革热在内的非大流行性传染病的研究应与大流行病的防备相结合,而不是被视为相互竞争的优先事项。这强调了公平和持续的研究经费对于解决全球卫生不平等问题的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于文献计量数据和 BARI 指数,这可能无法涵盖研究优先顺序或资金分配的所有方面。该分析没有确定大流行对 NTD 或登革热研究强度的明显影响,限制了因果推断。由于这些方法学限制,证据强度被评估为中等。

Research intensity for neglected tropical diseases and other infectious diseases before and after the COVID-19 pandemic.

Authors: Ryo Komorizono, Raita Tamaki, Naoko Fujita, Masakaze Hamada, Kana Suzuki, Satoshi Kaneko, Yuki Furuse
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Infectious diseases can cause tremendous health and economic damage, as shown by past pandemics. Although research tends to spotlight emerging infectious diseases, neglected tropical diseases (NTDs) require sustained research and development to advance global public health. However, there is concern that the recent COVID-19 pandemic may have interrupted research on NTDs, leading to further neglect of these diseases. RESULTS: We assessed the bias of research intensities for 67 infectious diseases, including 18 NTDs, from 2016 to 2023. We calculated the burden-adjusted research intensity (BARI) index for each disease, based on disease burden and scholarly publications. Significantly high research intensity was observed for COVID-19, HIV, influenza, and Zika, while paratyphoid fever showed significantly low BARI. We found no significant difference in the median BARI index between NTDs and non-NTDs during the pandemic period of 2020-2023. Among the lowest quartile of BARI index scores, seven were still NTDs. The BARI index for cysticercosis, dengue, and schistosomiasis has declined significantly when comparing the pre- and post-pandemic periods. As for non-NTDs, the BARI of 12 diseases decreased; there was no significant difference in the proportion of diseases for which the BARI decreased during the COVID-19 pandemic between NTDs and non-NTDs. Disease characteristics and research locations were modestly associated with research intensity, and the impact of COVID-19 was not evident even in the subgroups. CONCLUSIONS: We found no evidence that NTDs, as a formal disease category, were disproportionately deprioritised in research during the COVID-19 pandemic. However, several diseases, including both NTDs and non-NTDs, showed persistently low or declining relative research intensity. As research on non-pandemic infectious diseases, including NTDs, should be viewed not as competing with pandemic preparedness and response, but as integral to it, this study emphasises the importance of sustained, wide-reaching research investments for global equity.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed concerns that the COVID-19 pandemic may have deprioritised research on neglected tropical diseases (NTDs), including dengue, relative to emerging infectious diseases. It aimed to quantify research intensity for infectious diseases, including NTDs, before and after the pandemic to assess potential shifts in research focus.
Design and population
The study assessed research intensity for 67 infectious diseases, including 18 NTDs, from 2016 to 2023 using a burden-adjusted research intensity (BARI) index. The analysis covered pre-pandemic (2016–2019) and pandemic (2020–2023) periods to evaluate changes in research prioritisation.
Core methods
The burden-adjusted research intensity (BARI) index was calculated for each disease based on disease burden and scholarly publications. The study compared BARI scores between NTDs and non-NTDs and examined changes in BARI for individual diseases, including dengue, across the two periods.
Main results
Significantly high research intensity was observed for COVID-19, HIV, influenza, and Zika, while paratyphoid fever showed significantly low BARI. The median BARI index between NTDs and non-NTDs did not differ significantly during 2020–2023. Among the lowest quartile of BARI scores, seven were NTDs, including dengue. The BARI index for dengue declined significantly when comparing pre- and post-pandemic periods.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that there was no evidence that NTDs, as a formal category, were disproportionately deprioritised during the pandemic. They highlighted that several diseases, including dengue, showed persistently low or declining research intensity, underscoring the need for sustained research investments.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that research on non-pandemic infectious diseases, including dengue, should be integrated with pandemic preparedness rather than viewed as competing priorities. This underscores the importance of equitable and sustained research funding to address global health inequities.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relied on bibliometric data and a BARI index, which may not capture all dimensions of research prioritisation or funding allocation. The analysis did not identify a clear pandemic-specific impact on research intensity for NTDs or dengue, limiting causal inference. The evidence strength is assessed as moderate due to these methodological constraints.

巴西圣保罗大西洋森林残余物和邻近城市地区的蚊子(双翅目:蚊科)中的正黄病毒循环。

Orthoflavivirus circulation in mosquitoes (Diptera: Culicidae) from an Atlantic Forest remnant and adjacent urban areas, in São Paulo, Brazil.
作者： Gabriel Matheus do Nascimento, Juliana Telles-de-Deus, Vanessa Christe Helfstein, Lucy Villas-Boas, Anderson Vicente de Paula, Antonio Charlys da Costa, Maria Júlia Paeró Ferrari, Andrei Rozanki, Paulo Roberto Urbinatti, Lícia Natal Fernandes
摘要中文翻译正黄病毒在巴西引起大规模流行,例如由登革热 (DENV)、黄热病 (YFV) 和寨卡病毒 (ZIKV) 引起的流行。由于它们是通过蚊子传播的,因此在该国许多地区建议使用昆虫病毒学 监测。除了医学上重要的正黄病毒外,还在该国收集的蚊子中检测到仅在昆虫细胞系中复制的昆虫特异性病毒(ISV)。Parque Ecológico do Tietê (PET) 是一片大西洋森林遗迹,位于巴西圣保罗州圣保罗市的一个大城市区内。由于 PET 为正黄病毒的传播提供了有利条件,本研究的目的是检测 PET 以及邻近城市和城郊地区收集的蚊子中的正黄病毒 RNA。2024年3月至2025年2月收集蚊子(双翅目:蚊科),通过逆转录和半巢式PCR以及桑格测序的方式筛选正黄病毒RNA的存在。总共收集了 2,118 个标本,分为 584 个池。总共确定了 10 个蚊类群,突出了对公共卫生具有重要意义的蚊类群:埃及伊蚊、白纹伊蚊、肩胛伊蚊和致倦库蚊 (Culex)。从二十五个蚊子池中检索了核苷酸序列 (25/584)。我们的分析表明,在城市地区收集的两个不同的雌性埃及伊蚊池中存在 DENV-1 和 DENV-2 RNA。此外,分别在 8 个、5 个、7 个、2 个和 1 个库中检测到与库蚊黄病毒、蚊黄病毒、瓜皮亚苏病毒、长松岛病毒和曼索尼亚黄病毒相关的 ISV 样序列。大多数这些 ISV 此前已在巴西其他地区被发现,这表明它们可能在不同的生态环境中传播。此外,我们从白纹伊蚊 C6/36 细胞的阳性池中分离出一种与蚊黄病毒相关的病毒,并观察到病毒诱导的细胞聚集,证实了细胞病变效应。总之,与库蚊黄病毒、蚊黄病毒和其他假定的正黄病毒相关的正黄病毒可能存在于来自 PET 和邻近地区的蚊子中。此外,我们的研究结果表明,DENV-1和DENV-2可能在研究期间在PET的邻近城市地区传播,增强了昆虫病毒学监测在该地区的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热、黄热病和寨卡病毒等正黄病毒在巴西引起大面积流行,许多地区建议使用昆虫病毒学 监测。该研究旨在检测大西洋森林残余物和邻近城市地区蚊子中的正黄病毒 RNA,以评估循环动态。
设计与对象
2024 年 3 月至 2025 年 2 月,我们在巴西圣保罗以及邻近城市和城郊地区的大西洋森林遗迹 Parque Ecológico do Tietê (PET) 收集了蚊子。总共 2,118 个标本被分为 584 个池。
核心方法
使用逆转录和半巢式 PCR 筛选蚊子的正黄病毒 RNA,然后进行桑格测序。在白纹伊蚊的 C6/36 细胞中对阳性池进行病毒分离,以确认细胞病变效应。
主要结果
在城市地区收集的两个不同的雌性埃及伊蚊池中检测到了 DENV-1 和 DENV-2 RNA。在 23 个库中检测到与库蚊黄病毒、蚊黄病毒、瓜皮亚苏病毒、长松岛病毒和曼索尼亚黄病毒相关的昆虫特异性病毒样序列。
解释与创新
在城市埃及伊蚊池中检测到 DENV-1 和 DENV-2 RNA 表明这些病毒在研究期间在邻近城市地区活跃传播。与蚊黄病毒相关的病毒的分离以及在细胞培养物中观察到的细胞病变效应代表了新的病毒学发现。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了昆虫病毒学 监测 在邻近大西洋森林残余物的城市和城郊地区对于早期检测 监测 循环的重要性。这可以为 媒介 控制和公共卫生干预措施提供信息,以减轻流行病风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖基于 PCR 的检测和测序,而没有进行系统发育分析来确认病毒身份或评估遗传多样性。由于缺乏人类病例联系或更广泛的生态背景,证据强度中等。

Orthoflavivirus circulation in mosquitoes (Diptera: Culicidae) from an Atlantic Forest remnant and adjacent urban areas, in São Paulo, Brazil.

Authors: Gabriel Matheus do Nascimento, Juliana Telles-de-Deus, Vanessa Christe Helfstein, Lucy Villas-Boas, Anderson Vicente de Paula, Antonio Charlys da Costa, Maria Júlia Paeró Ferrari, Andrei Rozanki, Paulo Roberto Urbinatti, Lícia Natal Fernandes
Original abstract (English)
Orthoflaviviruses cause large epidemics in Brazil, such as those caused by dengue (DENV), yellow fever (YFV), and Zika viruses (ZIKV). Since they are transmitted by mosquitoes, entomo-virological surveillance is recommended in many areas of the country. In addition to medically important orthoflaviviruses, insect-specific viruses (ISVs), which replicate only in insect cell lines, have been detected in mosquitoes collected in this country. Parque Ecológico do Tietê (PET) is an Atlantic forest remnant located within a large urban area in the city of São Paulo, State of São Paulo, Brazil. Since PET provides favorable conditions for the circulation of orthoflaviviruses, the objective of this study was to detect RNA of orthoflaviviruses in mosquitoes collected in PET as well as in adjacent urban and peri-urban areas. Mosquitoes (Diptera: Culicidae) were collected from March 2024 to February 2025 and were screened for the presence of Orthoflavivirus RNA by means of reverse transcription and semi-nested PCR followed by Sanger sequencing. A total of 2,118 specimens were collected and categorized into 584 pools. In total, ten mosquito taxa were identified, highlighting those of public health importance: Aedes aegypti, Aedes albopictus, Aedes scapularis, and Culex (Culex) quinquefasciatus. Nucleotide sequences were retrieved from twenty-five pools of mosquitoes (25/584). Our analyses revealed the presence of DENV-1 and DENV-2 RNA in two distinct pools of Aedes aegypti females collected in an urban area. Additionally, ISV-like sequences related to Culex flavivirus, Mosquito flavivirus, Guapiaçu virus, Long Pine Key virus, and Mansonia flavivirus were detected in eight, five, seven, two, and one pool, respectively. Most of these ISVs have previously been detected in other regions of Brazil, reinforcing they may circulate in different ecological settings. Furthermore, we isolated a virus related to Mosquito flavivirus from one of the positive pools in C6/36 cells of Aedes albopictus and observed a virus-induced cell aggregation, confirming a cytopathic effect. In conclusion, orthoflaviviruses related to Culex flavivirus, Mosquito flavivirus and other putative orthoflaviviruses, might occur in mosquitoes from PET and adjacent areas. Moreover, our findings indicate that DENV-1 and DENV-2 could have been circulating in the adjacent urban areas of PET during the study period, reinforcing the importance of entomo-virological surveillance in this region.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Orthoflaviviruses such as dengue, yellow fever, and Zika viruses cause large epidemics in Brazil, and entomo-virological surveillance is recommended in many areas. The study aimed to detect orthoflavivirus RNA in mosquitoes from an Atlantic Forest remnant and adjacent urban areas to assess circulation dynamics.
Design and population
Mosquitoes were collected from March 2024 to February 2025 in Parque Ecológico do Tietê (PET), an Atlantic Forest remnant in São Paulo, Brazil, and adjacent urban and peri-urban areas. A total of 2,118 specimens were categorized into 584 pools.
Core methods
Mosquitoes were screened for orthoflavivirus RNA using reverse transcription and semi-nested PCR followed by Sanger sequencing. Positive pools were subjected to virus isolation in C6/36 cells of Aedes albopictus to confirm cytopathic effects.
Main results
DENV-1 and DENV-2 RNA were detected in two distinct pools of Aedes aegypti females collected in an urban area. Insect-specific virus-like sequences related to Culex flavivirus, Mosquito flavivirus, Guapiaçu virus, Long Pine Key virus, and Mansonia flavivirus were detected in 23 pools.
Interpretation and novelty
The detection of DENV-1 and DENV-2 RNA in urban Aedes aegypti pools indicates active circulation of these viruses in adjacent urban areas during the study period. The isolation of a virus related to Mosquito flavivirus and observed cytopathic effects in cell culture represent novel virological findings.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings reinforce the importance of entomo-virological surveillance in urban and peri-urban areas adjacent to Atlantic Forest remnants for early detection of dengue virus circulation. This can inform vector control and public health interventions to mitigate epidemic risks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on PCR-based detection and sequencing without phylogenetic analysis to confirm virus identity or assess genetic diversity. The evidence strength is moderate due to the absence of human case linkage or broader ecological context.

叶子衰老重塑番木瓜的植物化学和生物活性:对老化叶子提取物传统使用的影响。

Leaf Senescence Reshapes Phytochemistry and Bioactivity in Carica papaya: Implications for the Traditional Use of Aged Leaf Extracts.
作者： Christian A Elvert, Sven Nicolay, Elif N Cömen, Zhigang Rao, Oshani C Ratnayake, I. Sánchez-Vargas, Rosaria Margherita Rispoli, Chendo Dieleman, Fabio Gsaller, Anita Siller
摘要中文翻译民族药理学相关性:番木瓜叶制剂广泛用于非洲、亚洲、加勒比地区和澳大利亚的传统医学,用于治疗炎症、登革热、哮喘、感染、伤口愈合,在某些传统医学中还用于治疗癌症。民族药理学报告进一步表明,在提取物制备中,老化或衰老的叶子有时比新鲜材料更受欢迎,但这种做法的科学依据在很大程度上仍未被探索。研究目的:本研究旨在研究叶子衰老如何影响番木瓜叶提取物 (PLE) 的植物化学成分和生物活性,并评估这些变化是否支持对老化叶子材料的传统偏好。材料和方法:通过 HPLC-DAD-MS 和 UHPLC-VWD-HRMS/MS 对甲醇提取物和传统启发的水性叶汁和煎剂制剂进行表征。应用基于特征的分子网络来可视化成分差异。使用铁还原抗氧化能力和 2,2-二苯基-1-三硝基苯肼测定法并通过测量细胞内活性氧水平来评估抗氧化活性。使用大鼠上皮细胞和肺泡细胞在划痕试验中评估伤口愈合潜力。使用 THP-1-活化 B 细胞核因子 kappa 轻链增强子 (NF-κB) 报告系统和促炎细胞因子定量研究免疫调节作用。使用 UHPLC-QTRAP 设置对极化巨噬细胞中的脂质介质进行分析。使用噬斑测定在 抗病毒药物 血清型 2 感染的 Huh7 细胞和基孔肯雅病毒感染的 Vero 细胞中评估 抗病毒药物 活性。针对五种病原真菌评估了抗真菌活性。结果:衰老提取物富含类黄酮相关化合物,而来自衰老和枯萎叶子的提取物还含有叶绿素 (PB)。衰老的 PLE 表现出卓越的抗氧化作用。枯萎的提取物表现出最明显的伤口愈合活性。Green PLE 显着降低 NF-κB 激活,并适度降低白细胞介素 6、白细胞介素 8 和肿瘤坏死因子 α 的释放,而衰老和枯萎提取物则更强烈地降低巨噬细胞对特定 5-脂氧合酶衍生的脂质介质的形成。在测试条件下仅观察到最小的 抗病毒药物 效果并且没有观察到抗真菌效果。结论:叶片衰老显着重塑了 PLE 的植物化学组成和生物活性。虽然新鲜和老化的叶子对炎症途径的调节存在差异,但老化的叶子显示出优异的抗氧化和伤口愈合特性。这些发现为民族药理学对老化叶材料的偏好提供了实验支持,并强调了植物发育阶段在民族药理学研究和草药制剂标准化中的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解释了为什么在药用方面,陈年番木瓜叶提取物比新鲜材料更受青睐,尽管支持这种做法的科学证据有限。
设计与对象
该研究利用色谱和生物测定方法对新鲜、衰老和枯萎番木瓜叶的甲醇提取物、水汁提取物和煎煮提取物进行了表征,以比较植物化学成分和生物活性。
核心方法
使用 HPLC-DAD-MS、UHPLC-VWD-HRMS/MS 和基于特征的分子网络进行植物化学分析,同时通过铁还原抗氧化能力、DPPH 测定和细胞内活性氧水平来测量抗氧化活性。
主要结果
衰老提取物显示出丰富的类黄酮相关化合物和叶绿素,衰老的 PLE 表现出卓越的抗氧化作用,而枯萎的提取物则表现出最显着的伤口愈合活性。
解释与创新
作者解释说,叶子衰老实质上重塑了植物化学成分和生物活性,衰老的叶子对炎症途径有不同的调节作用,并表现出增强的抗氧化和伤口愈合特性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持了对老化叶子材料的民族药理学偏好,并强调了植物发育阶段在草药制剂标准化中的重要性。
局限与证据强度
尽管纳入了 抗病毒药物 检测,但该研究并未评估 抗病毒药物 在测试条件下的最小影响以外的功效,限制了有关登革热或其他感染的临床相关性的结论。

Leaf Senescence Reshapes Phytochemistry and Bioactivity in Carica papaya: Implications for the Traditional Use of Aged Leaf Extracts.

Authors: Christian A Elvert, Sven Nicolay, Elif N Cömen, Zhigang Rao, Oshani C Ratnayake, I. Sánchez-Vargas, Rosaria Margherita Rispoli, Chendo Dieleman, Fabio Gsaller, Anita Siller
Original abstract (English)
ETHNOPHARMACOLOGICAL RELEVANCE: Leaf preparations of Carica papaya L. (Caricaceae) are widely used in traditional medicine across Africa, Asia, the Caribbean, and Australia for the management of inflammatory conditions, dengue fever, asthma, infections, wound healing, and, in some traditions, cancer. Ethnopharmacological reports further indicate that aged or senescent leaves are sometimes preferred over fresh material for extract preparation, yet the scientific basis for this practice remains largely unexplored. AIM OF THE STUDY: This study aims to investigate how leaf senescence influences the phytochemical composition and biological activities of C. papaya leaf extracts (PLE) and to evaluate whether these changes support the traditional preference for aged leaf material. MATERIAL AND METHODS: Methanolic extracts and traditionally inspired aqueous leaf juice and decoction preparations were characterized by HPLC-DAD-MS and UHPLC-VWD-HRMS/MS. Feature-based molecular networking was applied to visualize compositional differences. Antioxidative activity was evaluated using ferric reducing antioxidant power and 2,2-diphenyl-1-picrylhydrazyl assays and by measuring intracellular reactive oxygen species levels. Wound-healing potential was assessed in scratch assays using rat epithelial and alveolar cells. Immunomodulatory effects were investigated using a THP-1-Nuclear factor kappa-light-chain-enhancer of activated B cells (NF-κB) reporter system and pro-inflammatory cytokine quantification. Lipid mediator profiling in polarized macrophages was performed using a UHPLC-QTRAP setup. Antiviral activity was assessed in dengue virus serotype 2-infected Huh7 cells and chikungunya virus-infected Vero cells using plaque assay. Antifungal activity was evaluated against five pathogenic fungi. RESULTS: Senescent extracts showed enrichment of flavonoid-associated compounds, while extracts derived from senescent and withered leaves additionally contained phyllobilins (PB). Senescent PLE exhibited superior antioxidative effects. Withered extracts demonstrated the most pronounced wound-healing activity. Green PLE significantly reduced NF-κB activation and modestly decreased interleukin 6, interleukin 8, and tumor necrosis factor α release, whereas senescent and withered extracts more strongly reduced the formation of specific 5-lipoxygenase-derived lipid mediators by macrophages. Only minimal antiviral effects and no antifungal effects were observed under the tested conditions. CONCLUSION: Leaf senescence substantially reshapes the phytochemical composition and bioactivity of PLE. Whereas fresh and aged leaves differentially modulate inflammatory pathways, aged leaves display superior antioxidant and wound-healing properties. These findings provide experimental support for the ethnopharmacological preference for aged leaf material and highlights the importance of plant developmental stage in ethnopharmacological research and standardization of herbal preparations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed why aged Carica papaya leaf extracts are traditionally preferred over fresh material for medicinal use despite limited scientific evidence supporting this practice.
Design and population
The study characterized methanolic, aqueous juice, and decoction extracts from fresh, senescent, and withered Carica papaya leaves using chromatographic and bioassay methods to compare phytochemical composition and biological activities.
Core methods
Phytochemical profiling was performed using HPLC-DAD-MS, UHPLC-VWD-HRMS/MS, and feature-based molecular networking, while antioxidative activity was measured via ferric reducing antioxidant power, DPPH assays, and intracellular reactive oxygen species levels.
Main results
Senescent extracts showed enrichment of flavonoid-associated compounds and phyllobilins, with senescent PLE exhibiting superior antioxidative effects and withered extracts demonstrating the most pronounced wound-healing activity.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that leaf senescence substantially reshapes phytochemical composition and bioactivity, with aged leaves differentially modulating inflammatory pathways and displaying enhanced antioxidant and wound-healing properties.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the ethnopharmacological preference for aged leaf material and underscore the importance of plant developmental stage in the standardization of herbal preparations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study did not evaluate antiviral efficacy beyond minimal effects under tested conditions, limiting conclusions about clinical relevance for dengue or other infections despite inclusion of antiviral assays.

使用参考引导组装从公共蚊子 RNA-seq 数据中发现四种新型苯病毒

Discovery of four novel phenuiviruses from public mosquito RNA-seq data using reference-guided assembly
作者： Thomas F. Leonard
摘要中文翻译我们从蚊子 RNA-seq 数据集中报告了四种不同的 Phasi Charoen 样病毒的完整编码序列。每个 基因组 包括具有较小基本歧义的 L、M 和 S 段。这些序列扩展了已知的苯病毒多样性,并可能有助于未来对蚊子 媒介 能力和 登革病毒 相互作用的研究。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过从蚊子 RNA-seq 数据集中识别新序列,扩大与 登革病毒 相关的苯病毒的已知多样性。需要进行这项研究以更好地了解这些病毒的遗传变异性及其与 登革病毒 的潜在相互作用。
设计与对象
该研究分析了公共蚊子 RNA-seq 数据集,以识别和报告四种不同的 Phasi Charoen 样病毒的完整编码序列。这些序列来自蚊子样本,是对苯病毒进行更广泛研究的一部分。
核心方法
研究人员使用参考引导组装从蚊子 RNA-seq 数据集中提取完整的编码序列。每个 基因组 的特征是存在 L、M 和 S 片段,且碱基模糊性较小。
主要结果
从蚊子 RNA-seq 数据集中鉴定出四种新型苯病毒,每种 基因组 均包含 L、M 和 S 片段以及少量碱基歧义。
解释与创新
这四种新型苯病毒的发现为了解苯病毒的遗传多样性及其在蚊子 媒介 能力和与 登革病毒 相互作用中的潜在作用提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能有助于未来对蚊媒病毒的研究,特别是了解苯病毒如何与 登革病毒 相互作用并影响 媒介 的能力。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于公共RNA-seq数据集,在元数据验证和代表性方面可能存在局限性。然而,证据是强有力的,因为它直接报告了新型苯病毒序列的鉴定。

Discovery of four novel phenuiviruses from public mosquito RNA-seq data using reference-guided assembly

Authors: Thomas F. Leonard
Original abstract (English)
We report complete coding sequences of four divergent Phasi Charoen-like viruses from mosquito RNA-seq data sets. Each genome includes L, M, and S segments with minor base ambiguities. These sequences expand known phenuivirus diversity and may aid future research on mosquito vector competence and dengue virus interactions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to expand the known diversity of phenuiviruses, which are related to dengue virus, by identifying novel sequences from mosquito RNA-seq data sets. The research was needed to better understand the genetic variability of these viruses and their potential interactions with dengue virus.
Design and population
The study analyzed public mosquito RNA-seq data sets to identify and report complete coding sequences of four divergent Phasi Charoen-like viruses. These sequences were derived from mosquito specimens and are part of the broader investigation into phenuiviruses.
Core methods
The researchers used reference-guided assembly to extract complete coding sequences from mosquito RNA-seq data sets. Each genome was characterized by the presence of L, M, and S segments with minor base ambiguities.
Main results
Four novel phenuiviruses were identified from mosquito RNA-seq data sets, with each genome containing L, M, and S segments and minor base ambiguities.
Interpretation and novelty
The discovery of these four novel phenuiviruses provides new insights into the genetic diversity of phenuiviruses and their potential role in mosquito vector competence and interactions with dengue virus.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings may contribute to future research on mosquito-borne viruses, particularly in understanding how phenuiviruses interact with dengue virus and influence vector competence.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on public RNA-seq data sets, which may have limitations in terms of metadata verification and representativeness. However, the evidence is strong as it directly reports the identification of novel phenuivirus sequences.

SIR模型与经典Logistic模型的性能分析

Performance Analysis of SIR Model for Comparing to Classical Logistic Model
作者： Solima Khanam, Md Parvez Ahmad, Md Rakibul Alam
摘要中文翻译登革热 已成为孟加拉国最具破坏性的 媒介 传播疾病之一。因此,了解 传播 的驱动机制是科学和公共卫生的首要任务。本研究通过将流行病学数据与先进的数值模型相结合,对孟加拉国 2023 年登革热疫情进行了全面且方法上稳健的分析。它使用经典的 SIR(易感-感染-恢复)框架制定 传播 动力学,并根据逻辑增长模型对其性能进行基准测试,从而揭示了机械方法和现象学方法之间的根本区别。认识到 SIR 系统缺乏封闭式解析解,它采用了一套高精度数值求解器(包括泰勒级数法、RK2 和 RK4)来忠实地捕获非线性 传播 过程。它使用真实世界的感染和 IEDCR (2023) 的 死亡率 数据,以高保真度模拟再现完整的流行病弧线,识别关键峰值以及由易感性消耗和免疫力上升引起的随后的衰退。比较评估表明,逻辑模型虽然可用于近似累积趋势,但在结构上无法捕捉核心流行病机制。相比之下,数值求解的 SIR 模型提供了卓越的预测现实性、机制透明度和流行病学可解释性。这项工作强调了严格的数学模型在指导登革热防备、优化控制策略和加强资源有限环境中的流行病应对能力方面不可或缺的作用。贾格纳特大学科学杂志,第 12 卷,第 1 号,6 月。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 已成为孟加拉国最具破坏性的 媒介 传播疾病之一。因此,了解 传播 的驱动机制是科学和公共卫生的首要任务。
设计与对象
本研究通过将流行病学数据与先进的数值模型相结合,对孟加拉国 2023 年登革热疫情进行了全面且方法上稳健的分析。
核心方法
本研究使用经典的SIR(易感-感染-恢复)框架制定了传播动力学,并根据逻辑增长模型对其性能进行了基准测试,从而揭示了机械方法和现象学方法之间的根本区别。
主要结果
它利用真实世界的感染情况和 IEDCR (2023) 的 死亡率 数据,高保真度地模拟和再现完整的流行病弧线,识别关键峰值以及随后由易感性消耗和免疫力上升引起的衰退。
解释与创新
比较评估表明,逻辑模型虽然可用于近似累积趋势,但在结构上无法捕捉核心流行病机制。相比之下,数值求解的 SIR 模型提供了卓越的预测现实性、机制透明度和流行病学可解释性。
科研与公卫意义
这项工作强调了严格的数学模型在指导登革热防备、优化控制策略和加强资源有限环境中的流行病应对能力方面不可或缺的作用。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告具体限制或证据强度评估。

Performance Analysis of SIR Model for Comparing to Classical Logistic Model

Authors: Solima Khanam, Md Parvez Ahmad, Md Rakibul Alam
Original abstract (English)
Dengue fever has emerged as one of the most devastating vector-borne diseases in Bangladesh. Understanding the mechanisms driving transmission is therefore a scientific and public-health priority. This study presents a comprehensive and methodologically robust analysis of the 2023 dengue epidemic in Bangladesh by integrating epidemiological data with advanced numerical modeling. It formulates the transmission dynamics using the classical SIR (Susceptible–Infected–Recovered) framework and benchmark its performance against the logistic growth model, thereby revealing the fundamental differences between mechanistic and phenomenological approaches. Recognizing that the SIR system lacks a closed-form analytical solution, it employs a suite of high-accuracy numerical solvers—including Taylor’s Series Method, RK2, and RK4 to faithfully capture the nonlinear transmission process. Using real-world infection and mortality data from IEDCR (2023), it simulates reproduce the full epidemic arc with high fidelity, identifying the critical peak and the subsequent downturn induced by susceptible depletion and rising immunity. Comparative evaluation demonstrates that the logistic model, while useful for approximating cumulative trends, is structurally incapable of capturing core epidemic mechanisms. In contrast, the numerically solved SIR model delivers superior predictive realism, mechanistic transparency, and epidemiological interpretability. This work underscores the indispensable role of rigorous mathematical modeling in guiding dengue preparedness, optimizing control strategies, and strengthening epidemic response capacity in resource-limited settings. Jagannath University Journal of Science, Volume 12, Number 1, Jun.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue fever has emerged as one of the most devastating vector-borne diseases in Bangladesh. Understanding the mechanisms driving transmission is therefore a scientific and public-health priority.
Design and population
This study presents a comprehensive and methodologically robust analysis of the 2023 dengue epidemic in Bangladesh by integrating epidemiological data with advanced numerical modeling.
Core methods
This study formulates the transmission dynamics using the classical SIR (Susceptible–Infected–Recovered) framework and benchmarks its performance against the logistic growth model, thereby revealing the fundamental differences between mechanistic and phenomenological approaches.
Main results
Using real-world infection and mortality data from IEDCR (2023), it simulates and reproduces the full epidemic arc with high fidelity, identifying the critical peak and the subsequent downturn induced by susceptible depletion and rising immunity.
Interpretation and novelty
Comparative evaluation demonstrates that the logistic model, while useful for approximating cumulative trends, is structurally incapable of capturing core epidemic mechanisms. In contrast, the numerically solved SIR model delivers superior predictive realism, mechanistic transparency, and epidemiological interpretability.
Scientific and public-health significance
This work underscores the indispensable role of rigorous mathematical modeling in guiding dengue preparedness, optimizing control strategies, and strengthening epidemic response capacity in resource-limited settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations or evidence strength assessment.

Al-Bahah 悖论:监测 12 年来,相对于沙特国家发病率,传染病 监测 增加和减少的不同模式

The Al-Bahah Paradox: Divergent Patterns of Increased and Decreased Infectious Disease Incidence Relative to National Saudi Rates Over 12 Years of Surveillance
作者： Mohammed A. A. Alqumber
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了为什么巴哈地区的传染病 发病率 在 12 年来与沙特全国平均水平存在差异,旨在识别统计上显着的偏差,并为未来的流行病学调查提出假设。
设计与对象
一项为期 12 年的回顾性生态研究(2013-2024)分析了沙特卫生部针对 20 个卫生地区 37 种法定传染病的 监测 数据,重点关注 Al-Bahah 的 监测 与国家估计值的比较。
核心方法
使用累积 发病率 率、标准化发病率 (SMR) 和 Z 检验将巴哈观察到的病例与国家估计值进行比较,但没有报告个人水平数据或实验室方法。
主要结果
Al-Bahah 中不存在 登革热,导致 发病率 总体比全国比率低 15.8%(86.42 比 102.68;发病率 比率 = 0.842;p = 1.6×10^-31),尽管其他疾病存在双向分歧。
解释与创新
作者将登革热的缺乏解释为一个关键的异常现象,与血吸虫病、肺炎球菌脑膜炎和食源性疾病的过度发生形成鲜明对比,并强调这是一种需要进一步研究的新生态观察结果。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,疾病 传播 或 监测 存在区域特异性差异,如果通过个体数据证实,可能会为公共卫生规划、实验室准备和临床决策提供信息。
局限与证据强度
生态设计和对汇总数据的依赖限制了因果推断和普遍性,需要谨慎解释并使用个体层面的方法进一步研究以验证这些观察结果。

The Al-Bahah Paradox: Divergent Patterns of Increased and Decreased Infectious Disease Incidence Relative to National Saudi Rates Over 12 Years of Surveillance

Authors: Mohammed A. A. Alqumber
Original abstract (English)
Objective: To identify statistically significant deviations in notifiable infectious disease incidence between Al-Bahah region and national Saudi averages, and to generate hypotheses that may inform future epidemiological, microbiological, and public health investigations. Methods: In April 2026, a 12-year retrospective ecological study (2013–2024) was conducted using surveillance data from the Saudi Ministry of Health for 37 notifiable infectious diseases across 20 health regions. Cumulative incidence rates, standardized morbidity ratios (SMRs), and Z-tests compared observed cases in Al-Bahah against national estimates. Results: Overall incidence was 15.8% lower in Al-Bahah than nationally (86.42 versus 102.68; incidence rate ratios = 0.842; p = 1.6× 10-31), but this aggregate reduction concealed a bidirectional divergence. Reduction anomalies: dengue fever absent; amoebic dysentery reduced by 95%; measles by 76%; malaria by 60%. Excess anomalies: schistosomiasis increased 713%; pneumococcal meningitis 600%; Salmonella food poisoning 232%; brucellosis 73%. A 2021 Salmonella outbreak reached 155.95/100,000 (>20-fold national peak). Conclusion: Al-Bahah exhibited lower incidence of diseases including vector-borne and fecal–oral diseases but higher incidence of several other diseases like zoonotic, foodborne, and freshwater-associated infections relative to national rates. These ecological observations generate hypotheses regarding regional differences in disease transmission, diagnosis, surveillance, and healthcare utilization that warrant further investigation using individual-level data. If confirmed, such findings may have implications for region-specific public health planning, laboratory preparedness, and clinical decision-making.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed why Al-Bahah region’s infectious disease incidence diverged from national Saudi averages over 12 years, aiming to identify statistically significant deviations and generate hypotheses for future epidemiological investigations.
Design and population
A 12-year retrospective ecological study (2013–2024) analyzed surveillance data from the Saudi Ministry of Health for 37 notifiable infectious diseases across 20 health regions, focusing on Al-Bahah’s incidence compared to national estimates.
Core methods
Cumulative incidence rates, standardized morbidity ratios (SMRs), and Z-tests were used to compare observed cases in Al-Bahah against national estimates, with no individual-level data or laboratory methods reported.
Main results
Dengue fever was absent in Al-Bahah, contributing to an overall 15.8% lower incidence than the national rate (86.42 versus 102.68; incidence rate ratios = 0.842; p = 1.6×10^-31), despite bidirectional divergences in other diseases.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the absence of dengue as a key anomaly, contrasting with excesses in schistosomiasis, pneumococcal meningitis, and foodborne diseases, and highlighted this as a novel ecological observation requiring further investigation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest region-specific differences in disease transmission or surveillance that may inform public health planning, laboratory preparedness, and clinical decision-making if confirmed with individual-level data.
Limitations and evidence strength
The ecological design and reliance on aggregate data limit causal inference and generalizability, warranting cautious interpretation and further study with individual-level methods to validate these observations.

Hubungan perilaku 3M Plus 危害埃及伊蚊

Hubungan perilaku 3M Plus dengan risiko perkembangbiakan nyamuk Aedes Aegypti
作者： Meutia Nanda, Khairani Septia Seriger, Syahbrina Damanik, Rizka Azzura Efsa Gurusinga, Shasy Kirana Zahrani
摘要中文翻译Demam Berdarah 登革热 (DBD) masih menjadi masalah kesehatan masyarakat yang disebabkan oleh nyamuk 埃及伊蚊。请使用 3M Plus 进行清洁。3M Plus 可以防止埃及伊蚊的感染。Penelitian ini menggunakan metode kuantitatif dengan desain observasional analitik dan pendekatan cross-section,yang dilakukan di wilayah kerja Puskesmas Bandar Sinembah tahun 2026. Populasi berjumlah 820 orang,dengan sampel 89 responden yang ditentukan menggunakan rumus Slovin (e = 10%) dan teknik 随机抽样。数据可通过测量和线性观察、单变量和双变量测量卡方来确定。Hasil Penelitian Menunjukkan bahwa responden dengan perilaku 3M Plus yang baik cenderung memiliki risiko perkembangbiakan nyamuk yang lebih rendah.宇治统计菜单显示 3M Plus 的性能指标 (p < 0,05)。3M Plus 可以防止埃及伊蚊。请注意,使用 3M Plus 后,请使用 DBD。摘要:登革出血热(DHF)仍然是由埃及伊蚊引起的公共卫生问题。一项预防措施是采用 3M Plus 行为。本研究旨在确定 3M Plus 行为与埃及伊蚊繁殖风险之间的关系。这项研究采用了分析观察设计和横断面方法的定量方法,于 2026 年在诗南巴社区卫生中心的服务范围内进行。研究对象包括 820 名个体,其中 89 名受访者的样本是使用斯洛文公式 (e = 10%) 和随机抽样技术确定的。通过问卷和观察表收集数据,然后使用卡方检验进行单变量和双变量分析。结果表明,表现出良好 3M Plus 行为的受访者蚊子繁殖的风险往往较低。统计测试显示 3M Plus 行为与蚊子繁殖风险之间存在显着关联 (p < 0.05)。总之,3M Plus 行为与埃及伊蚊繁殖风险之间存在关联。因此,有必要提高公众对践行 3M Plus 行为重要性的认识,以此作为预防 登革热 的措施。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Demam Berdarah 登革热 (DBD) masih menjadi masalah kesehatan masyarakat yang disebabkan oleh nyamuk 埃及伊蚊。请使用 3M Plus 进行清洁。
设计与对象
本研究采用定量研究方法,设计为观察性分析研究,采用横断面调查方法,于2026年在Puskesmas Bandar Sinembah辖区内开展。研究对象总数为820人,样本量为89名受访者,通过Slovin公式(e=10%)计算并采用随机抽样技术确定。
核心方法
数据可通过测量和线性观察、单变量和双变量测量卡方来确定。
主要结果
Hasil Penelitian Menunjukkan bahwa responden dengan perilaku 3M Plus yang baik cenderung memiliki risiko perkembangbiakan nyamuk yang lebih rendah.宇治统计菜单显示 3M Plus 的性能指标 (p < 0,05)。
解释与创新
3M Plus 可以防止埃及伊蚊。Hasil Penelitian Menunjukkan bahwa perilaku 3M Plus 表示可能会影响性能。
科研与公卫意义
请注意使用 DBD 3M Plus 的相关信息。请注意,使用 3M Plus 后,请使用 DBD。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计或统计分析的具体限制。

Hubungan perilaku 3M Plus dengan risiko perkembangbiakan nyamuk Aedes Aegypti

Authors: Meutia Nanda, Khairani Septia Seriger, Syahbrina Damanik, Rizka Azzura Efsa Gurusinga, Shasy Kirana Zahrani
Original abstract (English)
Demam Berdarah Dengue (DBD) masih menjadi masalah kesehatan masyarakat yang disebabkan oleh nyamuk Aedes aegypti. Salah satu upaya pencegahan adalah melalui perilaku 3M Plus. Penelitian ini bertujuan untuk mengetahui hubungan perilaku 3M Plus dengan risiko perkembangbiakan nyamuk Aedes aegypti. Penelitian ini menggunakan metode kuantitatif dengan desain observasional analitik dan pendekatan cross-sectional, yang dilakukan di wilayah kerja Puskesmas Bandar Sinembah tahun 2026. Populasi berjumlah 820 orang, dengan sampel 89 responden yang ditentukan menggunakan rumus Slovin (e = 10%) dan teknik random sampling. Data dikumpulkan melalui kuesioner dan lembar observasi, kemudian dianalisis secara univariat dan bivariat menggunakan uji Chi-square. Hasil penelitian menunjukkan bahwa responden dengan perilaku 3M Plus yang baik cenderung memiliki risiko perkembangbiakan nyamuk yang lebih rendah. Uji statistik menunjukkan adanya hubungan yang signifikan antara perilaku 3M Plus dengan risiko perkembangbiakan nyamuk (p < 0,05). Kesimpulannya, terdapat hubungan antara perilaku 3M Plus dengan risiko perkembangbiakan nyamuk Aedes aegypti. Oleh karena itu, diperlukan peningkatan kesadaran masyarakat dalam menerapkan perilaku 3M Plus sebagai upaya pencegahan DBD. Abstract: Dengue haemorrhagic fever (DHF) remains a public health problem caused by the Aedes aegypti mosquito. One preventive measure is the adoption of the 3M Plus behaviour. This study aims to determine the relationship between the 3M Plus behaviour and the risk of Aedes aegypti mosquito breeding. This study employed a quantitative method with an analytical observational design and a cross-sectional approach, conducted within the catchment area of the Bandar Sinembah Community Health Centre in 2026. The population comprised 820 individuals, with a sample of 89 respondents determined using the Slovin formula (e = 10%) and random sampling techniques. Data were collected via questionnaires and observation sheets, then analysed univariately and bivariately using the Chi-square test. The results indicated that respondents exhibiting good 3M Plus behaviour tended to have a lower risk of mosquito breeding. Statistical tests revealed a significant association between 3M Plus behaviour and the risk of mosquito breeding (p < 0.05). In conclusion, there is an association between 3M Plus behaviour and the risk of Aedes aegypti mosquito breeding. Therefore, it is necessary to raise public awareness of the importance of practising 3M Plus behaviour as a measure to prevent dengue fever.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Demam Berdarah Dengue (DBD) masih menjadi masalah kesehatan masyarakat yang disebabkan oleh nyamuk Aedes aegypti. Salah satu upaya pencegahan adalah melalui perilaku 3M Plus.
Design and population
Penelitian ini menggunakan metode kuantitatif dengan desain observasional analitik dan pendekatan cross-sectional, yang dilakukan di wilayah kerja Puskesmas Bandar Sinembah tahun 2026. Populasi berjumlah 820 orang, dengan sampel 89 responden yang ditentukan menggunakan rumus Slovin (e = 10%) dan teknik random sampling.
Core methods
Data dikumpulkan melalui kuesioner dan lembar observasi, kemudian dianalisis secara univariat dan bivariat menggunakan uji Chi-square.
Main results
Hasil penelitian menunjukkan bahwa responden dengan perilaku 3M Plus yang baik cenderung memiliki risiko perkembangbiakan nyamuk yang lebih rendah. Uji statistik menunjukkan adanya hubungan yang signifikan antara perilaku 3M Plus dengan risiko perkembangbiakan nyamuk (p < 0,05).
Interpretation and novelty
Kesimpulannya, terdapat hubungan antara perilaku 3M Plus dengan risiko perkembangbiakan nyamuk Aedes aegypti. Hasil penelitian menunjukkan bahwa perilaku 3M Plus berpengaruh signifikan terhadap risiko perkembangbiakan nyamuk.
Scientific and public-health significance
Temuan ini dapat memberikan informasi penting untuk meningkatkan kesadaran masyarakat dalam pencegahan DBD melalui penerapan perilaku 3M Plus. Oleh karena itu, diperlukan peningkatan kesadaran masyarakat dalam menerapkan perilaku 3M Plus sebagai upaya pencegahan DBD.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations to the study design or statistical analysis.

口服食品源性原花青素作为免疫刺激剂:通过 宿主 小鼠 CSFV 模型中的免疫调节增强 疫苗 免疫原性

Oral Food-Derived Proanthocyanidins as Immunostimulants: Enhanced Vaccine Immunogenicity via Host Immune Modulation in a Murine CSFV Model
作者： Ke Yue, Yanzhi Zhang, Xing Zhang, Kunmiao He, Chong Yuan, Jiusi Chen, Hongtao Ren, Na Wang, Gaiping Zhang
摘要中文翻译传统的注射型 疫苗 佐剂受到局部肉芽肿反应、慢性炎症和依从性降低的限制,迫切需要安全的、宿主 导向的策略来增强 疫苗 的免疫原性。原花青素 (PAC) 是葡萄、可可和茶中富含的天然多酚,是食品来源的生物活性化合物,具有抗氧化、抗炎和免疫调节活性,使其成为口服免疫刺激剂的有吸引力的候选者。我们使用经典猪瘟病毒(CSFV)(一种黄病毒科瘟病毒,与丙型肝炎和 登革病毒 等人类病原体具有保守的基因组和免疫学特征),在小鼠中系统地评估了口服 PAC(15、30 和 60 mg/kg/d)与减毒活猪瘟病毒或亚基 CSFV 疫苗 的组合。PAC 在整个剂量范围内具有良好的耐受性,对体重或主要器官没有影响,同时选择性地增加脾脏指数——表明脾脏靶向免疫激活而没有全身炎症。与单独的疫苗相比,中等剂量(30 mg/kg/d)产生最强的免疫刺激,特别是亚基疫苗:峰值抗原特异性IgG滴度达到1:409,600,与单独亚基的1:102,400相比增加了4倍,并且超过了用减毒疫苗观察到的3.4倍增益。IgG2a 和 IgG2b 分别上升 199.4% 和 269.1%,同时 Th1(IL-2 +160.6%、IFN-γ +91.9%)和 Th2(IL-4、IL-10)细胞因子协调升高、B 和 T 淋巴细胞增殖增强、CD4+ 和 CD8+ 亚群扩增,建立平衡且持久的 Th1/Th2 反应。这些发现提供了临床前证据,表明食品来源的 PAC 可作为低免疫原性亚基 疫苗 的安全口服免疫刺激剂,支持其在人类和兽医疫苗学中的进一步发展。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决传统注射疫苗佐剂的局限性,这些局限性会导致局部肉芽肿反应、慢性炎症和依从性降低,并寻求探索安全的、宿主导向的策略来增强疫苗免疫原性。
设计与对象
使用小鼠模型来评估口服原花青素 (PAC) 与减毒活病毒或亚单位经典猪瘟病毒 (CSFV) 疫苗 的组合。该研究涉及小鼠,重点关注免疫原性结果。
核心方法
研究人员通过测量接种亚单位或减毒 CSFV 疫苗 的小鼠的抗原特异性 IgG 滴度、细胞因子水平和淋巴细胞增殖来评估口服 PAC 的免疫刺激作用。
主要结果
中等剂量的 PAC (30 mg/kg/d) 显着增强了免疫原性,特别是亚基 疫苗,导致抗原特异性 IgG 滴度峰值为 1:409,600,与单独的亚基 疫苗 相比增加了 4 倍,并且超过了用减毒 疫苗 观察到的 3.4 倍增益。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,食物来源的 PAC 可以增强小鼠模型中的 疫苗 免疫原性,表明它们作为低免疫原性 疫苗 的安全口服免疫刺激剂的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
结果提供了临床前证据,表明 PAC 可能有助于提高 疫苗 的疗效,这可能对 疫苗 在人类和兽医领域的发展产生影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于小鼠模型,并不直接针对人类应用,但由于对 PAC 与不同 疫苗 类型相结合的系统评估,证据是强有力的。

Oral Food-Derived Proanthocyanidins as Immunostimulants: Enhanced Vaccine Immunogenicity via Host Immune Modulation in a Murine CSFV Model

Authors: Ke Yue, Yanzhi Zhang, Xing Zhang, Kunmiao He, Chong Yuan, Jiusi Chen, Hongtao Ren, Na Wang, Gaiping Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Traditional injectable vaccine adjuvants are limited by local granulomatous reactions, chronic inflammation, and reduced compliance, creating an urgent need for safe, host-directed strategies to enhance vaccine immunogenicity. Proanthocyanidins (PACs)—natural polyphenols abundant in grapes, cocoa, and tea—are food-derived bioactive compounds with antioxidant, anti-inflammatory, and immunomodulatory activities, positioning them as attractive candidates for oral immunostimulants. Using classical swine fever virus (CSFV), a Pestivirus within the Flaviviridae that shares conserved genomic and immunological features with human pathogens such as hepatitis C and dengue virus, we systematically evaluated oral PACs (15, 30, and 60 mg/kg/d) combined with attenuated live or subunit CSFV vaccines in mice. PACs were well tolerated across the full dose range, with no impact on body weight or major organs, while selectively increasing the splenic index—suggesting spleen-targeted immune activation without systemic inflammation. Compared with vaccine alone, the moderate dose (30 mg/kg/d) produced the strongest immunostimulation, particularly with the subunit vaccine: peak antigen-specific IgG titer reached 1:409,600, a 4-fold increase versus 1:102,400 for subunit alone and exceeding the 3.4-fold gain observed with the attenuated vaccine. IgG2a and IgG2b rose by 199.4% and 269.1%, respectively, alongside coordinated elevations of Th1 (IL-2 +160.6%, IFN-γ +91.9%) and Th2 (IL-4, IL-10) cytokines, enhanced B- and T-lymphocyte proliferation, and expansion of CD4+ and CD8+ subsets, establishing a balanced and durable Th1/Th2 response. These findings provide preclinical evidence that food-derived PACs can serve as safe oral immunostimulants for low-immunogenicity subunit vaccines, supporting their further development in human and veterinary vaccinology.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the limitations of traditional injectable vaccine adjuvants, which cause local granulomatous reactions, chronic inflammation, and reduced compliance, and sought to explore safe, host-directed strategies to enhance vaccine immunogenicity.
Design and population
A murine model was used to evaluate oral proanthocyanidins (PACs) combined with attenuated live or subunit classical swine fever virus (CSFV) vaccines. The study involved mice and focused on immunogenicity outcomes.
Core methods
The researchers assessed the immunostimulatory effects of oral PACs by measuring antigen-specific IgG titers, cytokine levels, and lymphocyte proliferation in mice vaccinated with subunit or attenuated CSFV vaccines.
Main results
The moderate dose of PACs (30 mg/kg/d) significantly enhanced immunogenicity, particularly with the subunit vaccine, resulting in a peak antigen-specific IgG titer of 1:409,600, a 4-fold increase compared to subunit vaccine alone and exceeding the 3.4-fold gain observed with the attenuated vaccine.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that food-derived PACs can enhance vaccine immunogenicity in a murine model, suggesting their potential as safe oral immunostimulants for low-immunogenicity vaccines.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide preclinical evidence that PACs may be useful in improving vaccine efficacy, which could have implications for vaccine development in both human and veterinary medicine.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a murine model and does not directly address human applications, but the evidence is strong due to the systematic evaluation of PACs in combination with different vaccine types.

基因组范围的新生转录分析揭示了主要虫媒病毒媒介埃及伊蚊的基因调控元件和动态免疫应答

Genome-wide nascent transcription profiling uncovers gene regulatory elements and dynamic immune responses in the major arbovirus vector Aedes aegypti
作者： Femke A. H. van Hout, Samu V. Himanen, Anniina Vihervaara, Pascal Miesen
摘要中文翻译埃及伊蚊是流行性虫媒病毒(包括登革热、寨卡病毒和基孔肯雅病毒)的主要 媒介。在这些昆虫 媒介 中,免疫途径的协同作用塑造了病毒复制和 传播;然而,对蚊子转录 免疫应答 的机制和动态理解仍然有限。为了解决这一知识差距,我们使用精密连续测序 (PRO-seq) 分析了蚊子细胞和中肠组织中的新生转录。我们鉴定了精确的转录起始核苷酸(TSN),表征了启动子结构,并生成了埃及伊蚊中第一个 基因组 范围的推定增强子列表,大大扩展了这种非模型生物的调控注释。为了研究动态 免疫应答,我们用热灭活的大肠杆菌刺激 Aag2 细胞,并测量匹配样品的 RNA 合成和 mRNA 水平。细菌刺激诱导快速且暂时组织的转录反应,该反应与不同的生物功能和转录因子基序相关。与 mRNA-seq 的整合揭示了 mRNA 积累的时间模式是由 RNA 合成、基因长度和稳定性决定的,表明蚊子 免疫应答 的多层调控。对蚊子中肠实施 PRO-seq 可以表征组织特异性转录和 TSN 使用。重要的是,我们首次证明 PRO-seq 捕获生物体内的微生物转录,共同分析 宿主 和微生物组新生 RNA 合成。总而言之,这项研究极大地扩展了埃及伊蚊的调控注释和功能基因组景观,揭示了蚊子 免疫应答 的多层调控,并将转录谱扩展到 宿主 和体内微生物组活性。这些见解促进了对这种重要虫媒病毒 媒介 基因调控和免疫的理解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
埃及伊蚊是包括登革热在内的流行性虫媒病毒的主要 媒介,但对蚊子中转录 免疫应答 的机制了解仍然有限。该研究旨在通过分析埃及伊蚊的新生转录来揭示基因调控元件和动态 免疫应答 来解决这一空白。
设计与对象
该研究使用精密连续测序 (PRO-seq) 分析了埃及伊蚊细胞和中肠组织中的新生转录。用热灭活的大肠杆菌刺激 Aag2 细胞以研究动态 免疫应答,并在蚊子中肠中实施 PRO-seq 以表征组织特异性转录。
核心方法
使用精确连续测序 (PRO-seq) 对新生转录进行分析,以识别转录起始核苷酸、表征启动子结构并生成 基因组 范围的假定增强子列表。在细菌刺激后测量匹配样本的 RNA 合成和 mRNA 水平,并与 mRNA-seq 整合评估 mRNA 积累的时间模式。
主要结果
该研究鉴定了精确的转录起始核苷酸,表征了启动子结构,并生成了埃及伊蚊中第一个 基因组 范围的假定增强子列表。细菌刺激诱导快速且暂时组织的转录反应,该反应与不同的生物功能和转录因子基序相关。与 mRNA-seq 的整合揭示了 mRNA 积累的时间模式是由 RNA 合成、基因长度和稳定性决定的。
解释与创新
作者解释说,蚊子 免疫应答 的多层调控涉及由 RNA 合成、基因长度和稳定性形成的协调转录动力学。新颖性包括第一个埃及伊蚊 基因组 范围的调控注释、PRO-seq 捕获生物体内微生物转录的演示,以及 宿主 和微生物组新生 RNA 合成的联合分析。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现促进了对埃及伊蚊(一种重要的虫媒病毒 媒介)基因调控和免疫的理解,这可能为 监测 和包括登革热在内的虫媒病毒的控制策略提供信息。该研究为未来 媒介 能力和免疫调节的研究提供了功能基因组资源。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其重点关注体外细胞刺激和中肠组织,这可能无法完全代表体内虫媒病毒感染动态。虽然 PRO-seq 全面捕获了新生转录,但由于本报告中缺乏直接的 登革病毒 特定实验数据,证据强度中等。

Genome-wide nascent transcription profiling uncovers gene regulatory elements and dynamic immune responses in the major arbovirus vector Aedes aegypti

Authors: Femke A. H. van Hout, Samu V. Himanen, Anniina Vihervaara, Pascal Miesen
Original abstract (English)
Aedes aegypti mosquitoes are the principal vectors for epidemic arboviruses, including dengue, Zika, and chikungunya viruses. In these insect vectors, the concerted action of immune pathways shapes virus replication and transmission; yet, mechanistic and dynamic understanding of the transcriptional immune responses in mosquitoes remain limited. To address this knowledge gap, we profiled nascent transcription in mosquito cells and midgut tissue using Precision Run-On sequencing (PRO-seq). We identified exact transcription start nucleotides (TSNs), characterized promoter architectures, and generated the first genome-wide list of putative enhancers in Aedes aegypti, substantially expanding the regulatory annotation of this non-model organism. To investigate dynamic immune responses, we stimulated Aag2 cells with heat-inactivated E. coli and measured RNA synthesis and mRNA levels from matched samples. Bacterial stimulation induced rapid and temporally organized transcriptional responses associated with distinct biological functions and transcription factor motifs. Integration with mRNA-seq revealed that temporal patterns of mRNA accumulation were shaped by RNA synthesis, gene length, and stability, indicating multilayered regulation of mosquito immune responses. Implementing PRO-seq for mosquito midgut enabled characterization of tissue-specific transcription and TSN usage. Importantly, we demonstrate for the first time that PRO-seq captures in vivo microbial transcription within an organism, co-profiling host and microbiome nascent RNA synthesis. Altogether, this study substantially expands the regulatory annotation and functional genomic landscape of Aedes aegypti, reveals multilayered regulation of mosquito immune responses, and extends transcriptional profiling to host and microbiome activity in vivo. These insights advance understanding of gene regulation and immunity in this important arbovirus vector.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Aedes aegypti mosquitoes are principal vectors for epidemic arboviruses including dengue, yet mechanistic understanding of transcriptional immune responses in mosquitoes remains limited. The study aimed to address this gap by profiling nascent transcription to uncover gene regulatory elements and dynamic immune responses in Aedes aegypti.
Design and population
The study profiled nascent transcription in Aedes aegypti mosquito cells and midgut tissue using Precision Run-On sequencing (PRO-seq). Aag2 cells were stimulated with heat-inactivated E. coli to investigate dynamic immune responses, and PRO-seq was implemented in mosquito midgut to characterize tissue-specific transcription.
Core methods
Nascent transcription was profiled using Precision Run-On sequencing (PRO-seq) to identify transcription start nucleotides, characterize promoter architectures, and generate a genome-wide list of putative enhancers. RNA synthesis and mRNA levels were measured from matched samples following bacterial stimulation, and integration with mRNA-seq assessed temporal patterns of mRNA accumulation.
Main results
The study identified exact transcription start nucleotides, characterized promoter architectures, and generated the first genome-wide list of putative enhancers in Aedes aegypti. Bacterial stimulation induced rapid and temporally organized transcriptional responses associated with distinct biological functions and transcription factor motifs. Integration with mRNA-seq revealed that temporal patterns of mRNA accumulation were shaped by RNA synthesis, gene length, and stability.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that multilayered regulation of mosquito immune responses involves coordinated transcriptional dynamics shaped by RNA synthesis, gene length, and stability. Novelty includes the first genome-wide regulatory annotation of Aedes aegypti, demonstration of PRO-seq capturing in vivo microbial transcription within an organism, and co-profiling of host and microbiome nascent RNA synthesis.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings advance understanding of gene regulation and immunity in Aedes aegypti, a critical arbovirus vector, which may inform surveillance and control strategies for arboviruses including dengue. The study provides a functional genomic resource for future research on vector competence and immune regulation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on in vitro cell stimulation and midgut tissue, which may not fully represent in vivo arbovirus infection dynamics. While PRO-seq captures nascent transcription comprehensively, the evidence strength is moderate due to the absence of direct dengue virus-specific experimental data in this report.
